prau.ma. iko yaṇaci (6-1-77>
iko yaṇaci|| pratyāhāreṣu tadvācyavācye nirūḍhā lakṣaṇā,
"dīrghājjasi ca"(pā.sū.6-1-105)iti jñāpakāt
"lvādibhyaḥ"(pā.sū.8-2-44)iti nardeaśācca| tenekśabdena
ṣaṭṣaṣṭirgṛhyante, yaṇśabdena sapta| bhāvyamānatvena
savarṇagrāhakatve'pi guṇānāmabhedakatvenānunāsikānāmapi
yavalānāṃgrahaṇāt| ato nāsti yathāsaṅkhyam| na ca
lakṣyārthabādhātpūrvabhāvinaṃ śakyārthabodhamādāya yathāsaṅkhyamastviti
vācyam| evamapi tṛtīyacaturthābhyām ṛkāralṛkārābhyāṃ pratyekaṃ
triṃśadupasthitau lṛvarṇānāṃ rephādeśasÂya ṛvarṇānāṃ lādeśasya ca
prasaṅgāt| tasmādiha "sthānentaratama"(1-1-50)
sūtreṇaiveṣṭasidhdirityabhipretyāha-- sÂthānata āntaryāditi|
prau.ma.- anaci ca(pā.sū.8-4-47>
anaci ca|| dveveti|| "yaro'nunāsika"(pā.sū.8-4-45) iti sūtrād
vāgrahaṇamanuvartate iti "nājjhalau"(pā.sū.1-1-10) iti sūtre kaiyaṭaḥ|
evaṃ ca "sarvatra śākalyasya" (pā.sū.8-4-51) iti sūtraṃ tatprapañcabhūtaṃ
pūrvotarasūtradvayaṃ ca nārambhaṇīyamiti bhāvaḥ| yatu
"dīrghādācāryāṇām"(pā.sū.8-4-52) iti sūtre ācāryāgrahaṇaṃ
pūjārthamityabhipretya vākk iti bhāṣyodāhṛte vikalpe
upapaticintārthaṃ kliśyante, tanmudhaiva; pūjārthatāyāḥ
prāmāṇikairanukteḥ| "triprabhṛtiṣu" (pā.sū.8-4-50) iti sūtre
śākaṭāyanagrahaṇasyāpi tathāpateḥ| naceṣṭāpatiḥ, saṃskarteti
trisakāraparakaiyaṭaharadatādigranthavirodhāt svagranthavirodhācca| ata
eva prātiśākhyabhāṣye'pi "ā tvā ratham" iti mantre takārasya
dvitvavikalpa udāhṛtaḥ| mādhavena cāste ityatra sakārasyadvitvavikalpa
uktaḥ| evaṃ ca dhātraṃśa ityatra takārasya dvitvamasadityuktirevāsatīti
dik||
prau.ma.-sthānivadādeśo'nalvidhau(pā.sū.1-1-56>
sthānivadādeśaḥ|| dhātvaṅgakṛtadhditāvyayasuptiṅpadādeśā iti
prācoktam| tat parigaṇanamudāharaṇamātraṃ vā ?nādyaḥ|
sicksacaṅṅadīnāṃ śnādeśasya śānacaścāsaṅgrahāpateḥ| nāntyaḥ|
avyayasyāṅgapadābhyāṃ pṛthaggrahaṇavaiyarthyāt| prastutyeti
vṛtikāroktaṃ tadudāharaṇamapi mandam| ktvāmātrasyānavyayatvāt,
kṛtvena sidhdatvāccetyāśayenāha--ādeśa iti| analvidhāviti kim ?
tena, tasmāt, tasya,tasmiṃśca vidhau mā bhūt| tatra alā vidhau
yathā-vyūḍhoraskena| atra sakārasya sthānivatvena visargavadaṭtvamā
śritya "aḍvyavāye"(pā.sū.8-4-2)iti ṇatvaṃ prāptam| alaḥ parasya--
dyauḥ, panthāḥ| halṅyādilopo na| alo vidhau dyaukāmaḥ| vali
lopo na| ali vidhau--yajeḥ ktaḥ, ka iṣṭaḥ|
"haśi ca"(pā.sū.6-1-114)ityutvaṃ na| al ceha sthānyavayava eva gṛhyate|
tena rāmāyetyādau "supi ca" (pā.sū.7-3-102) iti dīrghaḥ sidhdaḥ|
tadvidhau hi yañāditvamāśrikataṃ, yañcādeśāvayavo na tu sthānyavayava
iti| tadetatsakalamabhipretyāha- na tu sthānyalāśraya iti| āśrayaṇaṃ ceha
yathākathañcinna tu prādhānyenaivetyāgrahaḥ| tena prapaṭhaḷetyatra
valādilakṣaṇa iṇ na| tanniṣedhāditi| sthānivatvaniṣedhādityarthaḥ|| 
prau.ma.-acaḥ parasminpūrvavidhau (pā.sū.1-1-57>
acaḥ|| alvidhyarthamiti| tena vavraścetyatra vasya samprasāraṇaṃ na |
uradatvasya sthānivatvena saṃprasāraṇatayā "na saṃprasāraṇa-"
(pā.sū.6-1-37) iti niṣedhāt| na coradatvaṃ paranimitaṃ neti
vācyam| aṅgākṣipte pratyaye pare tadvidhānāt
ṭhprāgabhyāsavikārebhyo'ṅgādhikāraḥ' iti pakṣaṃ dūṣayitvā
ṭhāsapātamasamāpteḥ' iti pakṣasyaiva ākare sidhdāntitatvāt| kiṃ ca

etena "acaḥ pūrvatvavijñānādaicoḥ sidhdam" iti vārtikaṃ
vyākhyātam||

nedaṃ sidhyet| "subantāṇṇic" iti haradatādimate'ntarvartisupā

ṇerluptatvāt; na ca pratyayalakṣaṇam, varṇāśrayatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
dvirvacanādau ceti| yalopādaya ādiśabdagrāhyāḥ| ṭhvare' iti tu vare
yo'jādeśaḥ sa na sthānivaditi vyākhyeyam| sahavivakṣā'bhāve'pi
nipātanād dvandvaḥ saptamyaluk ca|
athodāharaṇāni, padānte-- kāni santi, kau staḥ|
dvirvacane--suddhayupāsyaḥ| vare--yāyāvaraḥ| "yaśca yaṅḥ" iti varac,

prau.ma.-saṃyogāntasya lopaḥ (pā.sū.8-2-23>
saṃyogāntasya|| tadantasyeti| alo'ntyaparibhāṣayeti bhāvaḥ| yadyapi
viśeṣaṇena tadantavidhilābhāt saṃyogasyetyeva sūtrayitumucitaṃ
tathāpi pratyekaṃ saṃyogasaṃjñeti pakṣe dṛṣatkarotītyādau lopaṃ
vārayituṃ saṃyogāvantau yasyeti dvivacanāntena
samāsalābhārthamantagrahaṇam| vṛtau hi
upasarjanānāmekatvasaṃkhyautsargikī dvitvādikaṃ tu prayatnalyamiti
bhāṣye sthitam||
"yaṇa--" iti vācanikam| yadvā vācyo vyākhyeyaḥ| vyākhyā ca
dvedhā--"jhalo jhali" (pā.sū.8-2-26)ityato jhalgrahaṇamapakṛṣya jhala eva
lopo vidhīyate iti, antaraṅge lope kartavye bahiraṅgasya
yaṇo'sidhdatvamiti vā| na ca ṣāṣṭhī asidhdaparibhāṣā
tripādīsthatvenāsidhdamantaraṅgaṃ lopaṃ na paśyatīti vācyam|
kāryakālapakṣābhyupagamāt| na ca "nājānantarye bahiṣṭvapraklṛptiḥ"
iti niṣedhaḥ| tasyānityatvāt, utarakālapravṛte lope
ajānantaryābhāvācca| tathā ca vārtikam--"saṃyogāntasya lope yaṇaḥ
pratiṣedhaḥ","na vā jhalo lopād", "bahiraṅgalakṣaṇādvā" iti| iha
yathoddeśapakṣe ādyaṃ vārtikadvayam, tṛtīyaṃ tu kāryakālapakṣe iti
vivekaḥ| yatvāhuḥ-- vārtikakṛto vacanaṃ, pratyākhyānaprakāradvayaṃ tu
bhāṣyakṛta iti| tannirmūlam, matabhede pramāṇābhāvāt|
yadapyāhuḥ--kāvyaṃ, kalatraṃ, śālvaṃ cācakṣāṇaḥ kāv, kalat, śāl;
adabhrayateraghab iti, tadapi na, pakṣatraye'pi saṃyogāntalopasya
durlabhatvāt| ādyapakṣasyaikarūpyārthaṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvamantaraṅgaṃ paraṃ paraṃ
bahiraṅgamityallopaṇilopayorasidhdatvasya supacatvāt| ata eva
kātantrapariśiṣṭe śuklayateḥ kvipi śukl ityudāhṛtam| vāntayoḥ
kvipi ūṭho durvāratvāt| na ca sthānivatvam, kvau vidhiṃ prati
tanniṣedhāt; vici "lopo vyoḥ--"(pā.sū.6-1-66)iti lopasya
prasaṅgācca| giriveśmayatestu kvipi yaṇaḥ pratiṣedhārthaṃ
vacanārambhapakṣe giriveḍityucitam| pratyākhyānapakṣe tu
māntameveti dik|
iti pakṣa iti|| pakṣadvayasyāpi bhāṣye sthitatvāditi bhāvaḥ| catvāri
rūpāṇīti| yatu vadanti-- dhakārayakārobhayadvitve ubhayoḥ punardvitve
lopavikalpe'nyataradvitve ca nava rūpāṇi| tathā hi-- ekadhaṃ
tridhā--ekayaṃ dviyaṃ triyaṃ ca| evaṃ dvidhaṃ tridhaṃ ca tridheti| na
cāntaraṅge dvitve bahiraṅgasya yaṇo'sidhdatvam,
traipādike'ntaraṅge bahiraṅgaparibhāṣā na pravartata iti
visarjanīyasūtre bhāṣyakaiyaṭayorvyavasthāpitatvāditi| atredaṃ
vaktavyam-- punardvitvamaśudhdam, ekasyāṃ vyaktau ekaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ lakṛdeva
pravartate iti "ekaḥ pūrvaparayoḥ" (pā.sū.6-1-84) iti sūtre bhāṣye
sidhdāntitatvāt| anyathā dvitvānāmanantyāpateḥ| na ca "jharo jhari"
(pā.sū.8-4-65) iti "halo yamām--"(pā.sū.8-4-64) iti ca lopena
nirvāhaḥ, lopasya vaikalpikatvāt| kathamanyathā tridhaṃ triyaṃ ceti
bhavaduktiḥ saṅgaccheta| kiṃ caivaṃ sati "sarvasya dve" pā.sū.(8-1-1)iti
punaḥ 
punaḥ pravarteta| ata eva "liṭi dhātoḥ"(pā.sū.6-1-8)iti
sūtre'bhyāsagrahaṇaṃ bhāṣye pratyākhyātam| sannantādyaṅntādvā ṇici caiṅ
dvitvābhāvārthaṃ taditi tu kaiyaṭādibhiruktam| tasmādiha tyaja vā
pañcamādīni rūpāṇi svīkuru vā rūpāntyamiti
navatvoktirasaṅgataiva| sakāradvatvenāṣṭau, visargadvitvena
ṣoḍaśeti tvanyadetat| 
yadapyuktaṃ traipādike'ntaraṅge ityādi, tadapi na, ṭhnārkuaṭa'
ṭhnārpatyaḥ'ityatra visargavāraṇāya vṛdhderasidhdatvasya tatraiva bhāṣye
spaṣṭatvāt| "saṃyogāntalope yaṇaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ" ityasya

tasmādiha yathoddeśapakṣo grāhyae bahiraṅgaparibhāṣāyā apravṛtaye|
tathā ca bhāṣye mayaḥ parasya yaṇo dvitve ṭhdadhyatra' ityudāhṛtam| na
caivamapi "iko'savarṇe--" (pā.sū.6-1-127) iti śākalena
rūpāntaranastviti vācyam| samāse tanniṣedhāt| na ca nityasamāsa
eva tanniṣedha iti vācyam| bhāṣye nityagrahaṇasya pratyākhyātatvāt|
yathotaraṃ munīnāṃ prāmāṇyāt|
nanviha yaṇeva durlabhaḥ, sudhīśabdasya dhyāyateḥ samprasāraṇena
niṣpannatvena "samprasāraṇācca"(pā.sū.6-1-108) iti pūrvarūpāpateḥ;iti
cet ? na, "samprasāraṇapūrvatve samānāhgagrahaṇam" iti vārtikokteḥ,
samprasāraṇasthānikasyātathātvācca| na caivamapi

pratyaye parata eva tasya pravṛteḥ, anantarasyeti nyāyena

prau.ma.-uraṇ raparaḥ (pā.sū.1-1-57) 
uraṇ raparaḥ|| āntaratamyāditi| rephaśiraskasyār ityasya rephadvārā
ṛkāreṇa sthānasāmyādityarthaḥ| pakṣe dvitvamiti| ṛdherÂdhasyeti
bhāvaḥ||
lopo veti| "jhayo honyatarasyām" (pā.sū.8-4-62) iti
sūtrādvikalpānuvṛteḥ| etacca "nājjhalau"(pā.sū.1-1-10)iti sūtre
bhāṣyakaiyaṭayoḥ spaṣṭam| prācastu vikalpānuvṛteranukternyīnatā|
yadapi vyākhyātṛbhiḥ "śaro'ci'(pā.sū.8-4-49) iti jñāpakamabhipretya
tatsaṃvādārthaṃ bhāṣyamupanyastam-- "anuvartate vibhāṣā śaro'ci
yadvārayatyayaṃ dvitvam" iti, tanna, tadutarabhāṣye eva uktajñāpakasya
dūṣitatvāt| lopasya hi nityatve "aco
rahābhyām--"(pā.sū.8-4-46) iti dvitvaṃ lopāpavādaḥ syāt| tathā ca
"śaro'ci" ityasya sārthakyaṃ spaṣṭameva| tasmādvikalpānuvṛtau
jñāpakāntaramanusartavyam, bhāṣyakārādivyākhyānameva vā śaraṇam|
dvitvalopayoriti| yatu dvitvavidhānasāmarthyād dvāveva śiṣyete
iti, tanna, lopasya vaikalpikatvena dvitvasya pakṣe caritārthatvāt|
tridhamiti| "yaṇo maya" lakṣaṇāntareṇa punardvitve tu caturdhamiti
bodhyam
||
prau.ma.- etyedhatyūṭsu (pā.sū.6-1-89) 
etyedhatyūṭsu||atra ecītyanuvartate| tacca etereva viśeṣaṇamaṃ
natvedhateḥ, avyabhicārāt| nāpyūṭaḥ, asambhavāditi prāñcaḥ| atra
kaiyaṭaḥ--edhaterapi viśeṣaṇam| mā bhavānpredidhadityatra
vyabhicārāt| yadyapīdaṃ kaiyaṭapustakeṣu prāyeṇa na dṛśyate, tathāpi
kvacid dṛśyata eva| na ca "ṇau caṅ--'(pā.sū.7-4-11) iiti hrasve kṛte
nāyamedhatiriti vācyam| ekadeśavikṛtasyānanyatvāt| na caivamapi
"ajāderdvitīyasya"(pā,.sū.6-1-2) iti dhiśabdasthāne dhidhiśabdādeśe
sati vasnasoriva prakṛtipratyayavibhāgasammoha iti vācyam| dviḥ
prayogo dvirvacanaṃ ṣāṣṭhamiti ṣāṣṭhabhāṣye sidhdāntitatvāt|
anyathehaiva ṇilopo na syāt| jighāṃsatītyādau sanaḥ sakāreṇa
viśiṣṭasya dvitve kṛte hrasvo na bhavatyeveti vācyam|

prau.ma.-svamornapuṃsakāt (pā.sū.--->
"svamornapuṃsakāt"|| ayaṃ luk pūrvavipratiṣedhena tyadādyatvasya
kimaḥ kādeśasya ca bādhakaḥ| paratvāddhi tyadādyatve'mbhāvena taṃ
kulamiti syāt|
vṛddhyautveti|| ṭhatisakhīni kulāni' ityatra "sakhyurasambuddhau" iti
ṇidvadbhāvādvṛdhdiḥ prāptā, ṭhvāriṇi' ityatra tu "acca gheḥ"
ityautvam, ṭhpriyakroṣṭūani' ityādau tṛjvadbhāvaḥ prāptaḥ| nanu
numnuṭoḥ ko viśeṣastatrāha--nāmīti| numastu aṅgabhaktatvāt
tasmin sati dīrgho na syāditi bhāvaḥ|
prau.ma.- tṛtīyādiṣu bhāṣitapuṃskaṃ puṃvadgālavasya (pā.sū.--->
"tṛtīyādiṣu"|| puṃvaditi| arthānnumhrasvayoḥ pratiṣedho'yam|
pravṛtinimitabhedāditi| pīluśabdasya hi vṛkṣatvavyāpyajātirvṛkṣe
pravṛtinimitam, phaletu phalatvavyāpyakajātirvṛkṣaviśeṣaprabhavatvaṃ
vā| ubhayathā'pi punnapuṃsakayornaikaṃ pravṛtinimitamiti bhāvaḥ| 
prau.ma.- asthidadhisakthyakṣṇāmanaRudātaḥ (pā.sū.--->
"asthidadhi"|| atidadhneti| dadhi atikrāntenetyarthaḥ| yatu
priyadadhneti uraḥprabhṛtiṣu ṭhdadhimadhuśalī' iti pāṭhāt nityaḥ kap
prāpto bhāṣyakāraprayogādeva priyadadhiśabde na bhavatītyāhuḥ|
taccintyam| bhāṣye tādṛśapāṭhasya bahuṣu pustakeṣvanupalambhāt|
striyāṃ tu priyadadhnā priyadadhnyai iti kecit| tanna|
sannipātaparibhāṣayā bahiraṅgaparibhāṣayā ca ṅīpo'pravṛteḥ|
tasmāt puṃsīva striyamapi atidadhnetyādikameva boddhyam|
sudhinī iti| paratvānnumā iyaṅ bādhyate| sudhiyeti| puṃvatvapakṣe
numo'bhāvādiyaṅ| bahvyaḥ śreyasyo yasya tat bahuśreyasī kulam|
atra napuṃsakahrasvatvaṃ na, ṭhīyasā bahuvrīherna' iti hrasvamātrasya
pratiṣedhāditi śrīpatipuruṣotamaviśvarūpādayaḥ| anye tu
upasarjanahrasva eva niṣidhyate, napuṃsakahrasvatvaṃ tu syādeva,
bahuśreyasi bahuśreyasinī ityādyāhuḥ|
snuḥ prasthaḥ sānura striyāmityubhayaliṅgaḥ sānuśabdaḥ| tasya
napuṃsakatve rūpamāha--snūnī sānūnīti| puṃsi śambhuvat|
priyakroṣṭūanīti| yatu prācā priyakroṣṭﬁaṇītyuktam|
tatpūrvavipratiṣedhaparabhāṣyavārtikavirudhdam|
yatu vyācakhyuḥ "vibhāṣā tṛtīyādiṣvaci"(pā.sū.---)
ityetadviṣayako vipratiṣedhaḥ| bhāṣye priyakroṣṭane
araṇyāyetyasyaivodāhṛtatvāditi; tanna, udāharaṇasya saṅkocakatve
mānābhāvāt| yadāhuḥ--na codāharaṇamādaraṇīyamiti| kiṃ ca
pūrvavipratiṣedhasya vaikalpikatṛjvadbhāvamātraviṣayakatve tatra
tṛjvadbhāvagrahaṇaṃ vyarthameva syāt|
priyakroṣṭuanetyādervipratiṣedhaṃ vināpi pakṣe susādhatvāt| na ca
priyakroṣṭretyādinivṛtistatphalam| puṃvadbhāvapakṣe tasya
durvāratvāt svayamapi tathodāhṛtatvācca| na ca samāhārādvigau
pañcakroṣṭuanetyādau puṃvadbhāvābhāvāt tatra tṛjvadbhāvanivṛtiḥ
phalamiti vācyam| evamapi bhāṣyodāharaṇāsāmañjasyāt| upalakṣaṇaṃ
taditi cet, hantaivaṃ priyakroṣṭūanītyevopalakṣyatāṃ
bhāṣyodāhṛtaprātipadikāṃśānugrahāyeti dik|
sulveti| puṃvatpakṣe numābhāvādyaṇ| suluneti| "oḥ supi" iti
yaṇaṃ bādhitvā paratvānnum|
yadigantamiti| etacca rā dāne iti dhātau atiri brāhmaṇakulamiti
prakramya mādhavena spaṣṭīkṛtam| anye tu sudyośabdo bhāṣitapuṃskaḥ|
sa evedānīmigantaḥ, ekadeśavikṛtasyānanyatvāt; ataḥ puṃvadbhāvo
bhavatītyāhuḥ| tathā ca rūpamālādau puṃvadbhāva udāhṛtaḥ|
tasmādejanteṣu klībeṣu puṃvadbhāvo mādhavamate nāsti,
matāntare tvasti| ataḥ pradyavā prarāyā sunāvetyādi prācodāhṛtamapi
nātīva duṣyati||
|| ityajantā napuṃsakaliṅgāḥ ||

ekaikaśa ityuktam| tadayuktam, śasaiva vīpsāyā uktatvena
dvirvacanāyogāt| yenanāprāptinyāyena dvirvacanāpavādaḥ śas iti
sidhdāntāt| 
yatvāhuḥ "sarvasya dve"ityatra ekaikaśo dadātīti bhāṣyaprayogāt
"tānyeka---"iti sūtre ekaśa iti sūtrakāranirdeaśāccobhayaṃ sādhviti|
tanna, udāhṛtabhāṣyasyānyaviṣayatvāt| "yatra taddhitenānuktā vīpsā
bhavati tatra dvirvacanam" ityupakramya hi "tadyathā ekaikaśo dadāti" iti
bhāṣye udāhṛtam| vyākhyānaṃ ca kaiyaṭena--alpārtho'pyekaśeṣo'sti
tasya vīpsāyāṃ dvirvacane "bahvalpārthāt--" iti śaspratyaya iti| na
caiṣo'rthaḥ prakṛte sambhavatītyāstāṃ tāvat| rāma iti|
saṃjñāśabdo'yamavyutpanna iti pakṣe'rthavatsūtreṇa prātipadikasañjñā,
"karaṇādhikaraṇayośca" ityadhikāre ghāpavādena "halaśca" iti ghañā
ramante'sminniti vyutpādane tu "kṛtaddhita--" iti sūtreṇeti
vivekaḥ|
prau.ma.-sarūpāṇāmekaśeṣa ekavibhaktau (pā.sū.1-2-46>
"sarūpāṇāmekaśeṣa ekavibhaktau" || ekavibhaktau yānīti| vibhaktiḥ
sārūpye upalakṣaṇaṃ na tvekaśeṣe nimitam| evaṃ
cānaimitikatvenāntaraṅgo'mekaśeṣaḥ subutpateḥ prāgeva
pravartate| yadyetannārabhyeta tarhi pratyekaṃ vibhaktiḥ syāt, dvandvaśca
pravarteta| ārabdhe tvekaśeṣe'nekasubantavirahād dvandvasyāprāptireva
phalitā bhavatīti bhāvaḥ| sarūpāṇyeveti|
etaccotarasūtrasthaivakārasyehānukarṣaṇāllabhyate| tena
mātṛśabdayorjananīvāciparicchetṛvācinornaikaśeṣaḥ|
mātṛbhyāmityādau sārūpye'pi āṬjasādiṣu vairūpyāt| eka eveti|
etacca sahavivakṣāyāmeva pravartate| itaretarayogadvandvasya viṣaye iti
yāvat| yatra hyanekasyārthasya militasyetarānvayaḥ sa tathābhūto'sya
viṣayaḥ| idaṃ ca "idito num dhātoḥ" iti
liṅgāllabhyate,nyāyācca| tathā hi-- yaḥ śiṣyate sa
lupyamānārthābhidhāyīti sthite śrūyamāṇaikaśabdopasthitaṃ
sarvamaviśeṣādaikarūpyeṇaivānvetumarhati| yadvā, pūrvasūtrād
"dvandve" ityanuvartya dvandve prasakte satīti vyakhyeyam|
syādetat| dvandve kṛte satītyartho'stu| maivam, ṭhpanthānau'
ṭhpanthānaḥ' ityādau samāsāntāpateḥ| ṭhkarau' ṭhkarāḥ' ityādau
ekavadbhāvāpateḥ| ṭhsarve' ṭhsarveṣām'ityādau "dvandve ca" "vibhāṣā
jasi"ityanayorāpateśceti dik| yatu prācā ṭhrāma-rāma-āṬ'iti
sthite ityuktam, ekavibhaktau parata iti ca| tatretthamuktisambhavaḥ--rāma
rāma ityatra avayavayoriva samudāyasyāpi prātipadikasaṃjñā'sti
militenārthavatvāt| na cārthavatve'pi samāsagrahaṇasya
niyamārthatvānnaivamiti vācyam| yatra samudāye pūrvo bhāgaḥ padaṃ
tadviṣayako niyama ityuktatvāt, bahupaṭava ityādyanurodhena niyamasya
sajātīyaviṣayatāyā āṬcityācca| tathā caikājdvirvacananyāyena
samudāyaprātipadikādeva dvavacanādyautpatiḥ|tāvatāpyavayavārthānāṃ
saṃkhyānvayenānugrahasaṃbhavāt| nanvevaṃ rāmakṛṣṇāvikyādiṣvapi
dvandvaṃ vinaiva samudāyādvibhaktiḥ syāditi cet ? maivam|
dvandvavidhāvanekasubantamityasyānuvṛtyā ekājdvivacananyāyasya
bādhāditi dik| itthamuktisaṃbhave satyapi samudāyādekavibhaktau
parata iti pakṣaḥ kimarthamupekṣita iti cet ? śrṛṇu, asminpakṣe
mātṛmātarau ityādāvapyekaśeṣaḥ syāt| nanvekavibhaktāvityāvartya
evakāraṃ cānukṛṣya ekavibhaktau yāni sarūpāṇyavati vyākhyeyamiti
cenna| āvṛtau mānābhāvāt| vibhakterekaśeṣasya viśeṣaṇatve
phalābhāvācca| na ca payaḥ payo jarayatītyādivyāvṛtiḥ phalam,
sahavivakṣāyāmityasyāvaśyakatvāt, anyathā payaḥ payo nayatītyatra
gauṇamukhyakarmaṇorekaśeṣāpateḥ| devadeva ityādāvatiprasaṅgācca|
pratyuta āvṛtyā ubhayaviśeṣaṇatve bhikṣāṇāṃ samūho bhaikṣamiti na
sidhyet| tathā hi, bhikṣā bhikṣā bhikṣā ām a iti sthite
"antaraṅgānapi vidhīn bahiraṅgaḥ" iti luki kṛte
vibhaktiparatvābhāvādekaśeṣo na syāt| lupte pratyayalakṣaṇābhāvāt|
kiṃ ca rāmāvityādau paratvānnityatvācca "vṛddhiteci" ityekadeśe
kṛte vibhaktiparatvābhāvādekaśeṣo na syāt| ubhayata
āśrayaṇe'ntādivadbhāvābhāvāditi dik|
yatu prācā vyākhyātrā pakṣāntaramupanyastaṃ-- savibhaktikānāmekaśeṣa
iti| tanna| nivartamānānāṃ vibhaktyā sahaiva nivṛtāvapi śiṣyamāṇe
ekavacanaśravaṇaprasaṅgāt|
athocyeta yugapadadhikaraṇavacanatāyāṃ
dvivacanabahuvacanāntānāmekaśeṣaḥ| dvandvo'pyevam| tathā hi,
ajahatsvārthāyāṃ vṛtau ekaikamanekārthābhidhāyi
parasparasamabhivyāhārāt| tathā ca alaukike dvandvaikaśeṣayoḥ
prakriyāvākye dvivacanabahuvacanayoreva praveśād
vibhaktyantānāmekaśeṣa iti pakṣo nirbādha eveti| tadapi na,
jahatsvārthāyāṃ vṛtau tadayogāt| pakṣāntare'pi
prathamapravṛtasyaikavacanasya tyāgāyogāt| anyathā
ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣādyaukacchedāpateḥ| tatrāpi pūrvapadasya utarapadārthe
upasaṃkrameṇa ṣaṣṭhaḷadyayogāt| tathā ca bhāśaṣyam "seyaṃ
yugapadadhikaraṇavacanatā duḥkhā ca durupapadā ca" iti| 
yadapi vyācakhyuḥ--vyaktipakṣe yāvadvyakti tataccchabdaprayoge prāpte
ekasyaiva prayogo yathā syāditi sūtram| ākṛtipakṣe tu
ekenaivākṛtyavisābhūtānekavyaktilābhādanarthakamiti| tanna,
vyaktipakṣe'pi "na brāhmaṇaṃ hanyāt" ityatra brāhmaṇaśabdenaivaikenāpi
anekasya tantreṇa suvacatvāt| anyathā jātipakṣe'pi "vāsena
samyakkṣamayośca tasmin" ityādinānārthānurodhena sūtraṃ
vinā'nirvāhāt| tasmājjātivyaktipakṣayorubhayorapi tantreṇa
prayogānnirvāhe'pi sarvatra tantreṇaiva bodhanīyamiti
niyāmakābhāvātpākṣiko dvandvaḥ sūtraṃ vinā durvāraḥ| ghaṭo'yaṃ
ghaṭo'yamati nānāśabdaprayogadarśanāt, tadvat ghaṭaghaṭāviti dvandvasya
nyāyaprāptatvāt| na caivamapi ghaṭakalaśāviti dvandvāpatiḥ|
"virūpāṇāmapi samānārthānām" iti vārtikāt| rūpyate bodhyate
iti vyutpatyā rūpaśabdasya arthaparatvamāśritya vārtikārthasyāpi
sūtreṇaiva kroḍīkārācca|tasmād ghaṭaghaṭau
ghaṭakalaśāvityādipākṣikāniṣṭavāraṇāya pakṣadvaye'pi
sūtramāvaśyakamityavadheyam|
evaṃ sthite sūtravaiyarthyameva hṛdi nidhāya yadiha vyācakhyuḥ--
nānenaikaśeṣo vidhīyate ityādi| yacca samudāyādekasyāṃ
vibhaktāviti pakṣaṃ pratyākhyāya punarāhuḥ--rāma rāma āṬ iti sthite
iti| alaukikametatprakriyāvākyamūhyate na tu laukikam| alaukike ca
vibhaktyekādeśādikāryaṃ na pravartate|
prayuktānāmevānvākhyānādityādi| tatsarvaṃ sandarbhaviruddhaṃ
nyāyaviruddhaṃ ca| prayuktāneva hyanvākhyātumalaukikamutprekṣyate|
śāstrīyakāryaṃ ca sarvamalaukikeṣveva pravartate na tu pariniṣṭhite|
"prathamayoḥ pūrvasavarṇaḥ" ityādīnāṃ pravṛtiṃ vinā
pariniṣṭhitarūpālābhāt| pariniṣṭhite
tatpravṛterdurupapādatvāccetyāstāṃ tāvat|
prau.ma.- ami pūrvaḥ (pā.sū.6-1-00>
"ami pūrvaḥ" || amyacīti| ami yo'c tasmin| amo'vayave'cīti
yāvat| tena makārasahitasya na pūrvarūpam| evam
akṣādūhinyāmityādāvapi ūhinyāṃ yo'c tasminnityādi bodhyam|
"iko yaṇaci" ityajgrahaṇasyānuvṛternirvivādatvāt|

prau.ma.- tasmāccchaso naḥ puṃsi (pā.sū.6-1-00>

śasa ityavayavaṣaṣṭhī| sa cāvayavaḥ paratvena
viśeṣyate--pūrvasavarṇadīrghāt parasya śaso'vayavasyeti|

noktam, asaṃbhavāt| na hi kṛtesavarṇadīrghe paraḥ śas saṃbhavati|
prau.ma.- aṭkupvāṅnumvyavāye'pi (pā.sū.8-4-00>
"aṭkupvāṅ"|| raṣābhyāmiti pañcamīnirdeaśādvyavahitasyāprāptau
vacanamidam| atra sarvairvyavāyo'saṃbhavī, ekaikamātravyavāya ityapi
nārthaḥ| kṣubhnādiṣu kṣubhnaśabdapāṭhasāmarthyāt, "sarūpāṇām"
ityādinirdeaśāccetyabhipretyāha--vyasthairyathāsambhavaṃ militaiśceti|
nanvevam ṭhādarśena'ityādau atiprasaṅgaḥ| na ca aḍādibhireva

yogavibhāgo'tra bodhyaḥ--"vyavāye'pi" ityeko yogo vidhyarthaḥ|
"aṭkupvāṅnum" ityanyo niyamārthaḥ| chāndaso bhiso luk|
niṣedhaṃ bādhitumiti| tena paryāṇaddhamityatra "upasargādasamāse'pi"

nakṣatradarśanasya kālaviśeṣo'palakṣaṇatvātsatyapi divā nakṣatradarśane

asatyapi nakṣatradarśane visṛjyate, tasya satvāt; tathehāpi bodhyam|
nanvevaṃ numgrahaṇaṃ vyartham, anusvārasyāṭtvenaiva saṃgrahāt| uraḥ

iti sūtre'pi numgrahaṇaṃ tyājyaṃ syāt| tatrāpyanusvārasya
lakṣaṇīyatvāt| anyathā'sambhavāt| na hi ṣatvapravṛtivelāyāṃ
ṭhsapaÕṣi' ityadau num labhyate| ṣatvasyāsidhdatvena tataḥ

tatraivātivyāptiṃ vārayituṃ lakṣyānurodhānnumsthānike'nusvāre
lakṣaṇā''śrayaṇīyaiva, vyākhyānāt| tasya ca śartvena tadvyavāye

prau.ma.-yasmātpratyayavidhistadādi pratyaye'ṅgam (pā.sū.1-4-13>

prau.ma.-ato bhisa ais (pā.sū.7-1-00>

prau.ma.-nāmi (pā.sū.6-4-2>

prau.ma.- ādeśapratyayayoḥ (8-3-00>
"ādeśapratyayayoḥ"|| ekā'pi ṣaṣṭhī viṣayabhedādbhidyate ityāśayena
vyācaṣṭe--ādeśaḥ pratyayāvayavaśceti| yadi tu ādeśāvayavo gṛhyaeta
tarhi tisṛṇāmityādau doṣaḥ| yadi capratyayo yaḥ sakāra iti tarhi
rāmeṣvityādau na syāt| yadyapyevaṃ vairūpyeṇa vyākhyāne
sahavivakṣāvirahād dvandvo na yujyate, tathā'pi sautratvād bodhyaḥ|
vivṛtāghoṣasyeti| yatu vadanti--sakārasya
soṣmaṇo'ghoṣasyāntaratamaḥ ṣa eva mūrdhanya iti|
tannyūnam,ṭhakāre'tiprasaṅgāt| tathā ca prātiśākhyam--"varge
varge ca prathamāvaghoṣau yugmau soṣmāṇāvanunāsiko'nyaḥ" iti|
nanu dadhiseca ityādau prāk subutpateḥ samāse ṣatvaṃ vārayituṃ
padādādiḥ padādiriti pañcamīsamāso bhāṣye uktaḥ| tataścehāpi
"sātpadādyoḥ" iti niṣedhaḥ syāditi cet ? maivam, svādiṣviti
yā padasaṃjñā tāmāśrityoktaniṣedho na pravartate iti sātigrahaṇena
jñāpitatvāt| yatvāhuḥ--pratyayaviṣaye nāyaṃ niṣadha iti jñāpyata iti|
tanna, vyatise ityādau ṣatvāpateḥ| nanu padasyādiriti pakṣe
ṭhvyatise' ityatra ṣatvaṃ durvāram| na ca vyapadeśivadbhāvena se
ityasyāpi tiṅntatvātpadatvaṃ śaṅkyam| pratyayagrahaṇe yasmātsa
vihitastadādereva grahaṇāt| anyathā ṭhrāmeṣu' ityādāvapi
ṣatvaniṣedhāpatiriti cet ? satyam| na vayaṃ pratyayamātrasya
tadantatāṃ brūmaḥ, kiṃ tu prakṛtipratyayasamudāye pravṛtāṃ
padasaṃjñāmekadeśavikṛtanyāyena luptaprakṛtike pratyaye'pyānayāmaḥ|
ṭhrāmau' ṭhrāmāḥ' ityādāvapi hi ekadeśavikṛtanyāyenaiva
padatvam|ekaideśātprāgeva padasaṃjñāpravṛteḥ| na tvekādeśe kṛte
"suptiṅntam" ityasya prāptirasti| ubhayata āśrayaṇe nāntādivaditi
sidhdāntāt|
yatu vadanti--prakṛtisannidhāne pratyayasya padatvaṃ nāsti ṭhmantreṣu' ṭh
sarveṣām' ityādinirdeaśāditi, tanmandam, uktarītyā padatvābhāve
nyāyasidhde jñāpanasyāsambhavādanupayogācca|
prau.ma.- sarvadīni sarvanāmāni (pā.sū.1-1-00>
"sarvādīni sarvanāmāni"|| sarvādīni iti tadguṇasaṃvijñāno
bahuvrīhiḥ| "hali sarveṣām" iti nirdeaśāt| dvandve cetīti| sa hi
samudāyasya niṣedho, natvavayavānāmiti vakṣyate| na ca tadantavidhiṃ
vinā samudāye saṃjñāprasaktirastīti bhāvaḥ| nanu tadantasaṃjñāyāḥ kiṃ
phalam| na ca paramasarvasmāyityādau smāyādayaḥ phalam,
aṅgādhikāre tadantavidhinaiva tatsidhderityata āha--teneti| na
cehāpi ṭhprātipadikāt' ityanuvṛteḥ sarvanāmnāṃ sadviśeṣaṇe
tadantavidhirbhavatīti vācyam, "samāsapratyayavidhau pratiṣedhaḥ"
ityukteriti bhāvaḥ| akacceti| cakārātparamasarvata ityatra tasil| 
prau.ma.- jasaḥ śī (pā.sū.>
"jasaḥ śī"|| anekāltvāditi| na tu śitvāditi bhāvaḥ| 
avarṇāntāditi| avarṇāntādaṅgātparasyetyarthaḥ| ato yeṣāṃ keṣāṃ
teṣāmityādau nāvyāptiḥ| sarvanāmnā vihitasyāma iti| sarvanāmnaḥ
parasyeti tu noktam, dakṣiṇotarapūrvāṇāmityatra suṭprasaṅgāt|
"dvandve ca" iti niṣedho hi samudāyasya na tvavayavasya| na ca
tasmādayamām vahitaḥ, kiṃ tu samudāyādeveti na suṭ|
vihitaviśeṣaṇatvasyāśrayaṇe pramāṇaṃ tu "dakṣiṇotarapūrvāṇām" iti
bhāṣyaprayoga eva| 
pañcatriṃśaditi| pañca ca triṃśacceti dvandvaḥ| pañcādhikāstriṃśaditi
tatpuruṣo vā| yatu pratyāhārā eracatvāriṃśaditi prāco granthaṃ
vyācakṣāṇairuktam--nāyaṃ madhyamapadalopī tatpuruṣaḥ| tathā sati
"rājanyabahuvacanadvandvendhakavṛṣṇiṣu" "saṃkhyā" iti vidhīyamānaḥ
pūrvapadaprakṛtisvaro na syāt| tasmātsamāhāradvandva evāyam|
ekaviṃśatirityādau napuṃsakatvaṃ tu na bhavati
lokāśrayatvālliṅgasyeti| taccintyam, samāsabhedena svarabhede
iṣṭāpateḥ| tathā ca "bahuvrīhau saṃkhyeye" iti sūtre
saṃkhyāyāstatpuruṣasyopasaṃkhyānaṃ nistriṃśādyarthamanyatrādhikalopāt"
iti vārtikaṃ vyācakṣāṇo bhāṣyakāra āha--ekādhikā
viṃśaterekaviṃśatiḥ, dvāviṃśatiriti|
ata eveti| dvayordvivacanasyaiva nyāyyatvāditi bhāvaḥ| nanvevaṃ
sarvādiṣu pāṭho vyarthaḥ, sarvanāmakāryāṇāṃ smāyādīnāṃ
dvivacane'bhāvāt| na ca ubhābhyāṃ hetubhyāmubhayorhetvorityatra
"sarvanāmnastṛtīyā ca" iti pākṣikatṛtīyā tatphalamiti yuktam
"nimitakāraṇahetuṣu sarvāsāṃ prāyadarśanam" iti vārtikena
gatārthatvāt| na ca vṛtikṛtā "sarvanāmnastṛtīyā ca" iti sūtre
paṭhitatvādidaṃ vārtikaṃ sarvanāmasaṃjñāsāpekṣamiti vācyam bhāṣye
"hetau" iti sūtre tasya paṭhitatvāt| ata evānnena kāraṇena vasati,
annasya kāraṇasyetyudāhṛtaṃ haradatenetyāśaṅkyāha-tasyeha
pāṭhastviti| ayacprasaṅgāditi| ayaṃ bhāvaḥ--"ubhādudāto nityam"
ityatra "ubhādudātaḥ"yogo vibhajyate| avayavavṛteḥ saṃkhyāvācina
ubhaśabdādavayavinyarthe'yac syāt| ubhayoḥ maṇiḥ| tato "nityam" |
ubhaśabdād vṛtiviṣaye nityamayacsyāt svārthe|
tenobhayapakṣavinītanidrā ityādau ayayac yathā nityaṃ evaṃ
kapratyaye'pi syāditi| nanvevamakaci kṛte'pi ayaj durvāra iti
cenmaivam| yogavibhāgasyeṣṭasidhyarthatayā "ubhau sābhyāsasya" iti
nirdeaśena ca yatra dvivacanaṃ na śrūyate tatraivāyamiti vyākhyānāt|
tathā ca vārtikam--"anyābhāvo dvivacanaṭābviṣayatvāt" iti| akaci
hi sati "tanmadhyapatitaḥ tadgrahaṇena gṛhyate"iti nyāyenobhayaśabdasya
dvivacanaviṣayatvaṃ na vihanyate, kapratyaye tu vihanyataiva|
taduktamiti| vārtikakṛteti śeṣaḥ| yadyapi pūrvavārtike
ṭābapyuktastathā'pi nāsau vivakṣitaḥ, ṭābviṣayatvamapi dvivacanaparatva
eva boddhavyamiti kaiyaṭādibhirvyākhyātatvādityāśayena vyācaṣṭe--
dvivacanaparatvābhāve iti| ṭābekādeśasyāpi pūrvāntatvena grahaṇād
dvivacanaparatvamastīti bhāvaḥ| yadyapyubhāvityādau dvivacanaparatvaṃ
durupapādamubhayata āśrayaṇe'ntādivadbhāvavirahāt
tathāpyubhaśabdādutpannaṃ dvivacanaṃ lukā nāpahṛtaṃ yatra tato'nyatrāyac
itīha vivakṣito'rthaḥ| yadyapi bhāṣye kapratyayasyāpi
svārthikatvenobhaśabdāt parasya
dvivacanatvamastītyāśrityobhaśabdapāṭhaḥ pratyākhyātaḥ, tathā'pi
prauḍhivādamātraṃ tat| kṛtrimasyaiva dvivacanasyeha
grahītumucitatvāt| anyathā tratasilādāvatiprasaktatvāt| nanu
kārakavibhaktyantādvihitau tratasilau svārthikatve'pi dvatvamātraṃ
nābhidata iti ced na, kapratyayasyāpyajñātārthabodhakatvāt
pañcakapakṣe'dhikabodhakatvadhrauvyācceti dik|
| nāstīti | anabhidhānāditi bhāvaḥ| tathā cobhayaśabdasya
dvivacanānutvādādasarvavibhaktikatvenāvyayatve prāpte
"taddhitaścāsarvavibhaktiḥ" iti sūtre "kṛtaddhitānāṃ grahaṇaṃ ca pāṭhe"
iti bhāṣyaṃ kaiyaṭenāvataritam| astīti| pacatikalpaṃ
pacatirūpamityādivāraṇena pāṭhasyopakṣīṇatvādanabhidhāne pramāṇaṃ
nāstīti bhāvaḥ| tayappratyayāntasyeti| ubhayaśabdasyeti bhāvaḥ|
nityaiveti| ṭhpare'vara ubhayā amitrāḥ' iti tu chāndasatvādbodhyam|
avyutpannāviti| etacca paspaśāyāṃ kaiyaṭe spaṣṭam|
eke-- kāśikākārāḥ| ṭhetantvaṃ manye' ityudātasya ṭhutatvaḥ paśyan'
ityanudātasya ca darśanāditi bhāvaḥ| ekaḥ--prathamaḥ| saṃhitayā pāṭhe
tāntatvasyāsphuṭatve'pi tāntaśchetavyo'nyathā ekaśrutyā sakṛdeva
paṭhet| na ca vipratipatavyam "tvatvasamasimetyanuccāni" iti
phiṭsūtrāt| tathā ca ṛṅmantraḥ-- "starīru tvadbhavati sūta u tvam"
iti| vedabhāṣyakārāścetthaṃ vyācakhyuḥ-- tvaditi sarvanāmasu
paṭhito'nudāto'yamanyaparyāya iti| jayadevo'pi prāyuṅkta-
"tvadadharamadhuramadhūni pibantam" iti| tvato'nyasyā adhara iti
vigrahaḥ, na tu tavādhara iti| "paśyati diśi diśi rahasi bhavantam"
iti pūrvavākyena sahānanvayāpateḥ| nema iti| śrūyate ca --
"pranemasmindadṛśe soma' iti| sama iti| tathā ca śrūyate
"nabhantāmanyake same" | "māno vṛkāya vṛkye samasmai"| "uruṣyāṇo
aghāyataḥ samasmāt" | "uto samasminnā" ityādi |
"simaḥ kṛtsne ca śakte ca syānmaryādāvabuddhayoḥ" |
śrūyate ca--"ādrātro vāsastanute simasmai"| "ucchØakramatkamajate
simasmāt"| ubhayatrāpi simaśabdaḥ sarvaparyāya iti vedabhāṣyam|
gaṇasūtra iti| yadyapi bhāṣye aṣṭādhyāyīsthe sūtre "apuri iti
vaktavyam" iti vārtikaṃ paṭhitaṃ tathāpi tadgaṇasūtrasya śeṣaḥ, na tu
jasi vibhāṣāvidhāyakasya| ṭhata' ityadhikṛtya jasaḥ śīvidhānāt
ṭābantātprāptyabhāvāt| antarāyāmiti| prākārādbāhyāyāṃ
tadantarvartinyāṃ vetyarthaḥ| liṅgaviśiṣṭaparibhāṣayā ekādeśasya
pūrvāntatvena grahaṇādvā prāptirbodhyā| tyad, tad, etau
uktaparāmarśakau| tatrādyaśchāndasa iti gaṇaratnakāraḥ| tanna, "syaścchandasi
bahulam" iti sūtre cchandograhaṇavaiyarthyāpateḥ|
eko'nyārtho pradhāne ca prathame kevale tathā|
sādhāraṇe samāne'lpe saṃkhyāyāṃ ca prayujyate||
svābhidheyeti| svasya
pūrvādiśabdasyābhidheyenāpekṣyamāṇasyāvadherniyama ityarthaḥ| ṭhdiśaḥ
sapatnī bhava dakṣiṇāyāḥ' ityādāvapi vyavasthā'styeva; prasiddhatvātu
nāvadhivācī śabdaḥ prayujyate| na ca saṃjñātvānniṣedhaḥ
ādhunikasaṃketo hi saṃjñā| na ca dikṣu sā'sti iti astātiprakaraṇe
kaiyaṭaharadatādayaḥ| atra dikṣu cirantanaḥ, kuruṣu tu ādhunikaḥ
saṃketa ityatra vyākhyātṛvacanameva pramāṇam| etena ṭhviśveṣāṃ
devānām' iti vyākhyātam| vedaprasiddhatvāt| ihāmukasmāt
kuśalā iti avadhyanvayasaṃbhave'pi tanniyamo nāstīti bhāvaḥ| adhare
tāmbūlarāgaḥ, pratyutare ca śakta ityapi pratyudāhartavyam| kathaṃ tarhi
"tathā pareṣāṃ yudhi ca" iti kālidāsaḥ, "apare pratyavatiṣṭhate"
ityādi ca? atrāhuḥ--deśavācitayā vyavasthāviṣayayoreva
parāparaśabdayorupacārāccchatrau prativādini ca prayogaḥ| na
caivamupasarjanatā| na hi lākṣaṇikatvamupasarjanatvam, kiṃ tu
svārthaviśiṣṭārthāntarasaṃkramaḥ| sa ca samāsādivṛtāveva| ata eva
bhūvādisūtre "etānyapīti-- etatpratipādyāni vastūnītyarthaḥ"iti
kaiyaṭoktiḥ saṅgaccchate| vastutastu
parāparaśabdayordeaśāntaraniṣṭhatvādikamevārthaḥ, śatrutvādikaṃ tu
ārthiko'rthaḥ| asaṃjñāyāṃ kim ? utarāḥ kuravaḥ| sumerumavadhimapekṣya
kuruṣūtaraśabdo vartate ityastīha vyavasthā| kiṃ tu
ādhunikasaṅketo'yamityāhuḥ| 
prau.ma.-- "svamajñātidhanākhyāyām" (pā.sū.1-1-35>
svamajñātidhanākhyāyām || ātmātmīyajñātidhanavācī svaśabdaḥ, tatra
jñātidhanayoḥ paryudāsāt ātmā ātmīyau pariśiṣṭāvityāśayena
vyācaṣṭe--ātmīyā ityarthaḥ, ātmāna iti veti| yatvāhuḥ--ātmani
svaśabdo napuṃsakaḥ, tena sve svā ityudāharaṇaṃ tatra na sambhavatīti|
taccintyam| ātmanyapi svaśabdasya puṃstvāt| "vibhāṣā jasi" iti
prakaraṇe "svamātmīye" ityeva vaktavye gurunirdeaśaṃ kurvataḥ
sūtrakārasyaivātra pramāṇatvāt| yadapi svokte'rthe upaṣṭambhārthaṃ--
svo jñātāvātmani svaṃ triṣvātmīye svo'striyāṃ dhane|
iti amarasiṃhoktimāhuḥ| tadapi cintyam| kośe hi ātmanīti
pūrvānvayi| svamiti tu utarānvayi| tathā ca hemacandraḥ- 
dyauḥ svarganabhasoḥ svo jñātyātmanoḥ svaṃ nije dhane||iti|
medinīkāro'pi--
svaḥ syātpuṃsyātmani jñātau triṣvātmīye striyāṃ dhane||iti|
tasmāt sve svā ityasyātmāna iti vivaraṇaṃ samyageveti sthitam ||
jñātidhanavācistviti| tācchīlye ṇininā jñātidhanayorapi
ātmīyatvaṃ puraskṛtya pravṛtau sarvanāmatā'styeveti dhvanayati|
etadarthameva hi sūtre ākhyāgrahaṇaṃ kṛtam|
prau.ma.-antaraṃ bahiryogopasaṃvyānayoḥ (pā.sū.1-1-36>
"antaraṃ bahiryogopasaṃvyānayoḥ"|| bahirityanāvṛto deśo bāhyaṃ
cocyate| tatrādyamarthaṃ gṛhītvā''ha--bāhyā ityartha iti| dvitīye tu
ābhyantarā ityartho bodhyaḥ | bāhyaena hi ābhyantarasya yogo'sti|
athadvayamapyakāre sthitam| upasaṃkhyānaśabdo'pi
karaṇavyutpatyotarīyaparaḥ, karmavyutpatyātvantarīyaparaḥ|
prau.sū.--pūrvādibhyo navabhyo vā (pā.sū.7-0-00>
"pūrvādibhyo navabhyo vā"|| atredamavadheyam| etatsūtraṃ "jasaḥ śī"
ityatrānuvartya vākyabhedena vyākhyeyam "vā ccchandasi" ityetat "āmi
pūrvaḥ" ityatra yathā | evaṃ cāṣṭādhyāyyāṃ "pūrvapara--"ityāditrisūtrī
gatārtheti mahallāghavam| ekaśabdasyāṣṭāvarthā uktāsÂtatra
viśeṣamāha--saṃkhyāyāmiti| anyeṣu saptasu tu vacanāntaramapi
syādeva, eke, ekeṣāmiti yatheti bhāvaḥ|
prau.ma.-- na bahuvrīhau (pā.sū.1-1-29>
"na bahuvrīhau"|| kṛte bahuvrīhau niṣedho vyarthaḥ ṭhupasarjanībhūtā
na sarvādaya' iti vakṣyamāṇatvādata āha-cikīrṣite iti|
samāsātprāgeveti ca | prakriyeti| na ca nimitasyānapasarjanatvasya
vināśonmukhatvātprāgeva saṃjñā na bhaviṣyatīti vācyam|
akṛtavyūhaparibhāṣāyā anityatvāt| tatra caitatsūtrasyaiva
jñāpakatvāt| atibhavakāniti| etadeva jñāpanaphalamiti bhāvaḥ|
iṣṭāpatiṃ kṛtveti| "cchÂavoḥ śūḍ" iti satukkagrahaṇaṃ,
prakṛtipratyayāpativacanaṃ vā anityatve jñāpakam,
lakṣyānurodhādvyavastheti bhāvaḥ| yathotaramiti| tathā cādyatve
bhāṣyakāroktyaiva vyavastheti bhāvaḥ|
yatu prācoktam-- bahuvrīhau sarvādeḥ sarvanāmatā na syāt |
priyasarvāya | sūtrakāramate bahuvrīhau na sarvanāmatā| bhāṣyakāramate
gauṇatvamātre| ṭhtvatkapitṛko' ṭh matkapitṛka'
ityatrasamāsāvayavayoryuṣmadasmadoḥ
sarvanāmatvādanaṅgakāryatvenākac syāt samā bhūt, kapratyaya eva
syādityetadarthamidaṃ sūtramiti nyāsakṛnmatamiti| atredaṃ vaktavyam|
kiṃ bahuvrīhau saṃjñā neti vivakṣitam uta tadavayavānāṃ
sarvādīnāmiti ? nādyaḥ|
samāsāvayavayoryuṣmadasmadorityutaragranthavirodhāt, samudāye
prāptivirahācca| na ca tadantavidhinā prāptiḥ|
bahuvrīhyavayavānāmupasarjanatayā sarvādibahirbhāvena tadante
prāpterdūarāpāstatvāt| ata eva na dvitīyo'pi| na copasarjanānāṃ
pāṭhāt paryudāso vārtikakṛtokto na tu sūtrārūḍha iti vācyam|
anvarthasaṃjñābalena sūtrākṣaraireva tallābhāt| etena sūtrakāramate
bahuvrīhau na sarvanāmatetyapi parāstam| bahuvrīhāveveti
tasyārthaḥ| bhāṣyakāramate ityādyapi na| viśeṣopādānena hi
sūtrakāravārtikakārayormatamanyādṛśamiti pratīyate| devadato
vāmenākṣṇā paśyatītyukte dakṣiṇena na paśyatītivat| tacca na|
sītravārtikamate'pi gauṇaparyudāsasya#eṣṭatvāt| yatu tvatkapitṛka
ityādi| taditthaṃ prācā vyākhyātam--tvaṃ pitā yasya, ahaṃ pitā
yasyeti vigṛhya bahuvrīhisamāse kṛte samāsāvayavayoḥ
samāsāntaḥpātinoryuṣmadasmadoḥ sarvārthavācakatvena
sarvanāmasaṃjñakatvādajñātādyarthavivakṣāyām "avyayasarvanāmnām"
ityakac prasajyeta| tasya
smaiprabhṛtīnāmivāṅgādhikāravihitatvābhāvenāṅgasaṃjñānapekṣatvāt| tataśca tvakatpatṛka ityadyaniṣṭaṃ prasajyeteti| nyāsakṛtā'pi anvarthasaṃjñābalādevasaṃjñānirāse kṛte'kaj na bhaviṣyatītyāśaṅkya samudāyo hi viśeṣe vartate, pūrvapadantu sarvārthatāyā anapetamevetyuktam| tadetatsarvaṃ prāmādikameva| tathā hi-- jahatsvārthāyāṃ vṛtausamāsāvayavayorartha eva nāsti| ajahatsvārthāyāmapi svārthaviśiṣṭānyapadārthavṛtitvenopasarjanatvaṃ spaṣṭameva| evaṃ ca samāsāntaḥpātinoryuṣmadasmadoḥ sarvārthavācakatvenetyādi granthaḥ, samudāyo hityādinyāsagranthaśca pakṣadvaye'pi vyāhataḥ| kiṃ caivam atisarvāyetyatra smāyadeśo durvāraḥ, aṅgādhikāre tadantagrahaṇāt| tvaduktarītyā sarvaśabdasya sarvārthatānapāyāt| na hi pūrvapade sarvārthatā utarapade tu neti yuktikam| api caivaṃ gauṇatve sarvādīnāṃ sarvārthavācakatvaṃ nāstīti| svoktyā saha virodhaḥ syāditi yatkiṃcidetat| 
yadapi vyācakhyuḥ--tvaṃ pitā yasyetyādau vigrahavākye prāptā saṃjñā
niṣidhyate iti| tadapi na| vigrahavākyaṃ hi laukikamalaukikaṃ vā
vivakṣitam ? nādyaḥ| prakrayāvākye'yaṃ niṣedho na tu laukika iti
kaiyaṭagranthavirodhāt,
prakṛtasanadarbhagatapūrvāparasvagranthavirodhācca| na dvitīyaḥ| tasya
yuṣmad su pitṛ su ityākārakatvena tvaṃ
pitetyākārakatvāyogāt|
yadapi vyācakhyuḥ--nanvatisarvādau saṃjñā na pravartate
tvatkapitṛkādau tu saṃjñā pravartata ityatra kiṃ
vinigamakantatrāha--anaṅgakāryatveneti| aṅgaśabdaḥ samudāyaparaḥ|
tenātisarva ityatrāṅgasaṃjñā'pekṣitā sarvanāmno'ṅgāt
smāyādividhānādavayavayamātrasya satyapi sarvanāmatve doṣābhāvāt|
samudāye ca samudāyārthaṃ prati sarvāderupasarjanatvāt tadantasya
saṃjñā na bhavatīti yuktam| iha tu avayavamātrasya sā cikīrṣitā na
samudāyasya, avayavaśca svārthamātrapradhāna iti syāt prasaṅga iti
vaiṣamyamastītyāśaya iti| tadapi na| aṅgādhikāre tadantavidheḥ
sarvasaṃmatatvenāvayavamātre satyapi sarvanāmatve doṣo
nāstītyuktervyāhatatvāt| aṅgādhikāraparibhāṣayā smai syāditi
svagranthavirodhācca| athāyamāśayaḥ--prāk pravṛtā'pi saṃjñā
arthāntaropasaṃkrame sati gateti| tarhi akajapi gaccchatu| nanu
kāryakālapakṣe smāyādyarthaṃ punaḥ saṃjñāpekṣitā, sā ca durlabhetyāśayaḥ
iti cet ? astvevam| na tvasau prakṛtagranthārthaḥ|
nyāsagranthavisaṃvādena ṭhiti nyāsakṛnmatam' iti
vākyaśeṣavirodhāt| nyāsagaranthastvanupadamevodāhariṣyate|
yadapi vyācakhyuḥ--aṅgapradhānaṃ bādhyaṃ tadviruddhamanaṅgaṃ
bādhakamityarthaḥ| tathā ca kapratyayāpavādatvādakac syadityartha iti|
tadapi kliṣṭaṃ vyarthaṃ ca, nyāsagranthāsaṃmataṃ ca| tathā hi,
tvatkapitṛko matkapitṛka iti vṛtigranthaṃ vyākhyāya nyāsakāra
ujjagrāha--nanu ca anvarthasaṃjñākaraṇādevātra saṃjñānirāse kṛte'kaj
na bhaviṣyati| na hyatra sarvārthatā sambhavati viśeṣaviṣayatvāt|
naitadasti| samudāyo hi viśeṣe vartate pūrvapadaṃ tu
sarvārthatāyā anapetameva| bhavatu tarhi
anayoranantarayoretatsutraviṣayatā | priyaviśvāyetyādestu

smaiprabṛtīnāmaṅgakāryatvāt| viśvādiśabdānāmanaṅgatvāt| evaṃ
manyate--tvatkapitṛko matkapitṛka ityatrākaj mā bhūd ityetadarthaṃ
tāvadetadārabdhavyam| priyaviśvāyetyādāvapi vispaṣṭārthaṃ
bhaviṣyatīti| anena hi nyāsagranthenāṅgasaṃjñā'napekṣatvamevārtho
labhyate, sa ca svotprekṣitavyākhyāyāṃ dūrotsārita eva|
yadapi vyācakhyuḥ--yadyapi sūtravārtikakṛtorapyetanmataṃ tathā'pi
nyāsakṛtā vivṛtatvād nyāsakṛnmatamityaktamiti| tadapi sthavīyaḥ,
vārtikakṛtā asyārthasyāspṛṣṭatvāt| nyāsakṛtā samāse kṛte
satītyadivadatā tvaduktārthasya dūratyaktatvena dvivṛtatvābhāvācca| 
yadapyāhuḥ- nyāsakṛccchabdena sūtrakāra eva vivakṣita ityādi| tadapi na
| sūtrakāramate bahuvrīhāvityādipūrvahranthena saha virodhāt|

yadi tu prakriyāgrantho'pi kathaṃcidbhāṣyāvirodhena vyākhyeya
ityāgrahastarhītthaṃ vyākhyāyatām--nanu priyaviśvāyeti gauṇatayā
gatārtham| ato niṣkarṣamāha--sūtrakāramata iti| bahuvrīhāviti|
cikīrṣite satīti śeṣaḥ| gauṇatvamātra iti| mātraśabdo'vadhāraṇe,
gauṇatve eva na tu tataḥ prāk| tathā ca sūtraṃ pratyākhyātamiti bhāvaḥ|
nyāsakṛnmatasya tu atiphalgutvāt
prācīnatatadgranthapariśīvalanaśālibhirvibhāvanīyam|
prakṛtamanusarāmaḥ| saṃjñākāryamiti| sarvanāmasaṃjñāprayuktaṃ
smāyādikamityarthaḥ| antargaṇakāryamiti| "adḍḍatarādibhyaḥ"
"tyadādīnāmaḥ" "tadoḥ sassau" ityādikamityarthaḥ|
tṛtīyāsamāsārtheti| iha "pūrvasadṛśa--" iti vihitaḥ samāso gṛhyate
pratipadoktatvāt, na tu "kartakaraṇe kṛtā" iti| tena tvayakā
kṛtamityatra niṣedho na|
samudāyasyeti| tatraiva dvandvaśabdasya mukhyatvānna tu dvandve yāni
sarvādīnīti sambandhaḥ, kārakāṇāṃ kriyayaivānvayāt| na ca dvandve
vidyamānāni yānīti sambandhaḥ| vidyatikriyādhyāhāre gauravāt|
niṣedhyāyā bhavatikriyāyā eva prādhānyena tadanvayasyaiva nyāyyatvācca|
kiṃ ca tyadādīnāṃ dvandvo nāsti ekaśeṣeṇa bādhitatvāt| tataḥ
prācīnāstvadanyāstatra kākacorna viśeṣaḥ| na ca
dakṣiṇotarapūrvāṇāmityatreṣṭamapi puṃvadbhāvaṃ niṣeddhuaṃ kiṃ na
syāditi vācyam| "sarvanāmno vṛtimātra" iti mātragrahaṇaṃ hi
kvacitsarvanāmatvena dṛṣṭānāṃ samprati saṃjñā'bhāve'pi puṃvadbhāvaḥ|
tasmād nyāyabalād phalabalācca samudāyagrahaṇamevocitam| yadyapi
tvaccchabdastānta iti pakṣe'vayavaniṣedhe'pi phalaṃ suvacam, tathāpi
śāstrasyalpaviṣayatā syād nyāyaśca virudhyeteti bhāvaḥ|
suṭprasaṅga iti| varṇāśrametarāṇāmityādāviti bhāvaḥ| na ca
niṣedhasāmarthyam| anāṅgasya tratasilādervyāvṛtyā
caritārthatvāt| 
jasādhāramiti| yadyapi śībhāvena jas apahriyate, tathā'pi
āṬpacārikamādhāratvaṃ bodhyam| yadvā, jasaḥ iḥ jasiḥ, tasmin|
sautraḥ saptamyā luk, ityarthato vyācaṣṭe--jasādhāramiti|
prau.ma.- jarāyā jarasanyatarasyām(pā.sū.0-0-0>
"jarāyā jarasanyatarasyām"|| vibhaktāviti| etacca "aṣṭana ā vibhaktau"
ityato'nuvartate| tena jarāyā idaṃ jāramityādau nātivyāptiriti
bhāvaḥ| tadanusāribhiriti| inātoḥ pūrvavipratiṣedha
ekasutropātatvāt syādeśo'pi grahītumucita iti teṣāmāśayaḥ|
ata eva--
vītajanmajarasaḥ paraṃ śuci brahmaṇaḥ padamupaitumiccchatām| 
iti bhāraviprayogamasamañjasaṃ matvā vītajanmajarasa iti paṭhanīyamiti
tairuktam| etacceti| bhāṣyakṛtā hi "ṭāṅsi" iti sūtre inātau
pratyākhyāya nādeśa madādeśaṃ ca vidhāya nādeśe pare etvaṃ
yogavibhāgena sādhitam| "bahuvacane jhalyet" "osi ca"| tata
"āṅ" āiṅ ci pare'ta etvaṃ syāt| rāmeṇa| tata "āpaḥ sambuddhau ca"
ekāraḥ syāt saṃbuddhau āṅ oisi ca| na caivamanenetyatra hali lopaḥ
syāditi vācyam| anādeśasya nakāre pare viśiṣya vidhānāt| tathā
hi, "a nāpi" nakāre āpi ca pare'n syādityarthaḥ| sūtre a iti
nāntasya napuṃsakanirdeaśaḥ| na ca ṅseradādeśe kṛte pararūpaṃ
syāditi vācyam| akāroccāraṇasāmarthyāddīrghasambhavāditi| tathā ca
"jarāyā jarasanyatarasyām" iti sūtre uktam --ṭhgonardīyastvāha-
atijarairiti bhavitavyaṃ sannipātaparibhāṣayā' iti| evaṃ ca
pūrvavipratiṣedhenetyādi sarvaṃ bhāṣyavirudhdamityarthaḥ| 
prau.ma.-
paddannomāshṛnnaṣasanyūṣandoṣanyakañcchakannudannāsañcchasprabhṛtiṣu (0-0-0>
"paddanno"|| atra śasprabhṛtayo nimitatayopātāḥ| te ca
padādyādeśānurūpān prakṛtiviśeṣānākṣipantītyāha-- pādadanteti|
yadyapi "śīrṣaṃścchandasi" ityato'tra ṭhcchandasi'ityanuvartate, tathā'pi
bhāṣāyāmapi kvacidbhavati "māsaścchandasi" iti vārtike
cchandograhaṇād jñāpakāt iti vakṣyate| prāmādikamiti| tathā
hi,"āsno vṛkas vatikāmabhīke" iti mantre mukhādityarthaḥ
āṬcityāt| "vṛkasya cidvatikāmamantarā syāt"iti
mantrāntarasaṃvādācca| evaṃ "havyā juhvāna āsani" "āsanyaṃ
prāṇamūcuḥ" ityādāvapi| vyākhyānaṃ ca tathaiva vedabhāṣyeṣu| ata eva
haradatenāpi kāśikāyāḥ prāmādikatvamāviṣkṛtamiti dhyeyam|
prau.ma.- allopo'naḥ (pā.sū.0-0-0>
"allopo'naḥ"|| atra prāñcaḥ-- annantasya bhasyāṅgasyākārasya lopaḥ
syāditi| tathā sati ṭhtakṣṇā' ityādau takārākārasyāpi lopaḥ
syāt| bhasyāṅgasyāno'kārasya iti vyākhyāne tu ṭhanasā,manasā'
ityādau syāt| tantrāvṛtyādinā'nnantasya bhasyāno'kārasyeti
vyākhyāne'pi ṭhanastakṣṇā' ityādau ativyāptirevāto
vyācaṣṭe--aṅgāvayava ityādi| bhasyetyanenākṣiptaḥ
pratyayaviśeṣo'ṅgaṃ ceti dvayamano viśeṣaṇam| an tu akārasya,
ato noktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ| etacca "ṣyaṅḥ samprasāraṇam" iti sūtre
bhāṣye spaṣṭam|
prau.ma.-raṣābhyāṃ no ṇaḥ samānapade (0-0-0>
"raṣābhyām"|| pade itīyataiva sāmarthyādekapadatve labdhe'pi
samānagrahaṇaṃ yatsamānameva, akhaṇḍamiti yāvat| tatra yathā syātena
samāse na bhavati| rāmanāma| yūṣṇa iti| yūṣo maṇḍaḥ| 
ṭhmudgāmalakayūṣastu bhedī dīpanapācanaḥ'
iti darśanāt| iti pakṣe tviti| etena pañcanamīsamāsasya pākṣikatāṃ
dhvanayati| tathā hi, bhāṣye -bebhiditā, māthitikaḥ, apīpacanniti
prayojanatrayamuktam| taccānyathā sidhdam| "ekāca upadeśe" ityatra
vihitaviśeṣaṇādeva bebhiditetyatra iṇniṣedho na| mathitaṃ
paṇyamasyeti vigrahe ṭhaki ṭhasyekādeśe "yasyeti ca" ityallope
"isusuktāntātkaḥ" iti kādeśastu na bhaviṣyati| ṭhasyābhāvāt|na ca
sthānivadbhāvena ṭhatvamalvidhitvāt| "ṭhasyekaḥ"ityatra hi
sthānyādeśayorakāra uccāraṇārthaḥ| apīpacannityapi ṇilopasya
caṅntyakārayorekādeśasya ca sthānivadbhāvaṃ vinā'pi sidhdam|
vidhisāhacaryādabhyastādapi laṅ eva jherjusvidhānāt| na ca sicā
sāhacaryād luṅ eva kiṃ na syāditi vācyam|
pūrvasāhacaryātparasāhacaryasya balīyastvāditi dik|
pūrvatrāsiddhe neti| etacca nyāyasidhdamityavocāma| tripādyā
asidhdatvena tatratye kārye kartavye "acaḥ parasmin"
ityatideśasyāpravṛteḥ| tasyeti| tasya ṭhpūrvatrāsiddhe'ityasya | tathā ca
sthalatraye sthānivadbhāvo vaktavya ityarthaḥ| saṃyogādilope
cakryatra| latve nigālyate| ṇatve māṣavapanī| iha "yasyeti ca" iti
allopasya sthānivatvena nakārasya prātipadikāntatvābhāvād ṇatvaṃ
na| 
yatvāhuḥ--kathaṃcitsthānivatvābhāve'pi yūṣṇa ityādau "raṣābhyām"
ityasya nopayogaḥ, ṣṭuatvena sidhdeḥ| tathā ca
ṣagrahaṇamutarārthamiti ākare sthitam| tataśca
rabhasavādenaitadupanyastamiti| sa evāyaṃ rabhasābhāvaḥ| tathā hi,
utarārthatayā'pīha ṣagrahaṇaṃ sthitam| tataśca yūṣṇaḥ puṣṇāti ityādau
"raṣābhyām" iti ṇatvasya pravṛtiḥ kena vāryatām? ṣṭuatveneti
cenna| tasyāsiddhatvāt| na ca vacanāprāmāṇyātsiddhatvam|
puṣṭirityādau caratārthatvāt| tasya ṣayogeṇa ityaṃśo'caritārtha
iti cenna| tasya pṛthaganukteḥ| iha ṣātparasyatyuktiracaritārthetyasyāpi
tulyatvācca| ākare hi iha ṣagrahaṇaṃ vinā lakṣyāsiddhirnāsti
ityevābhipretaṃ, na tu puṣṇātītyādau "raṣābhyām"
ityasyāpravṛtiriti dik| 
prau.ma.- nalopaḥ prātipadikāntasya(0-0-0>
"nalopaḥ prātipadikāntasya"|| luptaṣaṣṭhīke iti| sautratvānna tu
ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣayoḥ pūrvapade, sāpekṣatvena samāsāsambhavāt
prātipadikasya padaviśeṣaṇatvācceti bhāvaḥ|tadantasyeti|
ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣaḥ| tasyānto yo nakārastasyetyarthaḥ| uktaṃ ca
haradatena--ṭhviśeṣyamantasyetyasya netyetadavibhaktikam, tathaiva
prātipadiketyetatpadaviśeṣaṇam' iti| yatu
prācoktaṃ--prātipadikāntasya padāntasya nasyeti| yacca
vyākhyātam--antasyetyubhābhyāṃ saṃbadhyate iti| tadgauravagrastam,
antasyetyasyāvṛtiprasaṅgād vyadhikaraṇaṣaṣṭhīdvayāpateśca| yadi tu
abhedenaiva sambandhena prātipadikeneva netyanenāpi padaṃ
viśeṣyate, tadā viśeṣaṇena tadantavidhau sautramantagrahaṇaṃ
śakyamakartum|
prau.ma.-vibhāṣā iṅśyoḥ (0-0-0>
ṭhvibhāṣā ṅśyoḥ'||śīiti "napuṃsakācca"iti vihito na tu
jaśśasoḥ śiḥ, tasmin bhatvāsambhavāt| "bhasya"
ityanenāsarvanāmasthānasyaiva
yajāderākṣepātadetadāha--asarvanāmasthāneti| ata eveti| doṣaṇī
iti prayogāt| yadyapi samāhāradvandvasya ekaśeṣe kṛte'pi bhāṣyaṃ
sayojyaṃ, tathā'pi kakudityasya pṛthakpadatvaṃ nyāyyam| anyathā kakuda
ekatvaṃ bāhvordvitvaṃ ca na labhyeta iti bhāvaḥ|
"tamupādravadudyamya dakṣiṇaṃ dorniśācaraḥ"
iti raghuḥ| tatra dakṣiṇaṃ doriti udyamyetyanena karmatayā sambadhyate|
doṣaṃ tasyeti|
"doṣaṃ tasya tathāvidhasya bhajataścāpasya gṛhṇan guṇam"|
iti śrīharṣaprayogaḥ| tatra doṣaṃ hastaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ ca bhajataścāpasya guṇaṃ
maurvīmatiśayaṃ ca gṛhṇan iti sambandhaḥ|
amaravyākhyātārastu--doṣaśabdaṃ striyāmapi svīkṛtya bhāgurimatena
ṭāpi doṣetyuktvā--
"dordoṣā ca bhujo bāhuḥ pāṇirhastaḥ karastathā"
iti dhanañjayanighaṇṭuamudājahrauḥ|
dvayorahnorbhava iti| "taddhitārtha" iti samāsaḥ, kālāṭṭhañ|
"dvigorluganapatye" iti luk| "rājāhaḥsakhibhyaḥ" iti ṭac|
"ahnohna etebhyaḥ" ityahnādeśaḥ| ahnaḥ sāya iti| syaterghañi
avasānavacanaḥ sāyaśabdaḥ| "saṃkhyāvisāya--"iti
jñāpakādekadeśisamāsaḥ|
||ityadantāḥ||
hāhāniti| gandharvaviśeṣavācakamavyutpannaṃ prātipadikamidam|
kaumārāstu--jahātīti hāhā iti kriyāśabdaṃ vadanto
viśvapāvadrūpamāhuḥ| tanmate pratyudāharaṇaṃ tu ṭhakāro vāsudevaḥ
syāt' tena sahavartanta iti sāḥ, tān sān iti bodhyam|
||ityādantāḥ||
prau.ma.--"āṅe nāstriyām"(pā.sū.--->

prau.ma.-acca gheḥ (pā.sū.--->

prau.ma.-halṅyābhyo dīrghātsutisyapṛktaṃ hal (pā.sū.--->

kim ? bibheda|
nanu prathamahalgrahaṇaṃ vyartham|halantātpareṣāṃ

saṃyogāntasya lope hi nalopādirna sidhyati|
rātu ternaiva lopaḥ syāddhalastasmādvidhīyate||

prau.ma.-svarādinipātamavyayam (pā.sū.-->
"svarādinipātamavyayam"|| svarādayo nipātāśceti
samāhāradvandvaḥ| svariti svarge paraloke ca| antariti madhye|
prātariti pratyūṣe| punariti aprathame viśeṣe ca| sunutar
antardhāne, svarādyāḥ pañca rephāntāḥ|| uccais mahati| nīcais alpe|
śanais kriyāmāndye| ṛdhak satye|
viyogaśīghrasāmīpyalāghaveṣvatyanye| ṛte varjane|
yugapadityekakāle| ārād dūrasamīpayoḥ| pṛthak bhinne| hyas
atīte'nhi| divā divase| rātrāviti niśa| sāyamiti niśāmukhe|
ciramiti bahukāle| manāk,īṣat, imāvalpe| joṣaṃ sukhe maune
ca| tūṣṇīmiti maune| bahis, avas imau bāhyae| samayeti samīpe
madhye ca| nikaṣetyantike| svayamityātmanetyarthe| vṛtheti vyarthe|

sahasetyā''kasmikā'vimarśayoḥ| vineti varjane|

prau.ma.- vibhāṣā (pā.sū.-->
"vibhāṣā" || etaditi| nyāyasiddhe'rthe liṅgadarśanamidam|
ṭighubhādisaṃjñānāmiva samasasaṃjñāyā api nityaprāptatvādvacanaṃ vinā

syādetat| "supsupā" ityapi tarhi nityaṃ syāt| na ceṣṭāpatiḥ,
apasiddhāntāt| tathā hi--caraṭsūtre vṛtikṛtoktaṃ pūrvaṃ bhūto
bhūtapūrvaḥ| supsupeti samāsa iti| tathā śākalasūtre
"sinnityasamāsayoḥ śākalapratiṣedha ityatra nityagrahaṇena nārtha idamapi
siddhaṃ bhavati vāpyāmaśvo vāpyaśva" iti bhāṣyaṃ kaiyaṭo
vyākhyāt--ṭhvāpyaśva iti supsupeti samāsaḥ| "saṃjñāyām" iti
samāsasya nityatvātsiddhaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ' iti| tathā "anudātaṃ
padamekavarjam" "asiddhavadatra" ityādisūtreśaṣvapi kaiyaṭe spaṣṭam|
ata āha--supsupetīti samāseti| yogavibhāgenaiva siddhe samāse
utarasūtraiḥ punarvidhānaṃ prācīnasya kvācitkatāṃ jñāpayati| evaṃ ca
ivena samāsasyāpi kvācitkatvādudbāhuriva vāmana ityādi
siddham| iveneti vārtikaṃ tu yadyapi "kugati" ityatra paṭhaḷte tathā'pi

"nadībhiśca"||svarūpasya saṃjñāyā nadyāśca neha grahaṇaṃ
bahuvacananirddeśāt kiṃ tvarthasya| na ca tasya samāsaḥ saṃjñā bhavati|
atastadvācināmayaṃ samāsaḥ| te ca na kevalaṃ viśeṣaśabdā eva kiṃ tu
sāmānyaśabdo'pi|
nityasamāsa iti| etacca vṛtau spaṣṭam| śākalasūtre
kaiyaṭasvaraso'pyevam| nanvevaṃ nyāyasāmyāt bahuvrīherapi
nityasamāsatvaṃ syāditi cenmaivam,
yasyetyādipadāntarasamabhivyāhareṇānyapadārthapratīteḥ| ata eva
"saṃjñānavagamāt" ityuktaṃ na tvanyapadārthā'navagamāditi| saṃjñāyāṃ
kim ? śīghragaṅgo deśaḥ| 
athāvyayībhāve'sādhāraṇān samāsāntānāha--"avyayī"| samāsānta iti|
samāsasya samāsārthotarapadasya vā caramāvayava ityarthaḥ|
tenopaśaradamityatra "nāvyayībhāvāt" ityam, dvipurītyādau
"dvigoḥ" iti ṅīp ca sidhyati| jarāyā iti| gaṇasūtram|
pratipareti| akṣṇaḥ paramiti| aviṣaya ityarthaḥ| vṛtaviṣaye'kṣiśabda
indriyamātraparaḥ| samakṣamiti| akṣṇo yogyam|
"nadī"|| iha svarūpasyaiva grahaṇaṃ na saṃjñāyāḥ,
paurṇamāsyāgrahāyaṇyoḥ pṛthaggrahaṇāt| upanadīti| ṭajabhāve
napuṃsakahrasvaḥ| idaṃ tvavadheyam| "nadībhiśca" iti sūtre
viśeṣāṇāmiva svarūpasya grahaṇaṃ viśeṣāṇāmevetyatra
mānābhāvādityuktam| tena pañcanadamiti siddhyati| atra ca
nityo'c| taduktam-- 
godāvaryāśca nadyāśca saṃkhyāyā utare yadi|| iti|
etacca "acpratyanvava" ityatrā'jiti yogavibhāgātsiddham| 
pūjārthamiti| anyatarasyāṅgrahaṇasya prakṛtatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
|| ityavyayībhāvaḥ ||

prau.ma.- maghavā bahulam(pā.sū.6-4->

prau.ma.-ṣṇāntā ṣaṭ (pā.sū.1-1->
"ṣṇāntā ṣaṭ"|| strīliṅganirdeaśaḥ saṃkhyāṃ viśeṣayitum|
tatsāmarthyācca pūrvatra saṃjñāparamapi saṃkhyāgrahaṇamiha saṃjñiparaṃ

dṛḍhea'nyadapi bādhyatām" iti nyāyāt|

devadatasya gurukulamitivat samudāyena sambandhānnāsamarthasamāsa
iti; tadāpātataḥ| avayavena sambandhaṃ vyākhyāya samudāyena
sambandhādityukteḥ parasparavyāghātāt| kiṃ ca yatropasarjanapadārthaḥ
sasambandhikastatra dvayīvidhā| sāpekṣo'pi samasyata ityekā|
pradhānapadārthasyaiva kulādergurvādineva devadatādinā'pi sambandhaḥ|
sa ca guruṇā saha utpatigarbhaḥ| devadatena saha tu
tadīyagurūtpādyatvarūpo gurughaṭita iti dvitīyā vidhā| uktaṃ ca
hariṇā--
sambandhiśabdaḥ sāpekṣo nityaṃ sarvaḥ samasyate|
vākyavatsā vyapekṣā hi vṛtāvapi na hīyate|| iti||
samudāyena sambandho yeṣāṃ gurukulādinā|
saṃspṛśyāvayavāṃste tu yujyante tadvatā saha||iti ca||
atra upasarjanaṃ na sambandhikam| yena dvayī vidhā'vakāśamāsādayet|
nanvihāpyantaśabdaḥ sasambandhikārtho'stīti cet ? upadeśakāle yau
ṣakāranakārāviti vadadbhistadanvayasya tyaktatvāt| anyathā hi

antaśabdo hi avayavini sākāṅkṣo na tu yatra kutracit| anyathā
ṛddhasya rājapuruṣa iti syāt| rājño'pi īśiturīśatavyaṃ prati
sākāṅkṣatvāt|
"rājā prabhau nṛpe candre yakṣe kṣatriyaśakrayoḥ" iti koṣāt|

saṃnipāteti| tathā copadeśakāla iti vyākhyānaṃ niṣphalamapīti

prau.ma.-aṣṭābhya āṬś (pā.sū.-->

seṭi" iti seḍgrahaṇājjñāpakāt pūrvasmādvadhau

prau.ma.- ReĀ prathamayoram (pā.sū.-->

prau.ma.- atvasantasya cādhātoḥ (pā.sū.-->

prau.ma.-tyadādiṣu dṛśo'nālocane'kañ ca (pā.sū.-->

prau.ma.- numvisarjanīyaśarvyavāye'pi (pā.sū.-->

prau.ma.- puṃso'suṅ (pā.sū.-->
"puṃso'suṅ"|| vivakṣita iti| tena paramapumānityatra

prau.ma.- na mu ne (pā.sū.-->

prau.ma.-ṅmo hrasvādaci ṅmuṇnityam (pā.sū.8-3-32>

mātraśabdasyeti| tasya cāvadhāraṇamarthaḥ| ṭhmātraṃ kārtsnye'vadhāraṇe'
ityamaraḥ|prātipadikārthādīnyeva
tanmātramityevakāreṇāsvapadavigrahaḥ| mayūravyaṃsakāditvātsamāsaḥ|
kṛṣṇa iti| yadyapi nīlarūpavatparo'yamaniyataliṅgastathā'pi
vāsudeve bhagavati niyataliṅga eveti bhāvaḥ| jñānamiti| bhāve
lyuṭ| droṇa iti| na ca prātipadikamātre prathamehā'stviti vācyam ?
tathā sati paricchedyaparicchedakabhāvasya saṃsargavidhayā
bhānāyogānnāmārthayorabhedasaṃsarga eveti vyutpateḥ| na
cāṣaṣṭhaḷrthabahuvrīhau vyabhicāraḥ, ekārthībhāvābhyupagamāt| nāpi
nipātārthe, teṣāṃ dyotakatā'bhyupagamāt| na ca siddhānte'pi
prakṛtipratyayārthayorabhedānvayo durlabhaḥ, anyatrādṛṣṭatvāditi
vācyam ? devatārthakataddhitādau dṛṣṭatvāditi bhāvaḥ| vacanaṃ
saṃkhyeti| tathaiva prācāṃ vyavahārāt|
iha sūtre'rthe liṅge ca prathametyetāvadeva suvacam| itaratsarvaṃ
tyaktuaṃ śakyam| tathā hi--arthe prathametyukte kasyārthe ityākāṅkṣāyāṃ
yasmātsvādividhistasyeti labhyata eva| svādayaśca
prātipadikālliṅgapratyayaviśiṣṭācca vidhīyante| tadarthatvañca tato
niyatopasthitikatvameva| evaṃ ca mṛḍānītyādau
puṃyogamahatvāderapi ṅyantānniyatabhānena prathamā
siddhyatītyaparamanukūlam| yathānyāse tu sā na syāt| yadi tu--
arthaśabdo vṛtiviṣayaparaḥ, vṛtistu śaktirlakṣaṇā ca, gauṇī tu
lakṣaṇāntarbhūtā pṛthagvāstu; trikaṃ prātipadikārthaḥ- tadā
liṅgetyapi māstu| lakṣyāṇāṃ vicitratve'pi na kṣatiḥ,
niyamāṃśatyāgāt| tathā ca arthe prathametyeva sāram| droṇo
vrīhirityapi droṇaśabdasya tatparimite upacārātsidhdam,
gaurvāhīka itivat| eko dvāvityādyapi ṭhna kevalā prakṛtiḥ
prayoktavyā' iti niṣedhādeva siddham, ananvitārthakaprayogāpekṣayā
anuvādakamātraprayogasya nyāyyatvāt| evaṃ mātragrahaṇamapi vyartham,
"sambodhane ca" iti jñāpakena karmādyādhikye prathamāyā apravṛteḥ|
yatu prācoktam--prātipadikārthaḥ sateti| yacca vyākhyānaṃ yo'rthaḥ
sannityeva kevalaṃ bhāsate na jātyādirūpe na
liṅgasaṃkhyākārakayogī, sa vivakṣitaḥ| yathāvyayārthaḥ|
yathā'vyayārtho hi kevalamastīti śakyate vaktuaṃ na punarīdṛśastādṛśa
itīti cet ? na, uccairnīcairityādiśabdānāṃ
sannityākārakapratītijanakatvasya sarvatantraviruddhatvāt,
astyādisāmānadhikaraṇyasyāpi asatvārthakeṣu durlabhatvāt, na
liṅgasaṃkhyākārakayogiti tvaduktavirodhācca
padāntaropasthāpitasya astisāmānādhikaraṇyamastīti cet ? na, tasya
avyāvartakatvāt, na punarityādivākyaśeṣavirodhācca, kṛṣṇaḥ
śrīrityādāvavyāpteśca| na ca liṅgagrahaṇātatsiddhiḥ,
mātraśabdenā'dhikavyavacchedāt|
yadapi prācā vyākhyātaṃ-yatrārthāntārānapekṣayā śabdapravṛtiḥ sa sarvaḥ
prātipadikārthaḥ, śabdapravṛtinimitamiti yāvat| yathā
uccairnīcairiti| aliṅgasaṃkhyāvetau kevalaṃ jātyādi nirapekṣaṃ
svārthamāhaturiti; tadapi na, pravṛtinimitamiha prakārībhūya
bhāsate| tathā ca tanmātrabodho'sambhavī| yadi so'pīṣṭastarhi
ghaṭādiśabdebhyo'pi syāditi saṃkhyākārakādyananvaya eva syāt|
yadapi droṇaparimite vrīhyādau droṇaśabdaḥ
so'yamityabhisambandhātpravartate| tatra gauṇamukhyanyāyānna prāpnotīti
parimāṇagrahaṇamiti; tanna, padakāryeṣvayaṃ nyāyo na
prātipadikakāryeṣviti siddhāntāt, anyathā siṃho māṇavaka
ityādyasiddhiprasaṅagācca|
yadapyāhuḥ--droṇādayaḥ śabdā lauhaṃ dārumayaṃ vā parimāṇameva na
vyabhicaranti, parimeyaṃ tu vyabhicarantyeva| atastasya
prātipadikārthatvābhāvādaprāpteḥ parimāṇagrahaṇamiti; tadapi na,
gauṇalākṣaṇikeṣu ṭhsiṃho māṇavakaḥ' ṭhmañcāḥ krośanti'
ityādiṣvavyāptyāpateḥ| tatra viśeṣyasya māṇavakāderbhede'pi
siṃhasadṛśa ityartho gauṇyāṃ niyatopasthitika eveti yadi, tarhi
vrīhyāderbhede'pi droṇaparimito niyatopasthitika eveti tulyam|
prau.ma.-saṃbodhane ca (pā.sū.-->
"sambodhane ca"|| mātragrahaṇātsaṃbodhanādhikye na prāpnotītyārambhaḥ|
iha saṃbodhanaṃ pra kṛtyarthaṃ prati viśeṣyaṃ, kriyāṃ prati viśeṣaṇamiti
sidhdāntaḥ| uktaṃ ca --
sambodhanapadaṃ yacca tat kriyāyāṃ viśeṣaṇam|
vrajāni devadateti nighāto'tra tathā sati|| iti.
evaṃ ca ṭhrāma māṃ pāhi' iti vākyasya rāmasaṃbandhisaṃbodhanaviṣayo
matkarmakaṃ rakṣaṇārthaḥ||
iti prathamā ||
prau.ma.- karturīpsitatamaṃ karma (pā.sū.-->
"karturīpsitatamaṃ karma"|| karturiti "ktasya ca vartamāne" iti
kartari ṣaṣṭhī| āpnoteḥ sannantāt "matibuddhipūjārthebhyaśca" iti
vartamāne ktaḥ| matiricchā na tu buddhiḥ ,punarbuddhigrahaṇāt| evaṃ ca
kartrā āptumiṣyamāṇaṃ karmetyarthaḥ| kartā ca dhātūpātavyāpārāśrayaḥ|
sa ca kenā''ptumiccchatītyākāṅkṣāyāṃ viśeṣaṇībhūtena
vyāpāreṇetyarthāllabhyate| tadetadāha--kriyayeti|
ktapratyayenopasthitaṃ vartamānatvaṃ neha vivakṣitam| tena ṭhkaṭṃa
kṛtavān' ṭhkaṭṃa kariṣyati' ityādisiddhyati| kārakamiti| etacca
"kārake" ityadhikārāllabhyate| tatra hi vyatyayena prathamārtheta saptamī|
pratisūtraṃ vākyaṃ bhitvā kārakasaṃjñā vidhīyate| tadyathā-apāye dhruvaṃ
kārakasaṃjñaṃ syāt| tataḥ "apādānam" uktaṃ kārakamapādānasaṃjñaṃ
syāt| punaḥ kārakaśabdānuvṛtisāmarthyāt viśeṣasaṃjñābhiḥ saha
samāveśaḥ| anvarthā ceyaṃ saṃjñā--karotīti kārakamiti|tena
kriyā'nanvayino na bhavati--brāhmaṇasya putraṃ panthānaṃ pṛccchatīti| iha
hi brāhmaṇaḥ putraviśeṣaṇaṃ na tu kriyānvayi|
yadvā kārakaśabdaḥ kriyāparaḥ, karoti kartṛkarmādivyapadeśāniti
vyutpateḥ| tathā ca apādānādisaṃjñāvidhau kriyayāmityasyopasthityā
kriyānvayināmeva tatatsaṃjñā vidhīyante| "kārakāddataśrutayoḥ"
ityādau tu kārakaśabdaḥ svaryate| tenaitadadhikāroktaṃ
kartrādiṣaṭkameva gṛhyate| asmin pakṣe vṛtāvapi kārakamityasya
kriyānvayītyarthaḥ| ādyapakṣe tu kārakasaṃjñā syāt| tacca kārakaṃ
karmasaṃjñaṃ syāditi vyākhyeyameva|
prau.ma.--anabhihite (pā.sū.2-3-1>
"anabhihite" || nanu bahupaṭuarityādau bahucā uktārthatvādyathā
kalpabādayo na, evaṃ kriyate kaṭaḥ ityādāvapi
tiṅadibhiruktārthatvād dvitīyā na bhaviṣyati, kiṃ ca kaṭṃa
karotītyādau sāvakāśā dvitīyā ṭhkṛtaḥ kaṭaḥ' ityādiṣu prathamayā
bādhiṣyate, niravakāśatvāt| na ca vṛkṣa ityādiravakāśaḥ, tatrāpi
pratīyamānāmastikriyāṃ prati kartṛtvena tṛtīyāprasaṅgāt| atha
nīlamidaṃ na tu raktamityādau viśeṣaṇāntaranivṛtitātparyake
astikriyāyā anāvaśyakatvāt prathamāyā avakāśaṃ brūṣe, tarhi
vipratiṣedhāt prathamāstu| evaṃ cānabhihitādhikāro vṛtheti cet? 
maivam, saṃkhyā vibhaktyartha iti pakṣe sūtrārambhāt| tathā
cā'nabhihite karmaṇi yadekatvaṃ tatra dvitīyaikavacanamamityarthaḥ|
