tasyāmavasthāyāṃ teṣāṃ cetanatvāt| smārayatīti| yatu--"ṇeraṇau"
iti sūtre ṭhsmarayatyenaṃ vanagulmaḥ svayameva' ityudāhriyate; tatra
bhāṣyaprayogādeva karmatvaṃ bodhyam| śabdāyateriti| śabdaṃ
karotītyarthe "śabdavaira" ityādinā kyaṅ, tato hetumaṇṇic|
dhātvartheti| etena ṭhśabdāyayati sainikairipūn' iti karma
prayuñjānāḥ parāstāḥ| yatu prācā--ṭhayakrandaśabdāyavheñāṃ na'
ityuktam| tatrā'yateruktistālannirmūlaiva| krandavheñostu
śabdakriyātvamātreṇa prāptisambhāvanā| vastutastu nāsau sūtrārtha
ityuktam|
yadapi ṭhśrugrahadṛśām' iti karmatvamuktam| tatra dṛśigrahaṇaṃ
prāmāṇikameva| śṛṇotestu śabdakarmakatvātsiddham| grahestu
dvikarmakatvaṃ yadyapi sudhākarādīnāṃ saṃmataṃ tathā'pi
bahūnāmasammatam| ata eva ṭhajigrahataṃ janako dhanustad' iti
bhaṭṭiprayogamajigrahat bodhitavāniti vyācakhyuḥ| tathā--
ayācitāraṃ na hi devadevamadriḥ sutāṃ grāhayituṃ śaśāka||
ityatra grāhayitum--udvāhyatvena bodhayitumiti|
yaktaṃ caitat| grāherdvikarmakatve hi
jāyāpratigrāhitagandhamālyāmityatra ktena prayojyāyā dhenorabhidhānaṃ
syāt, ṭhṇyante kartuśca karmaṇaḥ' ityukteḥ| tataśca jāyayā gandhamālye
pratigrāhitāmiti syāt| siddhānte tu pratigrāhite gandhamālye
yayeti vigrahaḥ| jāyāniṣṭhapreraṇaviṣayībhūtaṃ gandhamālyakarmakaṃ
yatpratigrahaṇaṃ tatkartrīmityarthaḥ| anyapadārthāntarbhāveṇa
viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvavaiparītyenaivaikārthībhāvakalpanāt|
abhivādidṛśoriti| abhivādayateraprāpte dṛśestu prāpte
vibhāṣeyam|
prau.ma.-abhiniviśaśca (pā.sū.-->
"abhini" || neralpāctaratvena pūrvanipāte kartavye
viparītoccāraṇaṃ
tādṛśasamudāyavivakṣārthamityāha--saṃghātapūrvakasyeti| teneha na-
ṭhniviśate yadi śūkaśikhā pade' iti| kvacinneti|
"eṣvartheṣvabhiniviṣṭānām" iti bhāṣyaprayogaśceha mānam|
upapadavibhaktimāha- ubhasarvatasorityādinā| ubhasarvayostasau
ubhasarvatasau, tadantayoryoge dvitīyā kāryetyarthaḥ| atra
prāñcaḥ--ubhaśabdenobhayaśabado lakṣyate, anyathā
taso'sambhavādityāhuḥ|
vastutastu vṛtiviṣaye ayacpravṛtāvapi ubhaśabdādvihato yastas
tadantamastyeveti yathāśrutaṃ sādhu| dhigityavibhaktiko nirdeaśo
gavityayamāhetivaditi prāñcaḥ| tanna, tathā sati apadāntatayā
dṛṣṭānte "lopaḥ śākalyasya" ityasyeva dārṣṭāntike jaśtvasyāpi
apravṛtiprasaṅgāt| na cāyaṃ gakārānta eveti bhramitavyaṃ, "kasya ca
daḥ" iti sūtre dhakidityudāharaṇasyāsaṅgatiprasaṅgāt| tasmād
dhigiti vibhaktyantameva| ṭhprakṛtivadanukaraṇam' ityatideśātsupo
luk| ubhayata kṛṣṇamiti| kṛṣṇasya pārśvadvaye'pītyarthaḥ|
ādyāditvātasiḥ| ṣaṣṭhaḷrthe dvitīyā| evamupapavibhaktau sarvatra bodhyam|

nāmno dvidhaiva sambandhaḥ sarvavākyeṣvavasthitaḥ|
sāmānādhikaraṇyena ṣaṣṭhaḷa vā'pi kvacidbhavet||
iti vṛddhoktau ṣaṣṭhīgrahaṇaṃ tadapavādavibhakterapyupalakṣaṇam|
dvidhetyuktiḥ sāmāmādhikaraṇyavaiyadhikaraṇyābhyāṃ nirvahyā,
ṣaṣṭhītadapavādayorekakoṭitayā vā| dhik kṛṣṇābhaktamiti| tasya
nindyatetyarthaḥ| kathaṃ dhiṅ mūrkheti ? sambodhanapadasya kriyānvaya iti
prāgevoktatayā dhikśabdayogābhāvāt| kriyapadaṃ ca kvacicchrataṃ
kvacidākṣiptamityanyadetat| tathā ca mūrkhasambodhyakaṃ
niṣiddhācaraṇādi nindyata iti vākyārthaḥ| etena ṭhhā tāta' ṭhhā devi
dhīrā bhava' ityādi vyākhyātam|
yatvāhuḥ--ṭhupapadavibhakteḥ kārakavibhaktirbalīyasī' iti; tanmandam,
sambodhane prathamaiva bhavati, saṃbodhanapadasya
kartṛkārakavācitvāditi; tanmandam, saṃbodhanapadasya
kartṛkārakatve uktisambhavābhāvat| deva prasīdetyādau vastugatyā
deva eva karteti cet ? naitāvatā kārakavibhaktitvaṃ labhyate| kiṃ ca
deva tvāṃ bhaje, tvāṃ bhajantītyādau saṃbodhyasya vāstavamapi na
kartṛtvamityāstāṃ tāvat| uparyuparīti| kathaṃ tarhi
ṭhuparyuparibuddhīnāṃ carantīśvarabuddhayaḥ' ? ucyate,
uparibuddhīnāmutānabuddhīnāmupari carantītyarthaḥ| evaṃ ca
āmreḍitatvābhāvānna dvitīyā| yadvā- pratipadoktasya "uparyadhyadhasaḥ
sāmīpye" iti kṛtadvitvasyātra grahaṇam, tena vīpsāyāṃ dvirvacane
sati nāsya pravṛtiḥ| "anyatrāpi dṛśyate" ityuktaṃ, taddarṣayati--abhitaḥ
parita iti| pratibhātīti| eṣa pratiśabdaḥ kriyāviśeṣakatvādupasargo
na tu karmapravacanīyaḥ|
antarā tvāṃ māmiti| tava mama ca madhye ityarthaḥ|
sāmarthyāditi| ata eva hetutvaparyantamapi śābdabodhe viṣaya iti
svīkriyata iti bhāvaḥ|
nadīmanviti| dvitīyāyāḥ sambandho'rthaḥ| sa ceha sāhityarūpa
ityanunā dyotyate|
prau.ma.- lakṣaṇetthambhūtākhyānabhāgavīpsāsupratiparyanavaḥ (pā.sū.-->
"lakṣaṇettham"|| vṛkṣaṃ pratīti| vṛkṣasambandhi dyotanamityarthaḥ|
saṃbandhaśca lakṣyalakṣaṇabhāva iti pratyādayo dyotayanti| bhakto viṣṇuṃ
pratīti| viṣṇorbhakteśca viṣayaviṣayibhāvaḥ pratyādidyotyaḥ|
lakṣmīriti| lakṣmīrūpasya bhāgasya hariṇā saha svasvāmibhāvaḥ| vṛkṣaṃ
vṛkṣamiti| iha vīpsā dvirvacanenaiva dyotyate, pariśabdastu kriyayā
saṃbadhyate| karmaṇi dvitīyā| karmapravacanīyasaṃjñayā upasargasaṃjñāyā
bādhāt ṣatvaṃ na | etadarthameva lakṣaṇādayo viṣayatayopātā iti
vyākhyātamākare| "pañcamyapāṅparibhiḥ" iti tu na bhavati,
varjanārthenāpetyanena sāhacaryāt|
mamābhiṣyāditi| mama bhāgaḥ syādityarthaḥ| māmabhiṣyādityapi prāñcaḥ
pratyudāharanti| tatra māṃ prāpnuyādityarthaḥ| upasargavaśena asteḥ
sakarmakatvānmāmiti dvitīyā, atra ca mama bhāga ityarthataḥ
paryavasānāt| abhāge iti karmapravacanīyatve niṣiddhe
"upasargaprādurbhyām" iti ṣatvam|
pūjāyāṃ ceti| "suḥ pūjāyām" iti pūrvasūtrāt
pūjāgrahaṇamanukṛṣyate|
prau.ma.-kālādhvanoratyantasaṃyoge (pā.sū.-->
"kālādhvanoḥ" || antaṃ-virāmamatikrānto'tyantaḥ sa cāsau saṃyogo
nirantarasannikarṣaḥ ityarthaḥ| dvitīyā syāditi| śrutatvāt
kālādhvavṛtibhyāmeva| māsasyeti| "kṛtvo'rthaprayoge
kāle'dhikaraṇe" iti ṣaṣṭhītyāhuḥ, tanna, tatra śeṣagrahaṇānuvṛtyā
ṭhdvirahno bhojanam' iti kṛdantena saha samāsāpravṛtereva
phalatvāt| tasmādiha "ṣaṣṭhī śeṣe" iti sūtreṇaiva ṣaṣṭhīti tatvam|
|| iti dvitīyā||
prau.ma.-svatantraḥ kartā (pā.sū.-->
"svatantraḥ kartā" || pradhānībhūtadhātvarthaṃ prati āśrayatvaṃ svātantryama|
āha ca-
dhātunoktakriye nityaṃ kārake kartṛteṣyate || iti|
prau.ma.- sādhakatamaṃ karaṇam (pā.sū.-->
"sādhakatamaṃ karaṇam"|| prakṛṣṭeti| yadvyāpārādanantaraṃ
kriyāniṣpatistatprakṛṣṭam| uktaṃ ca--
kriyāyāḥ pariniṣpatiḥ yadvyāpārādanantaram|
vivakṣyate yadā yatra karaṇaṃ tatadā smṛtam|| iti|
vivakṣyate ityanena sthālyādīnāmapi vaivakṣikakaraṇatvamastīti
dhvanayati|
āha ca--
vastutastadanirdeaśyaṃ na hi vastu vyavasthitam|
sthālyā pacyata ityeṣā vivakṣā dṛśyate yataḥ|| iti|
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ- kārakatvaṃ tadvyāpyakartṛtvādiṣaṭkaṃ ca vastuviśeṣe
viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvavanna vyavatiṣṭhate| na hi gaussarvaṃ prati gaureva
na tu kiṃcitpratyagauritivad viśeṣaṇaṃ viśeṣaṇameveti suvacam|
tathā ca kayācid dhātuvyaktyā upasthāpite'rthe kiṃ kartrādikamiti
praśene prakṛtavyaktyupātavyāpārāśrayatayā vivakṣitaḥ kartā,
vyāpāravyadhikaraṇaphalāśrayaḥ karma,
kartṛkarmadvārakasambandhenāśrayo'dhikaraṇam| pacyartho
vyāpāraścānekadhā| evaṃ ca
paceradhiśrayaṇataṇḍulāvapanaidhopakarṣaṇāditātparyakatve tadāśrayo
devadataḥ kartā, jvalanatātparyakatve edhāḥ kartāraḥ,
taṇḍuladhāraṇādiparatve sthālī kartrī, avayavavibāgādiparatve
taṇḍulāḥ kartāraḥ| sthālyā pacatītyatra tṛtīyopātavyāpārāśrayā'pi
sthālī karaṇameva, na tu kartrī, devadatādivyāpārasyaiva
taddhātūpātatvāt| tathā--ādikhādibhyāmupāte'rthe prayojyaḥ kartā|
tasminnevārthe bhakṣayatinopāte karma| adhipūrvaiḥ śīhādibhirupāte'rthe
ādhāraḥ karma, kevalairupāte adhikaraṇamityādi | etena jñānasya
svaprakāśatve kartṛkarmavirodha ityādi parāstam,
śabdaviśeṣopādhikasya kartṛtvādeḥ pratyakṣādau uktisambhavābhāvāt|
etena parasamavetakriyājanyaphalaśālitvaṃ karmatvaṃ ced
apādāne'tavyāptimāśaṅkya dhātvarthatāvacchedakaphalaśāli karmeti
pariṣkurvanto'pi pratyuktāḥ, grāmaṃ gamayati devadatamityādau
gantaryavyāpteḥ| tanmate gamanasya prakṛtyarthatve'pi
tatvānavacchedakatvāt| tatra ṇico vyāparārthakatvaṃ na prakṛteḥ| tatve
vā pācayatyādiprayojyakartaryativyāpteḥ|
pāribhāṣikamanuśāsanopayogi karmatvaṃ vacanabalāt
vyavasthitamiti ced ? apādāne'pi tarhi tannāstītyavadhehi,
ekasaṃjñādhikāre'navakāśayā sāvakāśāyā bādhāt| ata eva
ṭhātmānamātmanā hanti' ityādau parayā kartṛsaṃjñayā
karmasaṃjñābādhamāśaṅkyāhaṅkārādiviśiṣṭātmabhedamāśritya
samāhitaṃ bhāṣye|
rāmeṇeti| iha yadyapi vibhaktyupasthitānāṃ kārakāṇāṃ kriyāṃ prati
viśeṣaṇataiva tathā'pi kṛdupasthitānāṃ viśeśyataiva, 'satvapradhānāni
nāmāni"ityukteḥ| dhātūpasthāpyayoḥ phalavyāpārayorapi
karmakatsamabhivyāhāre viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvavyatyāsaḥ| tathā ca
rāmamiṣṭho yo
vyāpārastadviṣayībhūtabāṇavyāpārasādhyaprāṇaviyogāśrayo vālīti
vākyārthaḥ|
yatu vājapeyādhikaraṇe mīmāṃsakairuktaṃ--ṭhkāṣṭhṬaḥ pākaḥ' ityapi
syāditi, tadiṣṭāpatigrastam| "kartṛkaraṇe kṛtā bahulam"
"kartṛkarmaṇoḥ kṛti" ityādīsūtrāṇāṃ kṛtprakṛtyarthena karakānvayaṃ
vinoktisaṃbhavasyaivābhāvāditi sudhībhirākalanīyam|
anabhihite kim ? hariḥ karoti| jīvantyanena jīvanaḥ| śābdikaḥ|
"śabdarduraṃ karoti" iti ṭhak| kṛtaṃ viśvaṃ yena kṛtaviśvaḥ,
viśvakarmakakṛtyāśraya ityarthaḥ; aṣaṣaṭhaḷrthabahuvrīhau
vyutpatyantarasvīkārāt| yathā caitatathā vakṣyate| prakṛtyādibhya iti |
ākṛtigaṇo'yam| tena ṭhnāmnā sutīkṣṇaścaritena dāntaḥ' ityādi
bodhyam| dvayordroṇayoḥ samāhāro dvidroṇam|
pātrāditvātstrītvābhāvaḥ| droṇadvayasambandhi dhānyamityarthaḥ| 
cāditi| karaṇaśabdānuvṛtyā "parikrayaṇe sampradānam"
ityutarasūtrasthānyatarasyāṃgrahaṇāpakarṣaṇena vā saṃjñayoḥ paryāye
labdhe samāveśārthaścakāraḥ| tena ṭhmanasādevaḥ' ityatra karmaṇyaṇ,
karaṇe tṛtīyā cetyubhayaṃ siddhyati| "manasaḥ saṃjñāyām" ityaluk|
kiṃ cākṣerdeavayate yajñadatenetyatra sakarmakatvādaṇi kartarṇau karmatvaṃ
na, "aṇāvakarmakāt" iti parasmaipadaṃ ca neti siddhāntaḥ|
yatu prācā karmasaṃjñaṃ vā syaditi vyākhyātam,
tadākaravirodhādupekṣyam| syadetat, karaṇasaṃjñāyā avakaśaḥ devanā
akṣāḥ, karaṇe lyuṭ| karmasaṃjñāyāstu- dīvyante akṣāḥ, karmaṇi laṭ|
ṭhakṣairdīvyati' ityatra saṃjñādvayaprayuktakāryadvayaprasaṅge paratvāt
tṛtīyaiva syānna tu dvitīyeti cet ? atrāhuḥ- kāryakālapakṣe
"karmaṇi dvitīyā" ityatra yadasyopasthānaṃ tasyanavakāśatvād
dvitīyeti| nanu ṭhdīvyante akṣāḥ' ityatra karmaṇyabhihite'pi
karaṇatvasyānabhidhānāt tṛtīyā syāt tathā ṭhdevanā akṣāḥ' ityatra
lyuṭā karaṇasyābhidhāne'pi karmaṇo'nabhidhānāt dvitīyā syāt ?
maivam, ekaiva hyatra śaktiḥ saṃjñādvayayoginī| tathā ca
ubhayatrāpyabhidhānameva na tvanabhihitatvam|
prau.ma.--sahayukte'pradhāne (pā.sū.-->
"sahayukte"|| putreṇeti | puturatra kriyāsaṃbandhaḥ śābdaḥ, putrasya tu
ātha iti tasyāprādhānyam| apradhānagrahaṇaṃ śakyamakartuma| na caivaṃ
piturapi tṛtīyāpatiḥ| tatra prātipadikarthamātre
antaraṅgatvātprathamotpateḥ| ṭhpiturāgamanam' ityādau
kārakavibhakteḥ prābalyācca|
prau.ma.-yenāṅgavikāraḥ (pā.sū.-->
"yenāṅgavikāraḥ"|| aṅgaśabdo'rśa ādyajantastadāha--aṅgina iti|
yeneti sarvanāmnā prakṛtyarthabhūto'vayava eva gṛhyate, sannidhānāt|
sacārthādvikṛta eva, nahyavikṛtenāvayavena śarīrasya vikāraḥ
saṃbhavati| tadetadāha--yenāṅgena vikṛteneti| 
saṃjānīta iti| "sampratibhyāmanādhyāne" iti taṅ| kṛdyoge paratvāt
ṣaṣṭhī pituḥ saṃjñātā| yatu haradatenoktam- ādhyāne paratvād
"adhāgartha" iti ṣaṣṭhī ṭhmātuḥ saṃjānāti' iti ;tanna, tatra
śeṣādhikārāt|
dravyādīti| uktaṃ ca--
dravyādiviṣayo hetuḥ karaṇaṃ niyatakriyam|| iti|
anāśrite tu vyāpāre nimitaṃ heturiṣyate || iti ca|
phalamapīti| etacca ṭhpratyaya' iti sūtre kaiyaṭe spaṣṭam| evaṃ ca
tādarthyacaturthyā saheyaṃ tṛtīyā vikalpyate| adhyayanāya vasati| ayaṃ
tu viśeṣaḥ--adhyayanasya phalena sahābhedaḥ saṃsargaḥ, upakārakatvena saha
tu nirūpakateti| gamyamāneti| na tu śrūyamāṇaivetyāgraha iti bhāvaḥ|
sādhaneti| sādhyamityatra prakṛtibhāgasyārthaṃ pratīti bhāvaḥ| ṭhmā
cāpalāyeti gaṇān vyanaiṣīt' ityatra tu yatadhvamiti śeṣaḥ|
aśiṣṭeti| etacca ṭhdāṇaśca sā ceccaturthyarthe' ityanena jñāpyate||
|| iti tṛtīyā ||
anvarthasaṃjñābalenāha-dānasyeti| tena ṭhajāṃ nayati grāmam' ṭhhastaṃ
nidadhāti vṛkṣe' ityādau nātiprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ| dānaṃ
cāpunargahaṇāya svasvatvanivṛtipūrvakaṃ parasvatvotpādanam| ata eva
ṭhrajakasya vastraṃ dadāti' ityādau na bhavati ; tatra hi dadātirbhāktaḥ|
etacca vṛtikṛnmatam |bhāṣyamate tu nānvarthatāyāmāgrahaḥ,
ṭhkhaṇḍikopādhyāyaḥ śiṣyāya capeṭāṃ dadāti' iti prayogāt|
ṭhrajakasya vastraṃ dadāti' iti tu śeṣatvavivakṣāyāṃ bodhyam| na
caivam ṭhajāṃ nayati grāmam' ityatrāpi prasaṅgaḥ ṭhyamabhipraiti'
ityuktyā hi yamiti nirdiṣṭasya śeṣitvaṃ, karmaṇaśca śeṣatvaṃ labhyate|
grāmasya ajāṃ prati śeṣitvaṃ nāsti| dānīya iti| bāhulakāt
sampradāne anīyar|
prau.ma.-rucyarthānāṃ prīyamāṇaḥ (pā.sū.-->
"rucyarthānāṃ prīyamāṇaḥ"|| prīñ tarpaṇe, asmāt karmaṇi laṭ| haraye
rocate iti| hariṃ prīṇayatītyarthaḥ| karmasaṃjñāyāṃ prāptāyāṃ vacanam|
prīyamāṇaḥ kim ? devadatāya rocate modakaḥ pathi| atra patho mā
bhūt|
prau.ma. ślāghanhuṅsthāśapāṃ jñīpsyamānaḥ (pā.sū.-->
"ślāghanhuṅ"|| ślāghā stutiḥ| kṛṣṇāya ślāghate| kṛṣṇaṃ
stautītyarthaḥ| anye tvāhuḥ--kṛṣṇāyātmānaṃ paraṃ vā ślāghyaṃ
kathayatītyartha iti| tatrādye pakṣe karmatve prāpte dvitīye tu
kārakaśeṣatvāt ṣaṣṭhaḷaṃ prāptāyāṃ vacanam| hanute iti| sapatnībhyaḥ
kṛṣṇaṃ hanuvānā tamevārthaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ bodhayatītyarthaḥ| yasya kasyacit
hanute iti vā| tiṣṭhate iti| sthityā svābhiprāyaṃ bodhayatītyarthaḥ|
"prakāśanastheyakhyayośca" iti taṅ| 
prau.ma.-dhārerutamarṇaḥ( pā.sū.-->
"dhārerutamarṇaḥ" || ṣaṣṭhaḷaṃ prāptāyāṃ vacanam| 
prau.ma.-spṛherīpsitaḥ (pā.sū.-->
"spṛherīpsitaḥ"|| paratvāditi| tena ṭhparaspareṇa spṛhaṇīyaśobham'
ṭhspṛhaṇīyaguṇairmahātmabhiḥ' ityādi siddham| yadā tu śeṣatvavivakṣā
tadā ṣaṣṭhī| tena --
ṭhkumārya iva kāntasya trasyanti spṛhayanti ca'|
iti siddham| etacca sarvaṃ haradatādirītyoktam|
vākyapadīyahelārājagranthayostu "spṛhayatiyoge karmasaṃjñāyāḥ
śeṣaṣaṣṭhaḷaśca bādhikeyaṃ sampradānasaṃjñā iti sthitam| tanmate
spṛhaṇīyamityatra dānīya itivat sampradāne kṛtyaḥ| kumārya ivetyatra
tu vibhaktivipariṇāmena kāntāya spṛhayantīti vyākhyeyam|
prau.ma.-krudhadruherṣyāsūyārthānāṃ yaṃ prati kopaḥ (pā.sū.-->
"krudhadruha" || krudha kope, druha drohe, īrṣya īrṣyāyām| asūyeti
kaṇḍvādiyagantaḥ|eṣāmartha ivārtho yeṣāṃ dhātūnāmityarthaḥ| tatra
krodho'marṣaḥ, droho'pakāraḥ,īrṣyā asahanam, asūyā tu guṇeṣu
doṣāropaḥ| krudhadruhorakarmakatvātadyoge ṣaṣṭhī prāptā, anyayoḥ
sakarmakatvād dvitīyā praptā|
prau.ma.- rādhīkṣyoryasya vipraśnaḥ (pā.sū.-->
"rādhīkṣyoḥ"|| kārakamiti| karmakārakamiti tu prācaḥ pramādaḥ,
ekasaṃjñādhikārāt| na ca prāthamikaprāptimātrābhiprāyeṇa
karmatvavyapadeśaḥ, kṛṣṇasambandhi śubhāśubhaṃ
paryālocayatītyarthābhyupagamena ṣaṣṭhaḷa eva prāpteḥ|
prau.ma.- anupratigṛṇaśca (pā.sū.-->
"anupratigṛṇaśca"|| gṛṇa iti śnāntasyānukaraṇāt ṣaṣṭhī| hotre iti
| karmatve prāpte|
prau.ma.- parikrayaṇe sampradānamanyatarasyām (pā.sū.-->
"parikrayaṇe" || krayo--dhanādyarpaṇenā''tyantikasvīkaraṇam, tasya
samīpaṃ pariśabda ācaṣṭe|tacca niyatakālaṃ tato nyūnatvāditi
vyācaṣṭe--niyateti| bhṛtyā--vetanādinā| tādarthye iti| tasmai
kāryāyedaṃ tadartham--kāraṇam, tasya bhāvastādarthyam| ṣyañā
upakārakatvarūpaḥ sambandho'bhidhīyate, ṭhkṛtaddhitasamāsebhyaḥ
sambandhābhimānaṃ bhāvapratyayena' iti siddhāntāt| upakārakatvaṃ
cānekadhā| tena brāhmaṇāya dadhītyādāvapi bhavati| bhaktirjñānāyeti|
jñānātmanā pariṇamata ityarthaḥ| prakṛtivikṛtyorbhedavivakṣāyāṃ
vikṛtivācakāccaturthī| abhedavivakṣāyāṃ tu paratvātprathamaiva,
bhaktirjñānaṃ kalpata iti| yadā tu "janikartuḥ" iti bhakterapādānatvaṃ
vivakṣyate tadā jñānasyābhihitakartṛtvātprathamaiva, bhakterjñānaṃ kalpate|
ṭhklṛpī'tyarthagrahaṇamityāśayenāha--sampadyata ityādi| utpāteneti|
prāṇināṃ śubhāśubhasūcako bhūtavikāra utpātaḥ, tena jñāpite'rthe
vartamānācturthītyarthaḥ| vātāyeti| vātasya jñāpiketyarthaḥ|
hiteti| samāsavidhānājjñāpakāditi bhāvaḥ| evaṃ sukhayoge'pi
bodhyam| 
"tumuṇvulau kriyāyām" iti sūtramahimnā kriyārthakamupapadaṃ
kriyāvācyeva phalatītyāśayenāha-kriyeti| kriyāphalakaṃ
kriyāvācaprakṛtikamityarthaḥ| ṭhtumun' iti ṇvulo'pyupalakṣaṇam|
phalebhyo yātītyasya phalānyāharaka ityapi vivaraṇe bādhakābhāvāt|
karmaṇīti| tathā ca dvitīyāpavādo'yamiti bhāvaḥ| 
yatu prācā vyākhyānamaprayujyamānasyaiva karmaṇi yathā syāt,
prayujyamānasya mā bhūditi niyamāya sūtramiti, tanna, aprāptasya
niyamāyogāt| na ca ṭhtādarthya' iti prāptiḥ| na hi phalārthā
yānakriyā kiṃ tvāharaṇārthā| āharaṇaṃ tu phalakarmakamityanyadetat|
tumuno bhāva eva vidhānātadarthasya bhāvavacanatve siddhe
punarbhavavacanagrahaṇaṃ sūtraviśeṣaparigrahārthamityāha- iti sūtreṇeti|
na ca tādarthye ityeva gatārthatā śaṅkyā, bhāvavacanenaiva tumuneva
tādarthyasya dyotitatayā prātipadikārthamātre prathamāprasaṅgāt|
yatu prācoktam--bhāvavacanādeva yathā syāt pācako vrajatīti
ṇvulantāt mā bhūditi niyamārthamidaṃ sūtramiti; tanna, ṇvalante
kartuḥ prādhānyāt kartāraṃ prati ca tādarthyābhāvāt,
duṇībhūtayānasya ca vibhaktyarthānanvayāt|
nanu ṣaṣṭhaḷpīti kutaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḷevāstu, alaṃ śabdastu paryāptītarārthaka
eva gṛhyatāmityāśaṅkyāha--tasmai prabhavatīti|
na tvāṃ tṛṇamityādi| nanu tṛṇādivad yuṣmaccchabdādapi pakṣe
caturthyā bhāvyam| maivam, ṭhanādare' iti hi karmaṇo
viśeṣaṇam--anādaradyotakaṃ yatkarmeti| tādṛśaṃ tṛṇameva na tu
yuṣmadarthaḥ| 
|| iti caturthī ||
prau.ma.- dhruvamapāye'pādānam (pā.sū.-->
dhruvamiti|| dhru gatisthairyayoḥ, asmātkuṭādeḥ pacādyac| dhruva sthairye
iti pāṭhe tu igupadhalakṣaṇaḥ kaḥ| dhruvaṃ--sthiram,
prakṛtadhātūpātagatyanāviṣṭatve sati tajjanya vibhādāśrayaḥ|
taccārthādavadherevetyāha--avadhibhūtamiti| dhāvata iti| iha
kriyāviśiṣṭasyāpyaśvasya prakṛtadhātūpātakriyāṃ pratyavadhitvaṃ na
viruddhyate | ṭhparasparasmānmeṣāvapasarataḥ' ityatra tu sṛdhātunā
gatidvayasyāpyupādānād ekaniṣṭhāṃ gatiṃ pratītarasyāpādānatvaṃ na
virudhyate| uktaṃ ca--
apāye yadudāsīnaṃ calaṃ vā yadi vā'calam|
dhruvamevātadāveśātadapādānamucyate ||
patato dhruva evāśvo yasmādaśvātpatatyasau|
tasyāpyaśvasya patane kuḍaḷdi dhruvamiṣyate||
meṣāntarakriyāpekṣamavadhitvaṃ pṛthak pṛthak|
meṣayoḥ svakriyāpekṣaṃ kartṛtvaṃ ca pṛthak pṛthak||iti|
parvatātpatato'śvātpatatītyatra tu
parvatāvadhikapatanāśrayo'śvastadavadhikaṃ
devadatādiniṣṭhapatanamityarthaḥ| pañcamyarthe'vadhau abhedena saṃsargeṇa
prakṛtyartho viśeṣaṇam| pratyayārthastu kriyāyāṃ, sa ca dharmī na tu
dharmamātram, vibhaktyarthe vihitasya bahuvrīherdharmiparatāyā
nirvavādatvāt| uddhṛtaudanā sthālītyatra hi
odanakarmakoddharaṇāvadhibhūtetyarthaḥ| jugupseti| bhāṣyakārastu
jugupsādīnāṃ tatpūrvakanivṛtilakṣakatāmāśritya sūtreṇa
tatsiddhimāhuḥ| evamutarasūtreṣvapi |
prau.ma.-bhītrārthānāṃ bhayahetuḥ (pā.sū.-->
"bhītrā" || kathaṃ tarhi--
"kasya bibhyati devāśca jātaroṣasya saṃyuge"
iti rāmāyaṇam ? atrāhuḥ-kasya saṃyuge iti | na caivaṃ
saṃyugasyaivāpādānasañjñāpatiḥ, parayā'dhikaraṇasaṃjñayā bādhāt|
adhikaraṇatvāvivakṣāyāṃ tviṣṭāpatiḥ| adhyayanāditi|
addhyayanasambandhinī glānirityarthaḥ|
prau.ma.- janikartuḥ prakṛtiḥ (pā.sū.-->
"janikartuḥ prakṛtiḥ"|| jananaṃ janiḥ--utpatiḥ| ṭhiñādibhyaḥ' iti
janerbhāve iñ, ṭhjanighasibhyāmiṇ' ityuṇādisūtreṇa iṇ vā|
"janivadhyośca" iti vṛddhipratiṣedhaḥ| tasyāḥ karteti śeṣaṣaṣṭhaḷa
samāsaḥ| tathā ca dhātvantarayoge'pi bhavati| ṭhaṅgādaṅdātsambhavasi'
iti yathā| tadetadāha- jāyamānasyeti| etena ṭhikśtipau
dhātunirdeaśe' iti ikā nirdeaśo'yamityāśritya "gamahana"
ityupadhālopaprasaṅgamarthāsaṅgatiṃ samasānupapati codbhāvayanto
mīmāṃsāvārtikakārāḥ samāhitā iti bhāvaḥ| iha sūtre prakṛtigrahaṇaṃ
hetumātraparamiti vṛtikṛnmatam, putrātpramodo jāyate'
ityudāharaṇam| upādānamātraparamiti tu bhāṣyakaiyaṭamatam|
tadubhayasādhāraṇamudāharaṇamāha--brahmaṇa iti| brahmā- hiraṇyagarbhaḥ| sa ca
hetureva, na tūpādānam| kiṃ ca bra hma māyāśabalam, taddhi
sarvakāryyopādānam, iti vādāntasiddhāntaḥ|
prau.ma.-bhuvaḥ prabhavaḥ (pā.sū.-->
"bhuvaḥ"|| bhavanaṃ bhūriti| sampadāditvādbhāve kvip| pūrvasūtre
samāsanirdiṣṭamapi kartṛgrahaṇamanuvartate,
svaritatvādityāha--bhūkarturiti| prabhavatyasmāditi prabhavaḥ| lyablopa
iti| lyabantasya gamyamānārthakatvādaprayoge ityarthaḥ| yataśceti|
yadavadhitvenāśrityādhvanaḥ kālasya vā nirmāṇaṃ paricchedaḥ pratipādyate
tasmādityarthaḥ| tena pañcamyantārthenā'rthadvārā
prayuktānnirmīyamāṇā'dhvavācinaḥ prathamāsaptamyau staḥ| kalāditi|
tadyauktādityapekṣyate|
prapañcārthamiti| na ca "itarastvanyanīcayoḥ"
ityamarokternīcārthakasyedaṃ grahaṇamiti vācyam, "asmātāro mandro
vā" ityādāviva "pañcamī vibhakte' ityanenaiva siddhatvāt|
yatu itara iti nirdiśyamānapratiyogika eva prayujyate | tadyathā-- caitraḥ
śūraḥ, itaraḥ kātara iti| ato nāyamanyārtha iti; tanmandam,
pratiyogyā''kāṅkṣāyā ubhayatra tulyatvāt udāhṛtakośavirodhācca|
ṛte kṛṣṇāditi| kathantarhi ṭhpuruṣārādhanamṛte' iti
?pramādo'yamityeke| anye tu ṭhubhasarvatasoḥ' ityatra dṛśigrahaṇāt
ṭhcaitraṃ yāvacchītam' ityādāviva ṛtayoge dvitīyāpi
sādhurityāhuḥ| yaktaṃ caitat, ṭhṛte dvitīyā ca' iti cāndrasūtrāt|
prabhṛtyarthayoga iti| prabhṛtyarthairyoga ityarthaḥ| tathā ca "kārtikyāḥ
prabhṛti" iti bhāṣyaṃ vivṛṇvan kaiyaṭaḥ prāyuṅkta--tata ārabhyetyarthaḥ
iti|
vacanagrahaṇāditi| sāmarthyādavadhimātraṃ vivakṣyate| tena vineti
viśeṣaṇāṃśastu neti bhāvaḥ| yadvā-- maryādeti śabda
ucyate'smiṃstanmaryādāvacanam--"āṅmaryādābhividhyoḥ" iti
sūtram, tatra ya āṅ dṛṣṭaḥ sa uktasaṃjña ityarthaḥ|
parihareti| ṭhparervarjane vā vacanam' itivārtikātpakṣe
dvirvacanābhāvaḥ| pariratreti| atara pañcamīvidhau varjanārthenāpena
sāhacaryāditi bhāvaḥ|
prau.ma.- pratinidhipratidāne ca yasmāt (pā.sū.-->
"pratinidhipratidāne ca"|| asmādeva nirdeaśādābhyāṃ yoge
pañcamītyāhuḥ| mukhyasyā'bhāve tatsadṛśa upādīyate sa pratinidhiḥ|
datasya pratiniryātanaṃ pratidānam|
prau.ma.-akartaryṛṇe pañcamī (pā.sū.-->
"akartari"|| pañcamī syāditi| tṛtīyāpavādo'yam| śateneti|
śatamiha utamarṇāya dhāryamāṇatvādṛṇam| "tatprayojako hetuśca" iti
cakāreṇa kartṛsaṃjñā ca|
yogavibhāgāditi| etacca "hetumanuṣyebhya" iti sūtre haradatagranthe
spaṣṭam| striyāṃ ceti| ṭhbāhulakaṃ prakṛtestanudṛṣṭeḥ' iti
vārtikanirdeaśo'pīha jñāpakaḥ|
prau.ma.- pṛthagvinānānābhistṛtīyānyatarasyām (pā.sū.-->
"pṛthagvinā"|| samuccayārthamiti| nipātānamanekārthatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
pañcamīti| tatra maṇḍūakaplutyā pañcamī| dvitīyā tu sannihitaiva|
dūraṃ, dūrāt, dūreṇa veti| iha saptamyapi vakṣyate| kiṃ ca
"dūrāntikārÂthebhya" iti
sūtrasyotaratrāpyanuvartanādadhikaraṇe'pyebhyo vibhakticatuṣṭayaṃ
bodhyam| tathā ca prayujyate--ṭhdūrādāvasathānmūtram' iti| āvasathasya
dūre ityarthaḥ| asatveti| satvavacanatvaṃ ca tadviśeṣyakatvam| evaṃ ca
sāmānādhikaraṇyena prātipadikārthaviśeṣyakaṃ vyudasyate| tadāha-
dūraḥ panthāḥ iti| etena "karaṇe ca stoka" iti sūtre ṭhkaraṇe
kim-kriyāviśeṣaṇe karmaṇi mā bhūt--stokaṃ pacati, kriyā na
dravyam' iti prācāṃ grantho vyākhyātaḥ| yatu--tamanūdya kriyāyā
viśeṣyatvena dravyatvā'vaśyambhāvāccintyamityūcustadeva cintyam,
dhātuvācyāyāḥ kriyāyāḥ asatvarūpasyākare prasaddhatvāt| na ca
viśeṣyatve tadanupapatiḥ,
kṛdabhihitabhāvavalliṅgasaṅkhyādyayogitvamātreṇa tadupapateḥ| yadyapi
"hataśāyikāḥ śayyante' ityādau bhāṣye saṃkhyānvayaḥ svīkṛtaḥ, ṭhpacati
bhavati' ityādau kartṛtvakarmatvānvayaśca, tathāpi liṅgasya
kārakāntarasya cā'bhāvo virbādha eva| uktaṃ ca--
kriyā na yujyate liṅgakriyānādhārakārakaiḥ|
asatvarūpatā tasyā iyamevāvadhāryatām||
|| iti pañcamī ||
rājñaḥ puruṣa iti| pratyayarthasya prakṛtyarthaṃ prati prādhānayādapradhānādeva
ṣaṣṭhī| tadarthaśca puruṣe viśeṣaṇam| pradhānātu na ṣaṣṭhī,
uktanyāyavirodhāt, śeṣatvābhāvācca| puruṣaśabdo hi yathāyathaṃ
prātipadikārthamātravṛtiḥ kārakārthako vā| yadā tu naivaṃ tadā
puruṣaśabdādapi ṣaṣṭhī bhavatyeva| yatu --apradhānaṃ śeṣa iti, tanna,
prātipadikārthamātre ṭhśuklo ghaṭaḥ' ityādau viśeṣaṇe
ṣaṣṭhīprasaṅgāt| na ca prathamayā bādhaḥ, tasyāḥ prādhānye
caritārthatvāt| karmadīnāmapīti| yathā viśeṣā'vivakṣāyāṃ
ṭhrūpavān' iti, tadvivakṣāyāṃ tu ṭhnīlaḥ pīta' ityādī prayujyate
tathedamapi nyāyasiddhamiti bhāvaḥ|
satāmiti| evaṃ ca ṭhnapuṃsake bhāve ktasya yoge śeṣatvavivakṣāyāṃ ca'
iti ṭhtṛptyarthānāṃ karaṇe ṣaṣṭhī vā' iti ca prācāṃ granthe paṭhaḷmānaṃ
nāpūrvaṃ vacanamiti bhāvaḥ| sarpiṣa iti| jño'vidarthasya karaṇe,
adhīgarthadayeśāṃ karmaṇi, kṛñaḥ pratiyatne , rujārthānāṃ
bhāvavacanānāmajvareḥ, āśiṣi nāthaḥ, jāsiniprahaṇanāṭakrāthapiṣāṃ
hiṃsāyām, vyavahṛpaṇoḥ samarthayoḥ, kṛtvo'rthaprayoge
kāle'dhikaraṇe ityaṣṭasūtrī tu neha vyākhyātā, tasyāḥ
samāsanivṛtiphalakatvāt| tathā hi--tatra ṭhśeṣa' ityanuvartate|
śeṣatvena vivakṣite ca karaṇādau ṣaṣṭhī siddhaiva| tadayamarthaḥ- iha
ṣaṣṭhaḷeva na tu talluk| tathā ca lukaḥ prayojakībhūtaḥ samāsa eva
neti| tathā ca vārtikam--ṭhpratipadavidhānā ṣaṣṭhī na ' ityādi|
hariścāha--
kārakairvyapadiṣṭe ca śrūyamāṇakriye punaḥ|
proktā pratipadaṃ ṣaṣṭhī samāsasya nivṛtaye|| iti|
tena śeṣatvavivakṣāyāṃ ṭhsarpiṣo jñānaṃ' ṭhmātuḥ smaraṇam'
ityādīnyasamastānyeva sādhūni| ṭhharismaraṇam' ityādīni tu
śeṣatvavivakṣāyāṃ kṛdyogaṣaṣṭhaḷa samāse bodhyāni| tatra ca
kārakapūrvakatvātkṛdutarapadaprakṛtisvaraḥ| śeṣaṣaṣṭhaḷa samāse
tvantodātatvaṃ syāt, taccāniṣṭam| tathā ca svarārtheyamaṣṭasūtrīti
paramaniṣkarṣaḥ| kiṃ ca ṭhmātuḥ smṛtam' ityādau samāsābhāvo'pi
phalam| na hi tatra kārakaṣaṣṭhī labhyate, "na loka" iti niṣedhāt|
āha ca--
niṣṭhāyāṃ karmaviṣayā ṣaṣṭhī ca pratiṣidhyate|
śeṣalakṣaṇayā ṣaṣṭhaḷa samāsastatra neṣyate|| iti|
evaṃ sthite vṛtyādibahugrantheṣu iha prakaraṇe udāharaṇaṃ
pratyudāharaṇaṃ ca yadyatiṅntaṃ dṛśyate tat sarvamupalakṣaṇatayā
neyamiti haradatādayaḥ|
prau.ma.-sarvanāmnastṛtīyā ca (pā|sū|-->
"sarvanāmnastṛtīyā ca"|| iha sarvanāmna iti ṣaṣṭhī, tadāha-sarvanāmnaḥ
prayoge iti| pañcamyāṃ tu hetuśabdāt ṣaṣṭhītṛtīye na syātāmiti
bhāvaḥ| nimitaparyāyeti| paryāyagrahaṇasya
phalamudāharati--evamityādinā| prācā tu ṭhkimartham' ityudāhṛtam|
tatra kimaḥ klībatvaṃ durlabhamÂ| sāmānye napuṃsakatāṃ samāsaṃ
cāśritya kathaṃcidvā neyam| asarvanāmna iti| ṭhnimitakāraṇahetuṣu
sarvāsāṃ prāyadarśanam' iti vārtikaṃ hi vṛtikāreṇa yadyapīhoktaṃ,
tathā'pi bhāṣyakṛtā "hetau" ityatra paṭhitam| tena sarvanāmā'prayoge
api pravartate| kiṃ tu prāyagrahaṇātatra tṛtīyadaya evetyarthaḥ|
varddhamānastu prathamāmapīccchati|
prau.ma.--ṣaṣṭhaḷtasarthapratyayena (pā.sū.-->
"ṣaṣṭhaḷtasarthapratyayena"|| pañcamyā iti | kathaṃ tarhi ṭhtataḥ paścāt' iti
? "tataḥ paścātsraṃsyate dhvaṃsyate ca" iti bhāṣyaprayogātpaścādyoge
pañcamyapi| pratyayagrahaṇaṃ cintyaprayojanam|
prau.ma.- enapā dvitīyā (pā.sū.-->
"enapā dvitīyā"|| kathaṃ tarhi ṭhtatrāgāraṃ
dhanapatigṛhādutareṇāsmadīyam' iti ? utareṇeti
tṛtīyāntatoraṇenetyanena samānādhikaraṇaṃ bodhyam|
prau.ma.-dūrāntikārthaiḥ ṣaṣṭhaḷnyatarasyām (pā.sū.-->
"dūrāntikārthaiḥ"|| anyatarasyāṃ grahaṇaṃ samuccayārtham| tena ca
viprakṛṣṭāpi pañcamī samuccīyate vyākhyānāt, na tu
ṣaṣṭhīdvitīyātṛtīyāḥ| tadetadāha-- pañcamī ceti|
prau.ma.-divastadarthasya (pā.sū.-->
"divastadarthasya"|| taccchabdena vyavahṛpaṇau parāmṛśyete| tau ca dyaūte
krayavikrayavyavahāre ca tulyārthau pūrvasūtre gṛhītau,
ityāśayenāha--dyaūte iti| karmaṇīti| iha ṭhśeṣe' iti na sambadhyate
utarasūtradvayārambhāditi bhāvaḥ| ata eveha trisūtryā
tiṅntamudāharati--śatasya dīvyatīti|
prau.ma.- preṣyabruvorhaviṣo devatāsampradāne (pā.sū-->
"preṣyabruvoḥ" || iṣyedêavadikasya gatyarthasya
loṇmadhyamapuruṣaikavacanaṃ ṭhpreṣya' iti, tatsāhacaryāt bruvirapi
tathābhūta eva gṛhyate| ata eveha śeṣagrahaṇaṃ na sambadhyate tiṅntena saha
samāsasyā'prasaktatvāt|
prau.ma.- kartṛkarmaṇoḥ kṛti (pā.sū.->
"kartṛkarmaṇoḥ kṛti"|| guṇeti| taduktam--
pradhāne niyatā ṣaṣṭhī guṇe tūbhayathā bhavet|| iti|
kṛtapūrvīti| kṛtaṃ pūrvamaneneti vigrahaḥ| pūrvaṃ kṛtavānityarthaḥ|
avivakṣitakarmakatayā bhāve ktaḥ| "supsupā" iti samāsaḥ|
"pūrvādiniḥ" "sapūrvācca" iti kartari iniḥ| tadvidhau "śrāddhamanena"
iti sūtrādanenetyanuvṛteḥ| atha kathaṃ "dhāyairāmodamutamam" iti
bhaṭṭiḥ ? atrāhuḥ--anityamidam, "tadarham" iti nirdeaśāt|
strīpratyayayoriti| kathaṃ tarhi "suṭtithoḥ" iti sūtre ṭhsuṭā
sūyuṭo bādho na' iti vṛtiḥ| karaṇatvavivakṣāyāṃ tṛtīyeti
gṛhāṇa|
prau.ma.- na lokāvyayaniṣṭhākhalarthatṛnām (pā.sū.-->
"na lokāvyayaniṣṭhā"|| uśca ukaśca ūkau, laśca ūkau ceti vigrahaḥ|

durāpe'rthe tvadadhīnā hi siddhayaḥ' ṭhapi vāgadhipasya durvacaṃ vacanaṃ

ityanvayo na tu ikṣvākūṇāṃ durāpa iti| tathā
vāgadhipasyavismayamityanvayo na tu vāgadhipasya durvacamiti|

taitirīyāṇāṃ prātiśākhyam| "yamānusvāranāsikyānāṃ nāsikā,

ityādau taparatvamasandehārthaṃ, na tu tatkālagrahaṇārtham| tena
plutasyāpi saṅgrahaḥ||

ccchandasyamūrdhaprabhṛtyudarreaṣu"iti sūtreṇa saḥ| cari-- jakṣatuḥ||
prau.ma.-saṃyogāntasya lopaḥ (pā.sū.8-2-23>
saṃyogāntasya|| tadantasyeti| alo'ntyaparibhāṣayeti bhāvaḥ| yadyapi
viśeṣaṇena tadantavidhilābhāt saṃyogasyetyeva sūtrayitumucitaṃ
tathāpi pratyekaṃ saṃyogasaṃjñeti pakṣe dṛṣatkarotītyādau lopaṃ
vārayituṃ saṃyogāvantau yasyeti dvivacanāntena
samāsalābhārthamantagrahaṇam| vṛtau hi
upasarjanānāmekatvasaṃkhyautsargikī dvitvādikaṃ tu prayatnalyamiti
bhāṣye sthitam||
"yaṇa--" iti vācanikam| yadvā vācyo vyākhyeyaḥ| vyākhyā ca
dvedhā--"jhalo jhali" (pā.sū.8-2-26)ityato jhalgrahaṇamapakṛṣya jhala eva
lopo vidhīyate iti, antaraṅge lope kartavye bahiraṅgasya
yaṇo'sidhdatvamiti vā| na ca ṣāṣṭhī asidhdaparibhāṣā
tripādīsthatvenāsidhdamantaraṅgaṃ lopaṃ na paśyatīti vācyam|
kāryakālapakṣābhyupagamāt| na ca "nājānantarye bahiṣṭvapraklṛptiḥ"
iti niṣedhaḥ| tasyānityatvāt, utarakālapravṛte lope
ajānantaryābhāvācca| tathā ca vārtikam--"saṃyogāntasya lope yaṇaḥ
pratiṣedhaḥ","na vā jhalo lopād", "bahiraṅgalakṣaṇādvā" iti| iha
yathoddeśapakṣe ādyaṃ vārtikadvayam, tṛtīyaṃ tu kāryakālapakṣe iti
vivekaḥ| yatvāhuḥ-- vārtikakṛto vacanaṃ, pratyākhyānaprakāradvayaṃ tu
bhāṣyakṛta iti| tannirmūlam, matabhede pramāṇābhāvāt|
yadapyāhuḥ--kāvyaṃ, kalatraṃ, śālvaṃ cācakṣāṇaḥ kāv, kalat, śāl;
adabhrayateraghab iti, tadapi na, pakṣatraye'pi saṃyogāntalopasya
durlabhatvāt| ādyapakṣasyaikarūpyārthaṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvamantaraṅgaṃ paraṃ paraṃ
bahiraṅgamityallopaṇilopayorasidhdatvasya supacatvāt| ata eva
kātantrapariśiṣṭe śuklayateḥ kvipi śukl ityudāhṛtam| vāntayoḥ
kvipi ūṭho durvāratvāt| na ca sthānivatvam, kvau vidhiṃ prati
tanniṣedhāt; vici "lopo vyoḥ--"(pā.sū.6-1-66)iti lopasya
prasaṅgācca| giriveśmayatestu kvipi yaṇaḥ pratiṣedhārthaṃ
vacanārambhapakṣe giriveḍityucitam| pratyākhyānapakṣe tu
māntameveti dik|
iti pakṣa iti|| pakṣadvayasyāpi bhāṣye sthitatvāditi bhāvaḥ| catvāri
rūpāṇīti| yatu vadanti-- dhakārayakārobhayadvitve ubhayoḥ punardvitve
lopavikalpe'nyataradvitve ca nava rūpāṇi| tathā hi-- ekadhaṃ
tridhā--ekayaṃ dviyaṃ triyaṃ ca| evaṃ dvidhaṃ tridhaṃ ca tridheti| na
cāntaraṅge dvitve bahiraṅgasya yaṇo'sidhdatvam,
traipādike'ntaraṅge bahiraṅgaparibhāṣā na pravartata iti
visarjanīyasūtre bhāṣyakaiyaṭayorvyavasthāpitatvāditi| atredaṃ
vaktavyam-- punardvitvamaśudhdam, ekasyāṃ vyaktau ekaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ lakṛdeva
pravartate iti "ekaḥ pūrvaparayoḥ" (pā.sū.6-1-84) iti sūtre bhāṣye
sidhdāntitatvāt| anyathā dvitvānāmanantyāpateḥ| na ca "jharo jhari"
(pā.sū.8-4-65) iti "halo yamām--"(pā.sū.8-4-64) iti ca lopena
nirvāhaḥ, lopasya vaikalpikatvāt| kathamanyathā tridhaṃ triyaṃ ceti
bhavaduktiḥ saṅgaccheta| kiṃ caivaṃ sati "sarvasya dve" pā.sū.(8-1-1)iti
punaḥ 
punaḥ pravarteta| ata eva "liṭi dhātoḥ"(pā.sū.6-1-8)iti
sūtre'bhyāsagrahaṇaṃ bhāṣye pratyākhyātam| sannantādyaṅntādvā ṇici caiṅ
dvitvābhāvārthaṃ taditi tu kaiyaṭādibhiruktam| tasmādiha tyaja vā
pañcamādīni rūpāṇi svīkuru vā rūpāntyamiti
navatvoktirasaṅgataiva| sakāradvatvenāṣṭau, visargadvitvena
ṣoḍaśeti tvanyadetat| 
yadapyuktaṃ traipādike'ntaraṅge ityādi, tadapi na, ṭhnārkuaṭa'
ṭhnārpatyaḥ'ityatra visargavāraṇāya vṛdhderasidhdatvasya tatraiva bhāṣye
spaṣṭatvāt| "saṃyogāntalope yaṇaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ" ityasya
bahiraṅgatvena pratyākhyātatvāt| svayamapi tathaivānūditatvācca|
tasmādiha yathoddeśapakṣo grāhyae bahiraṅgaparibhāṣāyā apravṛtaye|
tathā ca bhāṣye mayaḥ parasya yaṇo dvitve ṭhdadhyatra' ityudāhṛtam| na
caivamapi "iko'savarṇe--" (pā.sū.6-1-127) iti śākalena
rūpāntaranastviti vācyam| samāse tanniṣedhāt| na ca nityasamāsa
eva tanniṣedha iti vācyam| bhāṣye nityagrahaṇasya pratyākhyātatvāt|
yathotaraṃ munīnāṃ prāmāṇyāt|
nanviha yaṇeva durlabhaḥ, sudhīśabdasya dhyāyateḥ samprasāraṇena
niṣpannatvena "samprasāraṇācca"(pā.sū.6-1-108) iti pūrvarūpāpateḥ;iti
cet ? na, "samprasāraṇapūrvatve samānāhgagrahaṇam" iti vārtikokteḥ,
samprasāraṇasthānikasyātathātvācca| na caivamapi
"nabhūsudhiyoḥ"(pā.sū.6-4-85) iti yaṇniṣedhaḥ śaṅkyaḥ, āṅgatvena
pratyaye parata eva tasya pravṛteḥ, anantarasyeti nyāyena
"eranekācaḥ--'(pā.sū.6-4-82) iti "ossupi" (pā.sū.6-4-83) iti ca
sūtradvayena prāptasyaiva niṣedhācca|
dhātraṃśa iti| atra rephasya dvitvaṃ na | dvitvaprakaraṇe rahābhyāmiti
sākṣācchØatatvena nimitabhāvena rephasya kāryitvabādhāt| sudhyupāsya
ityādau tu sthānitvena nimitatvamikaḥ
"tasmādityutarasya"(pā.sū.1-1-67) ityādinirdeaśājjñāpakānna bādhyata
iti kaiyaṭaḥ| takārasaya tu bhavatyevetyuktam, tacca vā; ityato'tra
rūpadvayam| lākṛtiriti tvekameva|
prau.ma.- halo yamāṃ yami lopaḥ(pā.sū.8-4-64) 
halo yamām|| lopaḥ syādveti| "jhayo haḥ--"(pā.sū.8-4-62)
ityato'nyatarasyāmityanuvṛteḥ| etacca laṇsūtre bhāṣye spaṣṭam|
māhātmyamiti| atra malopo netyarthaḥ| "halo yarāṃ yari savarṇe
lopaḥ" iti tu na sūtritam| ārdhnot, mūrdhnaḥ, śārṅgam, ārtnī
ityādau rephātparasya lopāpateḥ||
prau.ma.-vānto yi pratyaye(6-1-69>
vānto yi| yakārāditi| viśeṣaṇamātmāntasya sañjñā
syādityetadarthakasya "yena vidhiḥ"(pā.sū(1-1-72)iti
sūtrasyāpavādabhūtena "yasminvidhistadādāvalgrahaṇe" iti
vārtikenāyamartho labhyate| odautoriti| pūrvasūtre tayoreva
vāntau prati sthānitvena nirṇītatvādityarthaḥ| nanu gavyūtirityatra
"hali sarveṣām"(pā.sū.8-3-22) iti, "lopaḥ śākalyasya"(pā.sū.8-3-19)

lunāteraco yat| "sārvadhātukārdhadhātukayoḥ"(pā.sū.7-3-84)iti
guṇaḥ| avaśyalāvyamiti|

bhāvaḥ||
lopo veti| "jhayo honyatarasyām" (pā.sū.8-4-62) iti

bhāṣyakaiyaṭayoḥ spaṣṭam| prācastu vikalpānuvṛteranukternyīnatā|
yadapi vyākhyātṛbhiḥ "śaro'ci'(pā.sū.8-4-49) iti jñāpakamabhipretya
tatsaṃvādārthaṃ bhāṣyamupanyastam-- "anuvartate vibhāṣā śaro'ci
yadvārayatyayaṃ dvitvam" iti, tanna, tadutarabhāṣye eva uktajñāpakasya
dūṣitatvāt| lopasya hi nityatve "aco
rahābhyām--"(pā.sū.8-4-46) iti dvitvaṃ lopāpavādaḥ syāt| tathā ca
"śaro'ci" ityasya sārthakyaṃ spaṣṭameva| tasmādvikalpānuvṛtau

prau.ma.- etyedhatyūṭsu (pā.sū.6-1-89) 
etyedhatyūṭsu||atra ecītyanuvartate| tacca etereva viśeṣaṇamaṃ
natvedhateḥ, avyabhicārāt| nāpyūṭaḥ, asambhavāditi prāñcaḥ| atra
kaiyaṭaḥ--edhaterapi viśeṣaṇam| mā bhavānpredidhadityatra
vyabhicārāt| yadyapīdaṃ kaiyaṭapustakeṣu prāyeṇa na dṛśyate, tathāpi
kvacid dṛśyata eva| na ca "ṇau caṅ--'(pā.sū.7-4-11) iiti hrasve kṛte
nāyamedhatiriti vācyam| ekadeśavikṛtasyānanyatvāt| na caivamapi
"ajāderdvitīyasya"(pā,.sū.6-1-2) iti dhiśabdasthāne dhidhiśabdādeśe
sati vasnasoriva prakṛtipratyayavibhāgasammoha iti vācyam| dviḥ
prayogo dvirvacanaṃ ṣāṣṭhamiti ṣāṣṭhabhāṣye sidhdāntitatvāt|
anyathehaiva ṇilopo na syāt| jighāṃsatītyādau sanaḥ sakāreṇa
viśiṣṭasya dvitve kṛte hrasvo na bhavatyeveti vācyam|
oṇerṛditkaraṇena bahiraṅgasyāpi "ṇau caṅ--"(pā.sū.7-4-1) iiti
hrasvasya prathamaṃ pravṛtiriti jñāpitatvāt|
atra mādhavaḥ--mā bhavānpredidhadityatra nātiprasaṅgaḥ| ṇyantasya
śabdāntaratvādedhatigrahaṇenāgrahaṇāt| ata eva hi "na
bhābhūpūkamigami--" (pā.sū.8-4-34)ityatra "ṇyantānāṃ ca
bhādīnāmupasaṃkhyānam" ityuktam| tatraiva
śabdāntaratvādaprāptāvupasaṃkhyānamiti kaiyaṭanyāsakāraharadatādibhiḥ
sarvairevoktam| kiṃ ca ṇyadhikasyāpi grahaṇe
"upasargātsunoti--"(pā.sū.6-1-91)ityatrābhiṣāvayatītyudāhṛtya
ṇyantenābheryogātsunotiṃ pratyanupasargatvātkathaṃ
ṣatvamityāśaṅkyābheḥ prakṛtyarthaviśeṣakatvena samādhānaṃ
bhāṣyakārādyauktamasaṅgataṃ syāt| na ca "heracaṅ"(pā.sū.7-3-56)iiti
liṅgāt "prakṛtigrahaṇe ṇyadhikasyāpi grahaṇam" iti bhramitavyam|
jñāpakasya kutvamātraviṣayakatvāt| tathā ca vārtikam--"heścaiṅ
pratiṣedhānarthakyamaṅgānyatvāt| jñāpakaṃ tu caṅe'nyatra ṇyante'pi
kutvabhāvasya" iti|
tasmādyauktivirodhādbhāṣyavārtikavṛtinyāsādigranthaiḥ
pūrvāparasvagranthena ca virodhādupekṣyo'yaṃ kaiyaṭa iti|
atredaṃ vaktavyam| astu viśiṣṭasya śabdāntaratvaṃ tāvatā'pi
prakṛtibhāgasyaidhrūpatvaṃ kenāpagatamityavadhāryatām| na hyupaidhata
ityādau tiṅntasyaidhrūpatā nāstīti prakṛtyaṃśe sā hīyate| tathā ca
"upasargātsunoti--"iti sūtre
vārtikam--"sidhdaṃtvavayavānyatvāt"iti|
yatvabhiṣāvayatītyatratyabhāṣyavirodhodbhāvanaṃ, tadviparītām| yataḥ
prakṛteḥ sunotirūpānapāyāt kaiyaṭamate sidhdā yathoktavyavasthā|
tvanmate tu rūpāntaratvena ṇyadhikasyāgrahaṇādasaṅgataiva
prāguktavyavasthā syāt| ko
hyaedhayatisāvayatyorviśeṣaḥ--yadedhaterṇyadhikasyāgrahaṇaṃ sunotestu
grahaṇamiti| yadapi ṇyantabhādīnāmupasaṃkhyānamupanyastaṃ tadapi
nāsmākaṃ pratikūlam, viśiṣṭasya śabdāntaratvena prakṛtyaṃśasya tu
ṇicā vyavadhānenopasaṃkhyānasyāvaśyakatvāt| ata eva na
tatratyanyāsādibhiḥ svoktyā vā virodhaḥ| pratyuta tavaiva virodhaḥ,
ṣāṣṭhe bhāṣyavārtikayoḥ "etyedhatyoḥ--"(pā.sū.6-1-89)iti yogaṃ
vibhajya "eci" iti viśeṣitatvāt| tasmādiha vṛtigrantho
mādhavagranthaścāsaṅgata evābhipretya vyācaṣṭe--ejādyoriti||
akṣauhiṇīti| ūhostyasya ūhinī akṣāṇāmūhinīti vigrahaḥ|
parimāṇaviśeṣaviśiśaṣṭā senā akṣauhiṇī|
"pūrvapadātsaṃjñāyām"(pā.sū.8-4-3) iti ṇatvam| yatu prācā
vigṛhītamakṣāṇāmūhaḥ samūhaḥ so'styasyā iti| tadapi uhinīśabdena
ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣa ityevaṃparatayaiva kathañcinneyam| ūhaśabdena samāse
tata inau tu vṛdhdirna syāt, antaraṅgeṇa guṇena bādhāt|
apavādabhūtāyā api vṛdhderūhinīśabdena vigrahe caritārthatvāt|
yathoktaṃ prāk--"iṇ-ṅ-śīnāmādguṇiḥ savarṇadīrghatvāt" iti||
prau.ma.- svādīreriṇoḥ (kā.vā.>

prau.ma.- pravatsatarakambalavasanadaśārṇānāmṛṇe (kā.vā.>
pravatsatareti|| atra kālāpānusāriṇo vatsaraśabdamapi paṭhitvā

paṭhanti, tatsarvaṃ bhāṣyādivirudhdam||prau.ma.-antādivacca
(pā.sū.6-1-6) 
antādivacca|| ihaikaḥ pūrvaparayorityanuvartate yathāsaṅkhyaṃ
cetyabhipretyāha--pūrvasyāntavaditi| sthānivatsūtreṇaiva gatārthamidaṃ
sūtram| na cālvidhyarthamidamiti bhramitavyam,
asyāpyalvidhāvaniṣṭatvāt| anyathā ayaje indramityatra
savarṇadīrghāpateḥ tathā ca vārtikam--"na vā atādrūpyātideśāt"
iti| na visarga iti| etacca sukhārta ityādāvapi tulyam| atra ca
kāryakālapakṣe bahiraṅgatvādasidhdamapi bodhyam| na ca 
"nājanantarye" iti niṣedhaḥ yatrāntaraṅge bahiraṅge vā
acarānantaryaṃ tatreti haradatapakṣe tatprāptāvapi yatra
paścātpravartamne'jānantaryamiti kaiyaṭamate naṣedhāprāpteḥ|
bahvapekṣatvena bahiraṅgāyāṃ vṛdhdāvajānantaryasatve'pi
paścātpravartamāne tadabhāvāt||
upasargeṇeti|| yatkriyāyuktāḥ prādayastaṃ pratyeva gatyupasargasañjñā
ityuktatvāditi bhāvaḥ| prārṣabhīyatīti| "śaro'ci"(pā.sū.8-4-49) iti

paraḥ" ityeva sidhdeḥ| yathā "ami pūrvaḥ"(pā.sū.6-1-107) iti| 
niyogo'vadhāraṇamiti| yatvāhuḥ--

nāvaklṛptau yadā dṛṣṭaḥ pararūpasya gocaraḥ| 
evastu viṣayo vṛdhderniyame'yaṃ yadā bhavet|| iti||

parasmin--"(pā.sū.1-1-57)iti tripādyāṃ na pravartate| evaṃ ca
"pūrvatrāsidhde na sthānivat" iti siddhānta uktayuktimūlako na tu
vācanika iti prapañcitaṃ śabdakaustubhe'smābhiḥ| nanvevaṃ mātuḥ
kāryamityādau ṣatvaṃ syāditi cet ? na, kaskādiṣu
bhrātuṣputraśabdasya pāṭhena ṅsiṅsorata ukārātparasya ṣatvaṃ neti
jñāpanāt| evaṃ ca ṭhapratyayasya' ityetadapivyadhikaraṇameveti
haradatenoktaṃ nātīvādartavyam| 
māturiti| atra hi "ṛta ut"(pā.sū.8-1-111)ityekādeśaśāstraṃ visargaṃ
prati paramparayā prayojakam| ākare tu "ekādeśaśāstranimitāt"iti
pracuraḥ pāṭhaḥ| tatra ekādeśaśāstraṃ nimitaṃ yasya ukārasya
tasmādityarthaḥ|
muhuḥ kāmeti| iha "kupvoḥ ka pau"(pā.sū.8-3-37)iti pravartate|
vṛtau muhuskāmeti sakāraḥ kvacit paṭhaḷte, tatprāmādikamiti
haradataḥ|
prau.ma.- dvistriścaturiti kṛtvo'rthe(pā.sū.8-3-43>
dvistriścaturiti|| iha dvitribhyāṃ sujantābhyāṃ sāhacaryāt catuḥ
śabdo'pi sujanta eva grahīṣyate| kṛtvo'rthagrahaṇaṃ
jñāpakam--sāhacaryaṃ na sarvatra vyavasthāpakamiti| tena
"dīdhīvevīṭām"(pā.sū.1-1-6)ityatra dhātusāhacarye'pi āgamasyeṭo
grahaṇam| ubhayatra vibhāṣā ceyam| caturityasya
"idudupadhasya--"(pā.sū.8-3-41)iti prāpte, itarayostvaprāpte| na ca
tayorapi "isusoḥ sāmarthye"(pā.sū.8-3-44)ityanena sidhdiḥ
śaṅkyā| isa- pratyayasya arthavataśca tatra grahaṇāt| catuṣkapāla iti|
ṭhidudupadhasya'(pā.sū.8-3-41)iti nityaṃ ṣaḥ|
vyapekṣāvirahe'pīti| tiṣṭhatu sarpiṣkuṇḍikāmānayetyādāvityarthaḥ|
vyapekṣāyāmiti| idaṃ sarpiṣkuṇḍikāyā ityatretyarthaḥ| yatu
pārāyaṇikā āhuḥ--ṭhkaskādiṣu sarpaṣkuṇḍikādīnāṃ pāṭha
utarapadasthatve'pi ṣatvārthaḥ' iti| tadbhāṣyavirodhādupekṣyam| 
ayaḥ sahiteti| na tvayaso vikāraḥ, "jānapada--"(pā.sū.4-1-42)iti
ṅīṣprasaṅgāt| ayaskarṇīti| aya iva karṇau yasyāḥ|
"nāsikodarauṣṭha--"(pā.sū.4-1-55)ityādinā ṅīṣ||

prau.ma.-sasajuṣo ruḥ (pā.sū.8-2-66>
sasajuṣo ruḥ|| padasyetyanuvṛtaṃ sasajūrbhyāṃ viśeṣyate, vaśeṣaṇena
tadantavidhiḥ| na ca sajūḥ śabdāṃśe "grahaṇavatā prātipadikena
tadantavidhirnāsti" iti niṣedhaḥ śaṅkyaḥ| tasya
pratyayavidhivaṣayatvāt| sāntaṃ sajuṣśabdāntaṃ ca yatpadaṃ tasya ruḥ
syāt, sa cālontyasya| evaṃ sthite phalitamāha-- padāntasyeti|
sajuṣśabdasyeti| tadantasya padasyetyarthaḥ, tena sajuṣa ityādau
nātiprasaṅgaḥ| na ca sajūrityatrāvyāptiḥ
"vyapadeśivadbhāvo'prātipadikena" iti niṣedhāditi vācyam|
"yena vidhiḥ" iti sūtre śabdakaustubhe'sya pratyākhyātatvāt|
"vyapadeśivapadbhāvo'prātipadikena" iti, "grahaṇavatā--" iti ca
paribhāṣādvayamapi pratyayavidhiviṣayakamiti diva utsūtre
kaiyaṭaharadatābhyāmuktatvācca| evaṃ ca paramasajūrbhyāmityādyapi
nirbādham| evaṃ sthite iha sajuṣśabdena tadantavidhirneti
prācāmuktirāpātata ityavadheyam||
prau.ma.- bhobhagoago'pūrvasya yo'ci(pā.sū.8-3-17>
bhobhago || asandhiḥ sautra iti| bhagoaghośabdayorokārasyākārasya
ca pūrvarūpaṃ sautratvānnetyarthaḥ| yadi tu bhos, bhagos,

trayāṇāmalo'ntyasya yaḥ syādapūrvasya rośceti vyākhāyate, tarhi
asandhirnyāyya eva| yuktaṃ caitat| anyathā vibhoridaṃ, suprabhā
gauryasya sa suprabhagustasya suprabhagoridaṃ, raghoridamityatrāpi
yatvāpateḥ| bhavatihyayaṃ bheśabdādipūrvo ruḥ| na cātra

bhavadbhagavadaghavatāmoccāvasya" iti vārtikena niṣpannānāṃ

ito'pi hetordhātvaṅgetyādiparigaṇanamaśudhdamityavadheyam|
nipātā iti| "bhobhago--"(pā.sū.8-3-17)iti sūtre

vṛtiharadatādimatenoktam| bhāṣyasvarasarītyā tu "vabhāṣā--"ityādi
vārtikamārabdhavyameva| kiṃ tu nipāto'pi bhoḥ śabdo'sti, tena
dvavacanādau strīnapuṃsakayośca na doṣaḥ| yaktataraṃ caitat| he
bhavannitivat he bho iti prayogasyāpīṣṭatvāt, tasya ca
vārtikārambha eva sidhdeḥ| "syuḥ pyāṭpāḍaṅgahaihebhoḥ"
ityamaroktānāṃ sambodhana#arthānāṃ nipātānāṃ
sahaprayogāyayogāt| kiṃ cātra bhavān haristatrabhavān itivat tatra
bho ityādyapi vārtike satyeva sidhyati| "itarābhyo'pi
dṛśyante"(pā.sū.5-3-14)iti sarvavibhaktyantātratasorbhavadādiyoga
eveṣṭatvāt| kiṃ cāmantritatve padātparasyāṣṭamikanaghātaḥ, "āmantrataṃ
pūrvam--"(pā.sū.8-1-72)ityavidyamānavadbhāvaḥ
"āmaekāntaram--"(pā.sū.8-1-55)iti vidhiḥ,
"vākyāderāmantritasya--"(pā.sū.8-1-8)iti dvirbhāvaḥ,

sidhyati, na tvanyatheti dik| bhago, agho iti nipātāvapi yadi
prāmāṇikau tarhi stāṃ nāma| āmantritakāryasidhdistu
vārtikāyataiveti sudhībhirākalanīyam| yatu

prau.ma.- arthavadadhāturapratyayaḥ prātipadikam(pā.sū.1-2-45>
arthavadadhātuḥ || pratyayaṃ pratyayāntaṃ ceti| sītre tantrādinā ubhayaṃ
vivakṣitamiti bhāvaḥ| tathā hi,pratyasyaiva paryudāse "kṛtaddhita"iti
sūtreṇa taddhitāntasya saṃjñāvidhānaṃ vyarthaḥ syāt| na ca tatrāpi
tadantaṃ na grāhyamiti vācyam|ṭharthavat'
ityanuvṛtisāmarthyātadantagrahaṇam| praśaṃsāyāṃ hi matup|
ekārthībhāvena laukike prayoge prasiddhatvaṃ ca praśaṃsāmūlam| na ca
ṭharthavat' ityasya pūrvasūtre caritārthatā| dhātupratyayaparyudāsenaiva
tallābhāt| tasmātpratyayāntaṃ ca varjayitvetyucitameva|

sarvaḥ kalpita eva| paramārthatastu sphoṭa evārthavāniti siddhāntāt|
uktaṃ ca hariṇā---
upāyāḥ śikṣamāṇānāṃ bālānāmupalālanāḥ| 
asatye vartmani sthitvā tataḥ satyaṃ samīhate ||iti|
kathamanyathā iṅe dvivacanamaḍādayaśca syuḥ| tathā ca pratyāhārāhnike
arthavanto varṇā iti praghaṭṭake adhyeti adhīta
ityatrekāro'rthavāniti bhāṣye spaṣṭamevoktam| evaṃ

śāstrakṛtsaṃmateti cet, na| " dvitīyatṛtīyacaturthaturyāṇi--" iti
sūtre pūraṇātsvārthe'npratyayasya pūraṇārthatāyā
bhāṣyakārairevoktatvāt| pratyaya iti mahāsaṃjñāṃ kurvataḥ
sūtrakṛto'pi saṃmatamevedam| yadapi "oṣadheśca--"ityādi| tadapi
na| anityatve'pyautsargikapravṛtyā iṣṭasiddheḥ, iṣṭasthale'pyapravṛtau
nyāyasya nirviṣayatvāpātācca, anityatvajñāpanasya

saphalatvaucityāt| "oṣadheśca--"ityādau

jñāpakamastu| 
yatu vadanti--pratyayaparyudāso na kartavyaḥ, pratyayāntaparyudāsenaiva

tanna| pratyayagrahaṇe yasmātsa vihitastadādergrahaṇāt| kevalasya ca
atathātvāt| anyathā tvaduktarītyā suptiṅerapi tadantatvāt padatvaṃ
syāt| tathā ca hariṣu, karoṣi, ityādau ṣatvaṃ na syāt

nañdvayopādānaṃ spaṣṭārtham| mahāsaṃjñākaraṇaṃ śrutyanurodhāt|
tathā cātharvaṇe paṭhaḷte--" ko dhātuḥ ? kiṃ prātipadikam ?kaḥ pratyayaḥ
" ? iti| ḍitthādīnyavyutpannānīhodāharaṇam| avyutpatipakṣasya

prau.ma.-sarūpāṇāmekaśeṣa ekavibhaktau (pā.sū.1-2-46>
"sarūpāṇāmekaśeṣa ekavibhaktau" || ekavibhaktau yānīti| vibhaktiḥ
sārūpye upalakṣaṇaṃ na tvekaśeṣe nimitam| evaṃ
cānaimitikatvenāntaraṅgo'mekaśeṣaḥ subutpateḥ prāgeva
pravartate| yadyetannārabhyeta tarhi pratyekaṃ vibhaktiḥ syāt, dvandvaśca
pravarteta| ārabdhe tvekaśeṣe'nekasubantavirahād dvandvasyāprāptireva
phalitā bhavatīti bhāvaḥ| sarūpāṇyeveti|
etaccotarasūtrasthaivakārasyehānukarṣaṇāllabhyate| tena
mātṛśabdayorjananīvāciparicchetṛvācinornaikaśeṣaḥ|
mātṛbhyāmityādau sārūpye'pi āṬjasādiṣu vairūpyāt| eka eveti|

yāvat| yatra hyanekasyārthasya militasyetarānvayaḥ sa tathābhūto'sya
viṣayaḥ| idaṃ ca "idito num dhātoḥ" iti
liṅgāllabhyate,nyāyācca| tathā hi-- yaḥ śiṣyate sa
lupyamānārthābhidhāyīti sthite śrūyamāṇaikaśabdopasthitaṃ

ṭhpanthānaḥ' ityādau samāsāntāpateḥ| ṭhkarau' ṭhkarāḥ' ityādau

jasi"ityanayorāpateśceti dik| yatu prācā ṭhrāma-rāma-āṬ'iti

militenārthavatvāt| na cārthavatve'pi samāsagrahaṇasya

tadviṣayako niyama ityuktatvāt, bahupaṭava ityādyanurodhena niyamasya

dvandvaṃ vinaiva samudāyādvibhaktiḥ syāditi cet ? maivam|
dvandvavidhāvanekasubantamityasyānuvṛtyā ekājdvivacananyāyasya
bādhāditi dik| itthamuktisaṃbhave satyapi samudāyādekavibhaktau
parata iti pakṣaḥ kimarthamupekṣita iti cet ? śrṛṇu, asminpakṣe
mātṛmātarau ityādāvapyekaśeṣaḥ syāt| nanvekavibhaktāvityāvartya
evakāraṃ cānukṛṣya ekavibhaktau yāni sarūpāṇyavati vyākhyeyamiti
cenna| āvṛtau mānābhāvāt| vibhakterekaśeṣasya viśeṣaṇatve
phalābhāvācca| na ca payaḥ payo jarayatītyādivyāvṛtiḥ phalam,
sahavivakṣāyāmityasyāvaśyakatvāt, anyathā payaḥ payo nayatītyatra
gauṇamukhyakarmaṇorekaśeṣāpateḥ| devadeva ityādāvatiprasaṅgācca|
pratyuta āvṛtyā ubhayaviśeṣaṇatve bhikṣāṇāṃ samūho bhaikṣamiti na
sidhyet| tathā hi, bhikṣā bhikṣā bhikṣā ām a iti sthite
"antaraṅgānapi vidhīn bahiraṅgaḥ" iti luki kṛte
vibhaktiparatvābhāvādekaśeṣo na syāt| lupte pratyayalakṣaṇābhāvāt|
kiṃ ca rāmāvityādau paratvānnityatvācca "vṛddhiteci" ityekadeśe

āśrayaṇe'ntādivadbhāvābhāvāditi dik|
yatu prācā vyākhyātrā pakṣāntaramupanyastaṃ-- savibhaktikānāmekaśeṣa
iti| tanna| nivartamānānāṃ vibhaktyā sahaiva nivṛtāvapi śiṣyamāṇe
ekavacanaśravaṇaprasaṅgāt|

ajahatsvārthāyāṃ vṛtau ekaikamanekārthābhidhāyi

prakriyāvākye dvivacanabahuvacanayoreva praveśād

yugapadadhikaraṇavacanatā duḥkhā ca durupapadā ca" iti| 
yadapi vyācakhyuḥ--vyaktipakṣe yāvadvyakti tataccchabdaprayoge prāpte
ekasyaiva prayogo yathā syāditi sūtram| ākṛtipakṣe tu
ekenaivākṛtyavisābhūtānekavyaktilābhādanarthakamiti| tanna,
vyaktipakṣe'pi "na brāhmaṇaṃ hanyāt" ityatra brāhmaṇaśabdenaivaikenāpi
anekasya tantreṇa suvacatvāt| anyathā jātipakṣe'pi "vāsena
samyakkṣamayośca tasmin" ityādinānārthānurodhena sūtraṃ
vinā'nirvāhāt| tasmājjātivyaktipakṣayorubhayorapi tantreṇa
prayogānnirvāhe'pi sarvatra tantreṇaiva bodhanīyamiti
niyāmakābhāvātpākṣiko dvandvaḥ sūtraṃ vinā durvāraḥ| ghaṭo'yaṃ
ghaṭo'yamati nānāśabdaprayogadarśanāt, tadvat ghaṭaghaṭāviti dvandvasya
nyāyaprāptatvāt| na caivamapi ghaṭakalaśāviti dvandvāpatiḥ|
"virūpāṇāmapi samānārthānām" iti vārtikāt| rūpyate bodhyate
iti vyutpatyā rūpaśabdasya arthaparatvamāśritya vārtikārthasyāpi
sūtreṇaiva kroḍīkārācca|tasmād ghaṭaghaṭau
ghaṭakalaśāvityādipākṣikāniṣṭavāraṇāya pakṣadvaye'pi
sūtramāvaśyakamityavadheyam|
evaṃ sthite sūtravaiyarthyameva hṛdi nidhāya yadiha vyācakhyuḥ--
nānenaikaśeṣo vidhīyate ityādi| yacca samudāyādekasyāṃ
vibhaktāviti pakṣaṃ pratyākhyāya punarāhuḥ--rāma rāma āṬ iti sthite
iti| alaukikametatprakriyāvākyamūhyate na tu laukikam| alaukike ca

prayuktānāmevānvākhyānādityādi| tatsarvaṃ sandarbhaviruddhaṃ

śāstrīyakāryaṃ ca sarvamalaukikeṣveva pravartate na tu pariniṣṭhite|

prau.ma.- ami pūrvaḥ (pā.sū.6-1-00>
"ami pūrvaḥ" || amyacīti| ami yo'c tasmin| amo'vayave'cīti
yāvat| tena makārasahitasya na pūrvarūpam| evam
akṣādūhinyāmityādāvapi ūhinyāṃ yo'c tasminnityādi bodhyam|
"iko yaṇaci" ityajgrahaṇasyānuvṛternirvivādatvāt|
"padāsvairibāhyā--" ityādinirdeaśāśceha liṅgam|
prau.ma.- tasmāccchaso naḥ puṃsi (pā.sū.6-1-00>
"tasmāccchasaḥ"|| taccchabdena sannihitaḥ pūrvasavarṇadīrghaḥ parāmṛśyate|
śasa ityavayavaṣaṣṭhī| sa cāvayavaḥ paratvena
viśeṣyate--pūrvasavarṇadīrghāt parasya śaso'vayavasyeti|
tadetadāha--paro yaḥ śasaḥ sakāra iti| paro yaḥ śas iti tu
noktam, asaṃbhavāt| na hi kṛtesavarṇadīrghe paraḥ śas saṃbhavati|
prau.ma.- aṭkupvāṅnumvyavāye'pi (pā.sū.8-4-00>
"aṭkupvāṅ"|| raṣābhyāmiti pañcamīnirdeaśādvyavahitasyāprāptau
vacanamidam| atra sarvairvyavāyo'saṃbhavī, ekaikamātravyavāya ityapi
nārthaḥ| kṣubhnādiṣu kṣubhnaśabdapāṭhasāmarthyāt, "sarūpāṇām"
ityādinirdeaśāccetyabhipretyāha--vyasthairyathāsambhavaṃ militaiśceti|
nanvevam ṭhādarśena'ityādau atiprasaṅgaḥ| na ca aḍādibhireva
vyavadhāne iti niyamaḥ| aprāpte vidhirayamityuktatvāt| satyam,
yogavibhāgo'tra bodhyaḥ--"vyavāye'pi" ityeko yogo vidhyarthaḥ|
"aṭkupvāṅnum" ityanyo niyamārthaḥ| chāndaso bhiso luk|
niṣedhaṃ bādhitumiti| tena paryāṇaddhamityatra "upasargādasamāse'pi"
iti ṇatvaṃ sidhyatīti bhāvaḥ| numgrahaṇamiti| tatsthānikasya
svābhāvikasya cānusvāramātrasyopalakṣaṇamityarthaḥ| tena ṭhbahi vṛddhau'
iditvānnum| tasyānusvāraḥ| ṭhtṛṃhū hiṃsāyāṃ'
svābhāviko'trānusvāraḥ| bṛṃhaṇam, tṛṃhaṇam| ubhayatrāpi ṇatvaṃ
sidhdam| ṭhivi prīṇane' prenvanam| iha "kṛtyacaḥ" "ijādeḥ sanumaḥ"
iti ṇatvaṃ na| yathā "nakṣatraṃ dṛṣṭvā vācaṃ visṛjet" iti vidhau
nakṣatradarśanasya kālaviśeṣo'palakṣaṇatvātsatyapi divā nakṣatradarśane
vāṅ na visṛjyate, upalakṣitasya kālaviśeṣasyābhāvāt| rātrau
asatyapi nakṣatradarśane visṛjyate, tasya satvāt; tathehāpi bodhyam|
nanvevaṃ numgrahaṇaṃ vyartham, anusvārasyāṭtvenaiva saṃgrahāt| uraḥ
keṇetyādau visargajihvāmūlīyādivyavadhāne hyaeṣaiva gatiriti
cetsatyamityāha--tacceti| syādetat| uktarītyā "numvisarjanīya"
iti sūtre'pi numgrahaṇaṃ tyājyaṃ syāt| tatrāpyanusvārasya
lakṣaṇīyatvāt| anyathā'sambhavāt| na hi ṣatvapravṛtivelāyāṃ
ṭhsapaÕṣi' ityadau num labhyate| ṣatvasyāsidhdatvena tataḥ
prāgevānusvārapravṛteḥ| yadyapi ṭhsuhinsu' ityatra saṃbhavatyeva,tathāpi

prau.ma.-yasmātpratyayavidhistadādi pratyaye'ṅgam (pā.sū.1-4-13>
"yasmātpratyayavidhiḥ"|| atra prācoktam--yasmāddhātoḥ
prātipadikādveti|tanna, tiṅntādīnāmapi tarabādikaṃ
pratyaṅgatvāt| saṅkocasya nirmūlatvāt| āmuṣyāyaṇa ityādau

subantāditi dik||
prau.ma.-ato bhisa ais (pā.sū.7-1-00>
"ato bhisa ais"|| nanvesityevāstu| na ca "ato guṇe" iti
pararūpāpatiḥ| ekāroccāraṇavaiyarthyāpateḥ|anyathā isameva
vidadhyaditi cet ? satyam, edaitordvimātratvāviśeṣeṇa
gauravābhāvādais kṛtaḥ| evaṃ ca pararūpaśaṅkaiva nāstīti
mahallāghavam| yatvāhuḥ--aiskaraṇaṃ nirjarasairityaikāraśravaṇarthamiti|
tadbhāṣyaviruddham| sannipātaparibhāṣayā
jarasādeśāpaternirjarairityeva bhāṣye siddhāntitatvāt| 
prau.ma.-nāmi (pā.sū.6-4-2>
"nāmi"|| paribhāṣāvirodhāditi| etacca "rudhādibhyaḥ śnam" iti
sūtre "śnānnalopaḥ" iti sūtre ca bhāṣyakaiyaṭayoḥ spaṣṭam|
ārambhasāmarthyāditi| "supi ca" ityasya tu na sāmarthyam|
rāmābhyāmityādau sāvakāśatvāditi bhāvaḥ| evaṃ ca rāmaśabde "nāmi"
ityasyopanyāso lekhatapramādāditi yadūcustaccintyam| 
prau.ma.- ādeśapratyayayoḥ (8-3-00>
"ādeśapratyayayoḥ"|| ekā'pi ṣaṣṭhī viṣayabhedādbhidyate ityāśayena
vyācaṣṭe--ādeśaḥ pratyayāvayavaśceti| yadi tu ādeśāvayavo gṛhyaeta
tarhi tisṛṇāmityādau doṣaḥ| yadi capratyayo yaḥ sakāra iti tarhi
rāmeṣvityādau na syāt| yadyapyevaṃ vairūpyeṇa vyākhyāne
sahavivakṣāvirahād dvandvo na yujyate, tathā'pi sautratvād bodhyaḥ|
vivṛtāghoṣasyeti| yatu vadanti--sakārasya
soṣmaṇo'ghoṣasyāntaratamaḥ ṣa eva mūrdhanya iti|
tannyūnam,ṭhakāre'tiprasaṅgāt| tathā ca prātiśākhyam--"varge
varge ca prathamāvaghoṣau yugmau soṣmāṇāvanunāsiko'nyaḥ" iti|
nanu dadhiseca ityādau prāk subutpateḥ samāse ṣatvaṃ vārayituṃ
padādādiḥ padādiriti pañcamīsamāso bhāṣye uktaḥ| tataścehāpi
"sātpadādyoḥ" iti niṣedhaḥ syāditi cet ? maivam, svādiṣviti
yā padasaṃjñā tāmāśrityoktaniṣedho na pravartate iti sātigrahaṇena
jñāpitatvāt| yatvāhuḥ--pratyayaviṣaye nāyaṃ niṣadha iti jñāpyata iti|
tanna, vyatise ityādau ṣatvāpateḥ| nanu padasyādiriti pakṣe
ṭhvyatise' ityatra ṣatvaṃ durvāram| na ca vyapadeśivadbhāvena se
ityasyāpi tiṅntatvātpadatvaṃ śaṅkyam| pratyayagrahaṇe yasmātsa
vihitastadādereva grahaṇāt| anyathā ṭhrāmeṣu' ityādāvapi
ṣatvaniṣedhāpatiriti cet ? satyam| na vayaṃ pratyayamātrasya
tadantatāṃ brūmaḥ, kiṃ tu prakṛtipratyayasamudāye pravṛtāṃ
padasaṃjñāmekadeśavikṛtanyāyena luptaprakṛtike pratyaye'pyānayāmaḥ|
ṭhrāmau' ṭhrāmāḥ' ityādāvapi hi ekadeśavikṛtanyāyenaiva
padatvam|ekaideśātprāgeva padasaṃjñāpravṛteḥ| na tvekādeśe kṛte
"suptiṅntam" ityasya prāptirasti| ubhayata āśrayaṇe nāntādivaditi
sidhdāntāt|
yatu vadanti--prakṛtisannidhāne pratyayasya padatvaṃ nāsti ṭhmantreṣu' ṭh
sarveṣām' ityādinirdeaśāditi, tanmandam, uktarītyā padatvābhāve
nyāyasidhde jñāpanasyāsambhavādanupayogācca|
prau.ma.- sarvadīni sarvanāmāni (pā.sū.1-1-00>
"sarvādīni sarvanāmāni"|| sarvādīni iti tadguṇasaṃvijñāno
bahuvrīhiḥ| "hali sarveṣām" iti nirdeaśāt| dvandve cetīti| sa hi
samudāyasya niṣedho, natvavayavānāmiti vakṣyate| na ca tadantavidhiṃ
vinā samudāye saṃjñāprasaktirastīti bhāvaḥ| nanu tadantasaṃjñāyāḥ kiṃ
phalam| na ca paramasarvasmāyityādau smāyādayaḥ phalam,
aṅgādhikāre tadantavidhinaiva tatsidhderityata āha--teneti| na
cehāpi ṭhprātipadikāt' ityanuvṛteḥ sarvanāmnāṃ sadviśeṣaṇe
tadantavidhirbhavatīti vācyam, "samāsapratyayavidhau pratiṣedhaḥ"
ityukteriti bhāvaḥ| akacceti| cakārātparamasarvata ityatra tasil| 
prau.ma.- jasaḥ śī (pā.sū.>
"jasaḥ śī"|| anekāltvāditi| na tu śitvāditi bhāvaḥ| 
avarṇāntāditi| avarṇāntādaṅgātparasyetyarthaḥ| ato yeṣāṃ keṣāṃ
teṣāmityādau nāvyāptiḥ| sarvanāmnā vihitasyāma iti| sarvanāmnaḥ
parasyeti tu noktam, dakṣiṇotarapūrvāṇāmityatra suṭprasaṅgāt|
"dvandve ca" iti niṣedho hi samudāyasya na tvavayavasya| na ca
tasmādayamām vahitaḥ, kiṃ tu samudāyādeveti na suṭ|
vihitaviśeṣaṇatvasyāśrayaṇe pramāṇaṃ tu "dakṣiṇotarapūrvāṇām" iti
bhāṣyaprayoga eva| 
pañcatriṃśaditi| pañca ca triṃśacceti dvandvaḥ| pañcādhikāstriṃśaditi
tatpuruṣo vā| yatu pratyāhārā eracatvāriṃśaditi prāco granthaṃ
vyācakṣāṇairuktam--nāyaṃ madhyamapadalopī tatpuruṣaḥ| tathā sati
"rājanyabahuvacanadvandvendhakavṛṣṇiṣu" "saṃkhyā" iti vidhīyamānaḥ
pūrvapadaprakṛtisvaro na syāt| tasmātsamāhāradvandva evāyam|
ekaviṃśatirityādau napuṃsakatvaṃ tu na bhavati
lokāśrayatvālliṅgasyeti| taccintyam, samāsabhedena svarabhede
iṣṭāpateḥ| tathā ca "bahuvrīhau saṃkhyeye" iti sūtre
saṃkhyāyāstatpuruṣasyopasaṃkhyānaṃ nistriṃśādyarthamanyatrādhikalopāt"
iti vārtikaṃ vyācakṣāṇo bhāṣyakāra āha--ekādhikā
viṃśaterekaviṃśatiḥ, dvāviṃśatiriti|
ata eveti| dvayordvivacanasyaiva nyāyyatvāditi bhāvaḥ| nanvevaṃ
sarvādiṣu pāṭho vyarthaḥ, sarvanāmakāryāṇāṃ smāyādīnāṃ

gatārthatvāt| na ca vṛtikṛtā "sarvanāmnastṛtīyā ca" iti sūtre
paṭhitatvādidaṃ vārtikaṃ sarvanāmasaṃjñāsāpekṣamiti vācyam bhāṣye
"hetau" iti sūtre tasya paṭhitatvāt| ata evānnena kāraṇena vasati,
annasya kāraṇasyetyudāhṛtaṃ haradatenetyāśaṅkyāha-tasyeha

ubhaśabdādavayavinyarthe'yac syāt| ubhayoḥ maṇiḥ| tato "nityam" |
ubhaśabdād vṛtiviṣaye nityamayacsyāt svārthe|
tenobhayapakṣavinītanidrā ityādau ayayac yathā nityaṃ evaṃ
kapratyaye'pi syāditi| nanvevamakaci kṛte'pi ayaj durvāra iti
cenmaivam| yogavibhāgasyeṣṭasidhyarthatayā "ubhau sābhyāsasya" iti
nirdeaśena ca yatra dvivacanaṃ na śrūyate tatraivāyamiti vyākhyānāt|
tathā ca vārtikam--"anyābhāvo dvivacanaṭābviṣayatvāt" iti| akaci
hi sati "tanmadhyapatitaḥ tadgrahaṇena gṛhyate"iti nyāyenobhayaśabdasya
dvivacanaviṣayatvaṃ na vihanyate, kapratyaye tu vihanyataiva|
taduktamiti| vārtikakṛteti śeṣaḥ| yadyapi pūrvavārtike
ṭābapyuktastathā'pi nāsau vivakṣitaḥ, ṭābviṣayatvamapi dvivacanaparatva
eva boddhavyamiti kaiyaṭādibhirvyākhyātatvādityāśayena vyācaṣṭe--
dvivacanaparatvābhāve iti| ṭābekādeśasyāpi pūrvāntatvena grahaṇād

durupapādamubhayata āśrayaṇe'ntādivadbhāvavirahāt
tathāpyubhaśabdādutpannaṃ dvivacanaṃ lukā nāpahṛtaṃ yatra tato'nyatrāyac
itīha vivakṣito'rthaḥ| yadyapi bhāṣye kapratyayasyāpi
svārthikatvenobhaśabdāt parasya
dvivacanatvamastītyāśrityobhaśabdapāṭhaḥ pratyākhyātaḥ, tathā'pi
prauḍhivādamātraṃ tat| kṛtrimasyaiva dvivacanasyeha
grahītumucitatvāt| anyathā tratasilādāvatiprasaktatvāt| nanu

nābhidata iti ced na, kapratyayasyāpyajñātārthabodhakatvāt
pañcakapakṣe'dhikabodhakatvadhrauvyācceti dik|
| nāstīti | anabhidhānāditi bhāvaḥ| tathā cobhayaśabdasya
dvivacanānutvādādasarvavibhaktikatvenāvyayatve prāpte

nityaiveti| ṭhpare'vara ubhayā amitrāḥ' iti tu chāndasatvādbodhyam|
avyutpannāviti| etacca paspaśāyāṃ kaiyaṭe spaṣṭam|
eke-- kāśikākārāḥ| ṭhetantvaṃ manye' ityudātasya ṭhutatvaḥ paśyan'
ityanudātasya ca darśanāditi bhāvaḥ| ekaḥ--prathamaḥ| saṃhitayā pāṭhe
tāntatvasyāsphuṭatve'pi tāntaśchetavyo'nyathā ekaśrutyā sakṛdeva
paṭhet| na ca vipratipatavyam "tvatvasamasimetyanuccāni" iti
phiṭsūtrāt| tathā ca ṛṅmantraḥ-- "starīru tvadbhavati sūta u tvam"
iti| vedabhāṣyakārāścetthaṃ vyācakhyuḥ-- tvaditi sarvanāmasu
paṭhito'nudāto'yamanyaparyāya iti| jayadevo'pi prāyuṅkta-
"tvadadharamadhuramadhūni pibantam" iti| tvato'nyasyā adhara iti
vigrahaḥ, na tu tavādhara iti| "paśyati diśi diśi rahasi bhavantam"
iti pūrvavākyena sahānanvayāpateḥ| nema iti| śrūyate ca --
"pranemasmindadṛśe soma' iti| sama iti| tathā ca śrūyate
"nabhantāmanyake same" | "māno vṛkāya vṛkye samasmai"| "uruṣyāṇo
aghāyataḥ samasmāt" | "uto samasminnā" ityādi |
"simaḥ kṛtsne ca śakte ca syānmaryādāvabuddhayoḥ" |
śrūyate ca--"ādrātro vāsastanute simasmai"| "ucchØakramatkamajate
simasmāt"| ubhayatrāpi simaśabdaḥ sarvaparyāya iti vedabhāṣyam|
gaṇasūtra iti| yadyapi bhāṣye aṣṭādhyāyīsthe sūtre "apuri iti
vaktavyam" iti vārtikaṃ paṭhitaṃ tathāpi tadgaṇasūtrasya śeṣaḥ, na tu
jasi vibhāṣāvidhāyakasya| ṭhata' ityadhikṛtya jasaḥ śīvidhānāt

tadantarvartinyāṃ vetyarthaḥ| liṅgaviśiṣṭaparibhāṣayā ekādeśasya

uktaparāmarśakau| tatrādyaśchāndasa iti gaṇaratnakāraḥ| tanna, "syaścchandasi

eko'nyārtho pradhāne ca prathame kevale tathā|
sādhāraṇe samāne'lpe saṃkhyāyāṃ ca prayujyate||

ādhunikasaṃketo hi saṃjñā| na ca dikṣu sā'sti iti astātiprakaraṇe

prau.ma.-- "svamajñātidhanākhyāyām" (pā.sū.1-1-35>
svamajñātidhanākhyāyām || ātmātmīyajñātidhanavācī svaśabdaḥ, tatra
jñātidhanayoḥ paryudāsāt ātmā ātmīyau pariśiṣṭāvityāśayena
vyācaṣṭe--ātmīyā ityarthaḥ, ātmāna iti veti| yatvāhuḥ--ātmani
svaśabdo napuṃsakaḥ, tena sve svā ityudāharaṇaṃ tatra na sambhavatīti|
taccintyam| ātmanyapi svaśabdasya puṃstvāt| "vibhāṣā jasi" iti
prakaraṇe "svamātmīye" ityeva vaktavye gurunirdeaśaṃ kurvataḥ
sūtrakārasyaivātra pramāṇatvāt| yadapi svokte'rthe upaṣṭambhārthaṃ--
svo jñātāvātmani svaṃ triṣvātmīye svo'striyāṃ dhane|
iti amarasiṃhoktimāhuḥ| tadapi cintyam| kośe hi ātmanīti
pūrvānvayi| svamiti tu utarānvayi| tathā ca hemacandraḥ- 
dyauḥ svarganabhasoḥ svo jñātyātmanoḥ svaṃ nije dhane||iti|
medinīkāro'pi--
svaḥ syātpuṃsyātmani jñātau triṣvātmīye striyāṃ dhane||iti|
tasmāt sve svā ityasyātmāna iti vivaraṇaṃ samyageveti sthitam ||
jñātidhanavācistviti| tācchīlye ṇininā jñātidhanayorapi
ātmīyatvaṃ puraskṛtya pravṛtau sarvanāmatā'styeveti dhvanayati|
etadarthameva hi sūtre ākhyāgrahaṇaṃ kṛtam|
prau.ma.-antaraṃ bahiryogopasaṃvyānayoḥ (pā.sū.1-1-36>
"antaraṃ bahiryogopasaṃvyānayoḥ"|| bahirityanāvṛto deśo bāhyaṃ
cocyate| tatrādyamarthaṃ gṛhītvā''ha--bāhyā ityartha iti| dvitīye tu
ābhyantarā ityartho bodhyaḥ | bāhyaena hi ābhyantarasya yogo'sti|
athadvayamapyakāre sthitam| upasaṃkhyānaśabdo'pi
karaṇavyutpatyotarīyaparaḥ, karmavyutpatyātvantarīyaparaḥ|

prau.ma.-- na bahuvrīhau (pā.sū.1-1-29>
"na bahuvrīhau"|| kṛte bahuvrīhau niṣedho vyarthaḥ ṭhupasarjanībhūtā
na sarvādaya' iti vakṣyamāṇatvādata āha-cikīrṣite iti|
samāsātprāgeveti ca | prakriyeti| na ca nimitasyānapasarjanatvasya
vināśonmukhatvātprāgeva saṃjñā na bhaviṣyatīti vācyam|
akṛtavyūhaparibhāṣāyā anityatvāt| tatra caitatsūtrasyaiva
jñāpakatvāt| atibhavakāniti| etadeva jñāpanaphalamiti bhāvaḥ|
iṣṭāpatiṃ kṛtveti| "cchÂavoḥ śūḍ" iti satukkagrahaṇaṃ,
prakṛtipratyayāpativacanaṃ vā anityatve jñāpakam,

bhāṣyakāroktyaiva vyavastheti bhāvaḥ|
yatu prācoktam-- bahuvrīhau sarvādeḥ sarvanāmatā na syāt |
priyasarvāya | sūtrakāramate bahuvrīhau na sarvanāmatā| bhāṣyakāramate

sarvādīnāmiti ? nādyaḥ|
samāsāvayavayoryuṣmadasmadorityutaragranthavirodhāt, samudāye

ityādi| taditthaṃ prācā vyākhyātam--tvaṃ pitā yasya, ahaṃ pitā
yasyeti vigṛhya bahuvrīhisamāse kṛte samāsāvayavayoḥ
samāsāntaḥpātinoryuṣmadasmadoḥ sarvārthavācakatvena
sarvanāmasaṃjñakatvādajñātādyarthavivakṣāyām "avyayasarvanāmnām"
ityakac prasajyeta| tasya
smaiprabhṛtīnāmivāṅgādhikāravihitatvābhāvenāṅgasaṃjñānapekṣatvāt| tataśca tvakatpatṛka ityadyaniṣṭaṃ prasajyeteti| nyāsakṛtā'pi anvarthasaṃjñābalādevasaṃjñānirāse kṛte'kaj na bhaviṣyatītyāśaṅkya samudāyo hi viśeṣe vartate, pūrvapadantu sarvārthatāyā anapetamevetyuktam| tadetatsarvaṃ prāmādikameva| tathā hi-- jahatsvārthāyāṃ vṛtausamāsāvayavayorartha eva nāsti| ajahatsvārthāyāmapi svārthaviśiṣṭānyapadārthavṛtitvenopasarjanatvaṃ spaṣṭameva| evaṃ ca samāsāntaḥpātinoryuṣmadasmadoḥ sarvārthavācakatvenetyādi granthaḥ, samudāyo hityādinyāsagranthaśca pakṣadvaye'pi vyāhataḥ| kiṃ caivam atisarvāyetyatra smāyadeśo durvāraḥ, aṅgādhikāre tadantagrahaṇāt| tvaduktarītyā sarvaśabdasya sarvārthatānapāyāt| na hi pūrvapade sarvārthatā utarapade tu neti yuktikam| api caivaṃ gauṇatve sarvādīnāṃ sarvārthavācakatvaṃ nāstīti| svoktyā saha virodhaḥ syāditi yatkiṃcidetat| 
yadapi vyācakhyuḥ--tvaṃ pitā yasyetyādau vigrahavākye prāptā saṃjñā
niṣidhyate iti| tadapi na| vigrahavākyaṃ hi laukikamalaukikaṃ vā

kaiyaṭagranthavirodhāt,

niṣedhasāmarthyam| anāṅgasya tratasilādervyāvṛtyā

prau.ma.- jarāyā jarasanyatarasyām(pā.sū.0-0-0>
"jarāyā jarasanyatarasyām"|| vibhaktāviti| etacca "aṣṭana ā vibhaktau"
ityato'nuvartate| tena jarāyā idaṃ jāramityādau nātivyāptiriti
bhāvaḥ| tadanusāribhiriti| inātoḥ pūrvavipratiṣedha
ekasutropātatvāt syādeśo'pi grahītumucita iti teṣāmāśayaḥ|
ata eva--
vītajanmajarasaḥ paraṃ śuci brahmaṇaḥ padamupaitumiccchatām| 
iti bhāraviprayogamasamañjasaṃ matvā vītajanmajarasa iti paṭhanīyamiti
tairuktam| etacceti| bhāṣyakṛtā hi "ṭāṅsi" iti sūtre inātau
pratyākhyāya nādeśa madādeśaṃ ca vidhāya nādeśe pare etvaṃ
yogavibhāgena sādhitam| "bahuvacane jhalyet" "osi ca"| tata
"āṅ" āiṅ ci pare'ta etvaṃ syāt| rāmeṇa| tata "āpaḥ sambuddhau ca"
ekāraḥ syāt saṃbuddhau āṅ oisi ca| na caivamanenetyatra hali lopaḥ
syāditi vācyam| anādeśasya nakāre pare viśiṣya vidhānāt| tathā
hi, "a nāpi" nakāre āpi ca pare'n syādityarthaḥ| sūtre a iti
nāntasya napuṃsakanirdeaśaḥ| na ca ṅseradādeśe kṛte pararūpaṃ
syāditi vācyam| akāroccāraṇasāmarthyāddīrghasambhavāditi| tathā ca
"jarāyā jarasanyatarasyām" iti sūtre uktam --ṭhgonardīyastvāha-
atijarairiti bhavitavyaṃ sannipātaparibhāṣayā' iti| evaṃ ca
pūrvavipratiṣedhenetyādi sarvaṃ bhāṣyavirudhdamityarthaḥ|

prau.ma.- allopo'naḥ (pā.sū.0-0-0>
"allopo'naḥ"|| atra prāñcaḥ-- annantasya bhasyāṅgasyākārasya lopaḥ
syāditi| tathā sati ṭhtakṣṇā' ityādau takārākārasyāpi lopaḥ

prau.ma.-raṣābhyāṃ no ṇaḥ samānapade (0-0-0>
"raṣābhyām"|| pade itīyataiva sāmarthyādekapadatve labdhe'pi
samānagrahaṇaṃ yatsamānameva, akhaṇḍamiti yāvat| tatra yathā syātena
samāse na bhavati| rāmanāma| yūṣṇa iti| yūṣo maṇḍaḥ| 
ṭhmudgāmalakayūṣastu bhedī dīpanapācanaḥ'
iti darśanāt| iti pakṣe tviti| etena pañcanamīsamāsasya pākṣikatāṃ
dhvanayati| tathā hi, bhāṣye -bebhiditā, māthitikaḥ, apīpacanniti
prayojanatrayamuktam| taccānyathā sidhdam| "ekāca upadeśe" ityatra
vihitaviśeṣaṇādeva bebhiditetyatra iṇniṣedho na| mathitaṃ
paṇyamasyeti vigrahe ṭhaki ṭhasyekādeśe "yasyeti ca" ityallope
"isusuktāntātkaḥ" iti kādeśastu na bhaviṣyati| ṭhasyābhāvāt|na ca
sthānivadbhāvena ṭhatvamalvidhitvāt| "ṭhasyekaḥ"ityatra hi
sthānyādeśayorakāra uccāraṇārthaḥ| apīpacannityapi ṇilopasya
caṅntyakārayorekādeśasya ca sthānivadbhāvaṃ vinā'pi sidhdam|
vidhisāhacaryādabhyastādapi laṅ eva jherjusvidhānāt| na ca sicā
sāhacaryād luṅ eva kiṃ na syāditi vācyam|
pūrvasāhacaryātparasāhacaryasya balīyastvāditi dik|
pūrvatrāsiddhe neti| etacca nyāyasidhdamityavocāma| tripādyā
asidhdatvena tatratye kārye kartavye "acaḥ parasmin"
ityatideśasyāpravṛteḥ| tasyeti| tasya ṭhpūrvatrāsiddhe'ityasya | tathā ca
sthalatraye sthānivadbhāvo vaktavya ityarthaḥ| saṃyogādilope
cakryatra| latve nigālyate| ṇatve māṣavapanī| iha "yasyeti ca" iti
allopasya sthānivatvena nakārasya prātipadikāntatvābhāvād ṇatvaṃ
na| 
yatvāhuḥ--kathaṃcitsthānivatvābhāve'pi yūṣṇa ityādau "raṣābhyām"
ityasya nopayogaḥ, ṣṭuatvena sidhdeḥ| tathā ca
ṣagrahaṇamutarārthamiti ākare sthitam| tataśca
rabhasavādenaitadupanyastamiti| sa evāyaṃ rabhasābhāvaḥ| tathā hi,
utarārthatayā'pīha ṣagrahaṇaṃ sthitam| tataśca yūṣṇaḥ puṣṇāti ityādau
"raṣābhyām" iti ṇatvasya pravṛtiḥ kena vāryatām? ṣṭuatveneti
cenna| tasyāsiddhatvāt| na ca vacanāprāmāṇyātsiddhatvam|
puṣṭirityādau caratārthatvāt| tasya ṣayogeṇa ityaṃśo'caritārtha
iti cenna| tasya pṛthaganukteḥ| iha ṣātparasyatyuktiracaritārthetyasyāpi
tulyatvācca| ākare hi iha ṣagrahaṇaṃ vinā lakṣyāsiddhirnāsti
ityevābhipretaṃ, na tu puṣṇātītyādau "raṣābhyām"
ityasyāpravṛtiriti dik| 
prau.ma.- nalopaḥ prātipadikāntasya(0-0-0>
"nalopaḥ prātipadikāntasya"|| luptaṣaṣṭhīke iti| sautratvānna tu
ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣayoḥ pūrvapade, sāpekṣatvena samāsāsambhavāt
prātipadikasya padaviśeṣaṇatvācceti bhāvaḥ|tadantasyeti|
ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣaḥ| tasyānto yo nakārastasyetyarthaḥ| uktaṃ ca
haradatena--ṭhviśeṣyamantasyetyasya netyetadavibhaktikam, tathaiva
prātipadiketyetatpadaviśeṣaṇam' iti| yatu
prācoktaṃ--prātipadikāntasya padāntasya nasyeti| yacca
vyākhyātam--antasyetyubhābhyāṃ saṃbadhyate iti| tadgauravagrastam,
antasyetyasyāvṛtiprasaṅgād vyadhikaraṇaṣaṣṭhīdvayāpateśca| yadi tu
abhedenaiva sambandhena prātipadikeneva netyanenāpi padaṃ
viśeṣyate, tadā viśeṣaṇena tadantavidhau sautramantagrahaṇaṃ
śakyamakartum|

iti raghuḥ| tatra dakṣiṇaṃ doriti udyamyetyanena karmatayā sambadhyate|
doṣaṃ tasyeti|
"doṣaṃ tasya tathāvidhasya bhajataścāpasya gṛhṇan guṇam"|
iti śrīharṣaprayogaḥ| tatra doṣaṃ hastaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ ca bhajataścāpasya guṇaṃ
maurvīmatiśayaṃ ca gṛhṇan iti sambandhaḥ|
amaravyākhyātārastu--doṣaśabdaṃ striyāmapi svīkṛtya bhāgurimatena

iti dhanañjayanighaṇṭuamudājahrauḥ|

nāmni ḍaśabdo'sti| yathā mṛḍa iti| saḍasyāpatyaṃ sāḍiḥ|
|| iti hāntāḥ ||
prau.ma.-diva āṬt (pā.sū.-->
"diva āṬt"|| prātipadikasyeti| avyutpannasya, ṭhdiverḍiviḥ' iti
nyāsohitasūtreṇa vyutpannasya vā, na tu ṭhdivu krīḍādau' iti
dhātoḥ, niranubandhakagrahaṇe na sānubandhakasyeti paribhāṣayā|
tenākṣadyaurityādau na bhavati| āṬtastakāra uccāraṇārthaḥ|
sudyaubhyāmiti| ukārasya samprasāraṇatvāt "halaḥ" iti dīrghaḥ prāpta
ṭhut' ititaparatvasāmarthyānna bhavati| yatu prācoktam--ṭhdivo vā
ātvamami' iti; tadupekṣitam, munitrayānukternaṣphalatvācca| dyāmiti
rūpaṃ tu dyośabdasyaiveti bodhyam||
|| vāntāḥ ||
prau.ma.-ṣaṭcaturbhyaśca (pā.sū.-->
"ṣaṭcaturbhyaśca"|| bahuvacananirdeaśādarthaprādhānyaṃ
vivakṣitacam|arthāccāmaḥ paratvaṃ śabdadvārakam| tena tadantavidhau
satyapi paramacaturṇāmityadāveva bhavati, na tu bahuvrīhau|
tadetadvakṣyati--ṭhgauṇatve tu nuṭ neṣyate' ityādinā|dvitvamiti|
"aco rahābhyām" ityanena|
|| rāntāḥ||
kamalā lakṣmīḥ || lāntāḥ ||
prau.ma.-mo no dhātoḥ (pā.sū.-->
"mo no dhātoḥ"|| padānta iti| jhalīti tu yadyapi prācā'nuvartitaṃ,
tathā'pi kāśikādāvabhāvāt niṣphalatvāccopekṣyam|
prau.ma.- kimaḥ kaḥ (pā.sū.-->
"kimaḥ kaḥ"||nanu ima ityeva sūtryatām| tyadādīnāmimo'syād iti
vyākhyāsyate| kiṃśabdaśca dveḥ prāk paṭhiṣyate| ṭhnānarthaka' iti
niṣedhādārambhasāmarthyāccālo'ntyavidhirna bhaviṣyati, tat kiṃ
kādeśenetyāśaṅkyāha--akacsahitasyāpīti| ṭeratve kaka iti
syāt| "ku tuhoḥ" ityādyatha ca kima ityavaśyaṃ vaktavyamiti bhāvaḥ|
nanvevaṃ gaṇakāryatvābhāvādupasarjanatve'pi syāt ? maivam,
tyadādīnāmityanuvartya vacanavipariṇāmena tyadādeḥ kima iti
vyākhyānāt| anuvṛtisāmarthyācceha sūtre dviparyantānām iti
niyamasya tyāgāt|
utsarga iti| tena "tadoḥ saḥ sau" iti sūtre'nantyayoriti kim ?
ṭhhe sa' iti bhāṣyaṃ ṭhbho acyuta bhavannacyuta' iti ca na viruddhyata iti
bhāvaḥ| nāstītyatra mūlaṃ tu pracuraprayogādarśanameva| yatvāhuḥ--
idamastu sannikṛṣṭaṃ samīpataravati caitado rūpam|
adamastu viprakṛṣṭaṃ taditi parokṣe vijānīyāt||1||
iti vacanādevaṃ vidhasyārthasyābhinukhīkaraṇānarhatvāditi
;tadāpātataḥ, sannikṛṣṭādiśabdānāmiva idamādīnāmapi
sambodhyasamarpakatve bādhakābhāvāt| viprakṛṣṭasyāpi būddhyā
sannidhāpitasya bahuśaḥ sambodhanadaśanācca| tathā ca bhaṭṭiḥ--
ṭhhāpitaḥ kvāsi he subhru bahvevaṃ vilalāpa saḥ' iti|
prau.ma.-anāpyakaḥ (pā.sū.-->

prau.ma.- inhanpūṣāryamṇāṃ śau (pā.sū.-->
"inhan"|| upadhāyā iti| upadhādīrghasyaivāyaṃ viyamaḥ| tena
ṭhvṛtrahāyate' ityatra "akṛtsārvadhātukayoḥ" iti dīrgho bhavatyeva|
tathā ṭhvṛtrahaṇī' ityatra "anunāsikasya kvijhaloḥ" iti yo dīrghaḥ
so'pi niyamena vyāvartyate, upadhādīrghamātrāpekṣayā
niyamavijñānāditi bhāvaḥ|

prau.ma.- maghavā bahulam(pā.sū.6-4->
"maghavā bahulam"||"arvaṇastrasāvanañaḥ" iti sūtrāt tṛ ityanuvartate|
tadapekṣayā ca maghaveti ṣaṣṭhaḷrthe prathamā| tadāha--maghavanśabdasyeti|
adhātoriti| adhātubhūtapūrvasyāpītyarthaḥ| añcatigrahaṇaṃ hi
niyamārtham--dhātoścedbhavati tarhyañcatereveti| evaṃ ca

nāntatvābhāvāddīrghatvaṃ na syādityāśaṅkyāha--iheti|
bahulagrahaṇāditi| kvacidanyadevetyarthakādityarthaḥ| nipātanāditi|
mahehakārasya ghakāraḥ avugāgamaḥ kanipratyayaścetyasya tritayasya
nipātanādityarthaḥ| pratyākhyātamiti| tathā ca "cchandasīvanipau"ityatra
kaiyaṭenoktam--"maghavā bahulam"ityetanna kartavyaṃ śvannukṣanniti
nipātanānmaghaśabdānmatupā ca bhāṣāyāmapi śabdadvayasya
sidhdatvāditi|
yatvatra vārtikakṛtoktam--
arvaṇastṛ maghonaśca na śiṣyaṃ chāndasaṃ hi tat|
matubvanyovidhānācca cchandasyubhayadarśanam||1|| iti,
yacca tadvyākhyānāvasare kaiyaṭena bhāṣāyāmasādhutetyuktam,
tadvanibantamaddhyodātamaghavan śabdaviṣayakam|cchandasyeva
vanibvidhānāt| ato na kaiyaṭasya pūrvā'paravirodha iti
dhvanayannāha--cchandasītyādi| antodātaṃ tviti| yadyapi śvannukṣannityatra
kaninantā ete ityujjvaladatādigranthamahimnā ādyaudātatvaṃ labhyate,
tathāpi "ukṣā samudro aruṇaḥ suparṇaḥ, pūṣā tveto nayatu,
agnimūrdhā, nirmajvānaṃ na parvaṇo jabhāra, tvamaryamā bhavati yat"

iti pratīkamupādāya maghaśabdasyākārasya "yasya" iti lopamāśaṅkya
ṭhnaitadasti, anyadeva hīdamavyutpannaṃ prātipadikam, tathā hi-
bhāṣāyāmapyasya prayogo dṛśyate' iti vadannyāsakāro'pi
maghavanśabdasyāntodātatve bhāṣāyāmapi sādhutve cā'nukūlaḥ| evaṃ
sthite matupamāśritya trādeśaṃ pratyācakṣāṇau bhāṣyavārtikakṛtau
maghavānityatra trādeśapakṣe'pi dīrghaṃ svīkuruta eva| phalabhede
pratyākhyānāsambhavāt| ato bahulagrahaṇānnā'sidhdatvamiti yaduktaṃ
tadbhāṣyādisaṃmatameva|

nāntā cetyuktam| tatpañcetyatra nalope
kṛte'pītyādisvagranthavirudhdam| sampratyanāntatve'pi upadeśe
nāntvamāsīditi kimasiddhatvena ? nanvayaṃ pūrvāparavirodhaḥ
kāśikāyāmapi tulya iti cet kiṃ tataḥ| "bādhe dṛḍhea'nyasāmyāt kiṃ
dṛḍhea'nyadapi bādhyatām" iti nyāyāt|
yatu vyācakhyuḥ-- upadeśakāle yau ṣakāranakārau tadantetyarthaḥ|
devadatasya gurukulamitivat samudāyena sambandhānnāsamarthasamāsa
iti; tadāpātataḥ| avayavena sambandhaṃ vyākhyāya samudāyena
sambandhādityukteḥ parasparavyāghātāt| kiṃ ca yatropasarjanapadārthaḥ
sasambandhikastatra dvayīvidhā| sāpekṣo'pi samasyata ityekā|
pradhānapadārthasyaiva kulādergurvādineva devadatādinā'pi sambandhaḥ|
sa ca guruṇā saha utpatigarbhaḥ| devadatena saha tu
tadīyagurūtpādyatvarūpo gurughaṭita iti dvitīyā vidhā| uktaṃ ca
hariṇā--
sambandhiśabdaḥ sāpekṣo nityaṃ sarvaḥ samasyate|
vākyavatsā vyapekṣā hi vṛtāvapi na hīyate|| iti||
samudāyena sambandho yeṣāṃ gurukulādinā|
saṃspṛśyāvayavāṃste tu yujyante tadvatā saha||iti ca||
atra upasarjanaṃ na sambandhikam| yena dvayī vidhā'vakāśamāsādayet|
nanvihāpyantaśabdaḥ sasambandhikārtho'stīti cet ? upadeśakāle yau
ṣakāranakārāviti vadadbhistadanvayasya tyaktatvāt| anyathā hi
upadeśakāle'ntau yasyeti vaktavyaṃ syāt| vastutastu tadapi na|
antaśabdo hi avayavini sākāṅkṣo na tu yatra kutracit| anyathā
ṛddhasya rājapuruṣa iti syāt| rājño'pi īśiturīśatavyaṃ prati
sākāṅkṣatvāt|
"rājā prabhau nṛpe candre yakṣe kṣatriyaśakrayoḥ" iti koṣāt| 
yadi tu ṛddhaṃpratyanākāṅkṣateti brūṣe tarhi samaṃ prakṛte'pi|
saṃnipāteti| tathā copadeśakāla iti vyākhyānaṃ niṣphalamapīti
bhāvaḥ| yatvāhuḥ--paribhāṣāyā anityatvālluk syāditi; tat
sāhasamātram| iṣṭasthale'pyapravṛtau paribhāṣāyā niṣphalatvāpateḥ|
kiñcopadeśa iti nirmūlamapi| yatu
vadanti--antagrahaṇasāmarthyādaupadeśikatvaṃ labhyate iti; tadapi na|

prau.ma.-nopadhāyāḥ (pā.sū.6-4-4>

prau.ma.-aṣṭana ā vibhaktau (pā.sū.7-->

ityutarasūtrādvalītyapakṛṣyate, tacca vibhakterviśeṣaṇamiti bhāvaḥ|
tenā'ṣṭānāmityatra nuṭaḥ paścādeva sannipātaparibhāṣāyā

paratvānnityatvācca ātve kṛte'nāntatve ṣaṭsaṃjñābhāvānnuṭ na

uktanyāyā'yogāditi bhāvaḥ|
prau.ma.-aṣṭābhya āṬś (pā.sū.-->

tatkathaṃ kṛtākāratetyata āha-aṣṭabhya iti| kṛtātvasyedamanukaraṇaṃ, na

asavanāmasthānavibhaktirudātā syāt| aṣṭābhiḥ| dīrghāt kim ?

seṭi" iti seḍgrahaṇājjñāpakāt pūrvasmādvadhau

prau.ma.- ReĀ prathamayoram (pā.sū.-->
"ReĀ prathamayoram" || ReĀ iti pṛthakpadaṃ luptaṣaṣṭhīkam| prathamāśabdaḥ
prathamādvitīyayoḥ samudāye gauṇa iti vyācaṣṭe--ReĀ
ityasyetyādinā| ReĀ suṭoram iti suvacam|
adhikaraṇatvavivakṣayeti| yadi tu śeṣe iti vibhaktiviśeṣaṇaṃ syāt
tarhi vyarthameva tat| ātvayatvābhyāṃ viśeṣavihitābhyāṃ svavaviṣaye

jñāpitatvāditi bhāvaḥ| 
yuvaṃ vastrāṇiti | śeṣe lopaḥ| yatu kāśikāyāmuktam--
pañcamyāśca caturthyāśca ṣaṣṭhīprathamayorapi|
yānyadvivacanānyatra śeṣe lopo vidhīyate||
iti, tadbhāṣāpekṣayā| cchandasi tu prathamādvivacanamapi śeṣe lopasya

kimarthamiti praśnaḥ|
aṅgakārye iti| etena ṭhaṅgavṛte punarvṛtāvavidhiḥ' iti paribhāṣā

pravṛtau-prāptāyāmavidhiriti hi tasyā arthaḥ| eṣā ca paribhāṣā
"jyādādīyasaḥ" "jñājanorjā" iti sūtradvaye'pi jñāpiteti bhāṣye
sthitam| anyathā hi jyādāditi akāraṃ na brūyāt| "bahorlopa"

dīrgho bhaviṣyatīti| na caivaṃ dvābhyāmityatra tyādādyatve kṛte "supi
ca" iti dīrgho na syāditi vācyam ?
dvayorekasyetyādinirdeaśenoktaparibhāṣāyā anityatvajñāpanāt|

iti sūtre bhāṣye'pi pariniṣṭhitasyetyeva pāṭhastarhi
vācanikatāmevāśritya pakṣāntaraparatayā yojyaḥ|
anityatvabalaphalitārthakathanaṃ tat, na tu jñāpyāṃśapraviṣṭamiti vāstu
