prau.ma.- ṭābṛci (pā.sū.4-1-9>
"ṭābṛci"|| pūrveṇa prāptasya ṅīpo'pavādaḥ| asiddhatvāditi|
kāryakālapakṣeṇedam| yadi tu yathoddeśaṃ ṣaṭsaṃjñā yadi vā
upadeśakāle ṣṇānteti vyākhyānaṃ tadā asiddhatvopavarṇamaṃ
nopayujyata ityāhuḥ|
vastutastu yathoddeśapakṣe'pi asiddhatvamupayuktameva
ekadeśavikṛtasyopasaṃkhyānaṃ hi sthānivatsūtre paṭhaḷta|
ādeśagrahaṇasāmarthyāt ṭhsarvesarvapadādeśā' ityāśritya
ānumāniko'pyādeśaḥ sthānivaditi vā vyākhyāyate| ubhayathā'pi
alvidhau na tatpravṛtiḥ| etacca ākare spaṣṭam|
bahuyajvānāviti| "na saṃyogāt' iti niṣedhānnāyamupadhālopī
tena "ana upadhālopinaḥ" iti na pravartate|
prau.ma.- ḍābubhābhyāmanyatarasyām (pā.sū.4-1-13>
"ḍābubhābhyām"|| nanu niṣedhaḍāporvacanasāmarthyāt paryāyaḥ
siddhaḥ, satyam| "anyatarasyām" iti yogo vibhajyate| tatra "manaḥ"
iti nivṛtam| ano bahuvrīherḍāb vāsyāt| ṅīpā saha
vikalpalābhārthamidam| "ana upadhālopina" iti sūtrantu niyamārtham|
ano yo'sau vikalpaḥ sa upadhālopina eveti| tathā ca suparvetyādau
anupadhālopini niṣedhārthamidam| bahurājñītyādau tu "ṛnnebhya"
ityeva ṅīp|tena bahudhīvarīti siddhyati| "ṛnnebhya" iti hi
ṅīpamanūdya tatsanniyogena vidhīyamāno rephaḥ| prakaraṇāntarasthena
ṅīpi kṛte na siddhyet| nanu "vano ra ca" iti sūtrameva ṅībrau
vidhatāmiti cenna, ubhayavidhau gauravāt| kiñcaivamapi ṅīpi
niṣiddhe ro'pi durlabhaḥ, sanniyogaśiṣṭatvāt| bhāṣye tu
sūtrabhaṅgena samāhitam| tadyathā-- "ano bahuvrīheḥ"
ityasyānantaramupadhālopino veti sūtramastu| "ana upadhā"
ityādipradeśāntarasthaṃ sūtraṃ, ḍāpsūtre "anyatarasyāṃ" grahaṇaṃ ca māstu
iti|
prau.ma.- ana upadhālopino'nyatarasyām (pā.sū.4-1-28>
"ana upadhā " || "bahuvrīherūdhaso ṅīṣ" ityato bahuvrīhigrahaṇaṃ
"saṃkhyāvyayāderṅīp" ityato ṅīp cānuvartate ityāśayenāha--
annantādityādi| niyamārthamidamityuktam| na caivaṃ pūrveṇa
vikalpasiddhau ihānyatarasyāṃ grahaṇaṃ vyarthamiti vācyam| asati hi
tasmin nityārtho vidhirevāyaṃ syānna tu niyamaḥ|
prau.ma.- pratyayasthātkāt pūrvasyāta idāpyasupaḥ (pā.sū.-->
"pratyayasthātkāt pūrvasya"|| kādityakāra uccāraṇārthaḥ| vyañjanamātraṃ
vivakṣitam| viśiṣṭavivakṣāyāṃ tu etikāścarantītyādau akaci na
syādityāśayenāha--kakārāditi| varṇanirdeaśe hi kārapratyayo
vihitaḥ| kvacitsamudāyāt prayogastu "uccaistarāṃ vā vaṣaṭkāraḥ"
iti nirdeaśarūpayatnasādhya iti bhāvaḥ| āpi para iti| etacca akārasya
viśeṣaṇam| tatra sāmarthyāt kakārā'kārābhyāṃ vyavāye'pi bhavati|
na tu rathakaṭaḷetyādau tricaturādivyavāye| ata eva sarvikādau
ādyā'kārasya na bhavati| tadetaduktam--akārasyekāraḥ syādāpi para
iti| kakāraviśeṣaṇatve tu vivakṣite āpparātkakārāt pūrvasyāta
ityavakṣyat| 
yatu prāñcaḥ--āpīti kakāraviśeṣaṇaṃ sarviketyādau tu ekādeśasya
sthānivadbhāvādakāreṇa vyavadhāne'pi vacanasāmarthyādbhaviṣyatīti;
tanna, rathakaṭaḷadāvatiprasaṅgāt, vyavahitasyāpi paratvānapāyāt|
na ca nirdiṣṭaparibhāṣayā nistāraḥ, anuvāde
paribhāṣāṇāmanupasthiteḥ| anūdyamānasya viśeṣaṇeṣviti hi tadarthaḥ|
atra ca "udīcāmāta" iti sūtre sthānegrahaṇaṃ liṅgam| phalaṃ tu
vṛddhasaṃjñāyāmikparibhāṣānupasthānāt śālīyadisiddhiḥ|
yadapyāhuḥ--āpīti vidhīyamāsyeto viśeṣaṇamiti; tatsthavīyaḥ|
āpītyasyā'ṅgaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ tu nā''śaṅkyameva, rathakaṭaḷa
putrakāmyetyatrātiprasaṅgāt| śaketi| pacādyac| sthagrahaṇaṃ
vispaṣṭārtham| kakāramātrasya pratyayasyābhāvādeva tātsthyalābhāt|
pūrvasya kim| sarviketyādau ṭābekādeśaṃ bādhitvā kātparasya mā
bhūt "tasmādityutarasya" iti paribhāṣaṇāt| yatu -
ṭhvārṇādāṅgam' iti paribhāṣayā api niṣphalatvāt| vastutastu
"pratyayastheki" iti saptamīnirdeaśa evocitaḥ| pūrvagrahaṇaṃ ca
sutyajam| ata iti taparaḥ kim| "kṛdādhārārcikalibhyaḥ kaḥ"| dhākā|
rākā| māmiketi| "kevalamāmaka" ityādinā sājñāccchandasereveti
niyamānna ṅīp| tyaktyaporiti| "udīcāmātaḥ sthāna" iti
vikalpasyāpavādaḥ| dākṣiṇātyiketi| dakṣiṇasyāmadūre dakṣiṇā
ājantaḥ| dakṣiṇā bhaveti vigrahaḥ| yatu --dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi bhaveti
prācā vigṛhītam; tanna, tyagvidhāvavyayasāhacaryādājantasyaiva
dakṣiṇāśabdasya grahaṇāt| anyathā--ṭhsarvanāmno vṛtimātre' iti
puṃvadbhāvāpateśca| yatu-samase eva puṃvaditi tanneti vakṣyate|
tyaki "kiti ca" ityādivṛddhau ṭāp, tato'jñātādyarthe kaḥ| "ke'ṇaḥ"
iti hrasvaṣṭāp| ihatyiketi| "avyayātyap"|
prau.ma.- na yāsayoḥ (pā.sū.-->
"na yāsayoḥ" || atra nyāsakāraḥ| yā ityetasya "udīcāmātaḥ sthāne"
iti vikalpe prāpte niṣedho'yamiti; tanna, ṭhātaḥ sthāne'
ityaṃśasyehābhāvāt| yā seti
yatadorupalakṣaṇamityāśayenāha--yatadoriti| svarūpasyāvivakṣāyāṃ
phalamudāharati- yakāṃ, takāmiti| yāsayorityapahāya
yatadorityeva vaktumucitam| upatyaketi| "upādhibhyāṃ
tyakannāsannārūḍhayoḥ" iti| ṭhkṣipakādīnāṃ ca'| netyanuṣajyate|
kṣipaketi| kṣiperigupadhalakṣaṇaḥ kaḥ| tato'jñātādau kaḥ| caṭa bhedane|
pacādyac| tataḥ kaḥ| tāraketi| tareṇvul| jyotirnakṣatraṃ,
netrakanīnikā ca| varṇakā- prāvāpaviśeṣaḥ| tantūnāṃ
vikārastāntavam| "orañ"| anyatra varṇiketi| granthaviśeṣasya vyākhyā
stotrakartrī ca| vartayatīti vartakā-śakunau itvaṃ vā| anyatra
nityameva| pitaraśca tāḥ devatāśca pitṛdevatāstadarthaṃ karma
pitṛdevatyam| "devatāntātādarthye yat" iti yat| aśnanti
brāhmaṇā yasyāṃ sā aṣṭakā|ṭhiṣyaśibhyāṃ takan'| aṣṭikānyeti| aṣṭau
parimāṇamasyāḥ "saṃkhyāyā atiśadantāyāḥ kan"| 
arthagataṃ strītvamākāre'ddhyāropya "yakapūrvāyāḥ" iti
strīliṅganirdeaśaḥ| tena strīpratyayo labhyata
ityāśayenāha--strīpratyayākārasyeti| sāṅkāśyiketi| saṅkāśena
nirvṛtaṃ sāṅkāśyam "vuñcchaṇ" ādisūtreṇa saṅkāśādibhyo ṇyaḥ|
tato bhavārthe "dhanvayopadhād vuñ" akādeśaḥ| sa ca sāṅkāśyabhavāṃ
striyamāheti tadakārasyāpi itvaṃ vikalpyeta; tasmādāta ityuktam|
nanu yathāyamakāro nākārasthānika evaṃ strīpratyayasthāniko'pi neti
dvyaṅgavikalametat| atrāhuḥ-- sati hyāta ityetasmin
strīliṅganirdeaśabalāt strīvācaka ākāraḥ strīpratyaya eveti labhyate|
asati tu tasmin strīvācakasya yo'kāra ityetāvallabhyeta tataḥ
sāṅkāśyiketyatrāpi prasajyeteti|
atredaṃ vaktavyama| yakapūrvāyāḥ sthāne, iti sthāneśabde jāgarūke
kathamavayavaṣaṣ
ṭhītvaṃ gamyatām| na ca vuñaḥ sthāne'yamiti vācyam, aketi
samudāyasya tathātve'pi akārarūpāvyavasyā'tathātvāt| vaño
yakapūrvatvābhāvācc| na hi tasyāmavasthāyāṃ "yasya" iti lopo'pi
labhyate| kiṃ ca pratipadoktasya strīpratyayasya lābhe bhavārthe vihitasya
vuñādeḥ prasaṅga eva nāsti| tasmādāta iti spaṣṭārthameva| aśviketi|
kaumārāstu bhastrādivākye'śvaśabdamapi paṭhanto'syāpi
itvavikalpamiccchanti| śubhamiti mānto nipātaḥ| suṣṭhua nayo yasyāḥ
sā sunayā| tataḥ kaḥ|
prau.ma.- bhastraiṣājājñādvāsvānañpūrvāṇāmapi (pā.sū.-->
"bhastraiṣā" || tadanteti| āṅgatvāditi bhāvaḥ| evaṃ ceti| uktaṃ ca
haradatena--
atrātaḥ sthāna ityetatsvaśabdasya viśeṣaṇam|
sambhavavyabhicārau hi tatra staḥ kākacoḥ satoḥ||
dvaiṣayoḥ sambhavo nāsti, nānyatra vyabhicāritā|
sarvanāmnaḥ svaśabdasya tena nā'yaṃ vidhirbhavet|| iti||
iha svaśabdasyetyasyā'nantaramata iti śeṣo bodhyaḥ| etacca iha
mūle'pi dhvanitam| evaṃca haṃsaṃ tanau sannihitaṃ carantaṃ
munermanovṛtimiva svikāyām|
iti śrīharṣaśloke "bhastraiṣā" iti vaikalpikamitvamiti vyācakṣāṇā
upekṣyā iti bhāvaḥ| ātmīyāyāmiti| ātmajñātidhaneṣu tu strītvameva
nāstīti bhāvaḥ| yatu-- jñātinidhanayoḥ svakā, svikā, iti
rūpadvayamākare sthitamityāhuḥ; taccintyam|
tādṛśasyā''karasyā'prasiddheḥ, liṅgānuśāsanavirodhācca| yatu-
svakā, svikā, iti kāśikāyāmudāhṛtaṃ; tatsaṃjñāviṣayakaṃ, na tu
jñātiviṣayakam| yadapi ṭhsvārthikāḥ kvacitprakṛtito
liṅgavacanānyativartante' iti parijahrauḥ| tadapi cintyam,uktarītyā
kapratyayāntāṭṭāpsambhave'pi prakṛterastrīliṅgatayā ṭhātaḥ sthāne'
ityaṃśasya durupapādatvāt| pratyudāharaṇānyuktvā udāharaṇanyāha-
nirbhastriketyādi| niṣkrāntā bhastrāyā nirbhastrā| upasarjanahrasvaḥ,
ṭāp| tato'jñātādau kaḥ| kṛtaṣatveti| sūtre vikṛtanirdeaśo
vivakṣitaviṣayo na tu "na yāsayoḥ" itivadupalakṣaṇamiti bhāvaḥ|
strīliṅganirdeaśādajādayaḥ strīliṅgā eva gṛhyante| teneha na,
śubhro'jo'syāḥ śubhrājikā| jānātīti jñaḥ| priyo jño'syāḥ
priyajñikā iti|
prau.ma.- abhāṣitapuṃskācca (pā.sū.-->
"abhāṣitapuṃskācca"||vihitaviśeṣaṇatayā vyākhyāyāḥ
phalamāha--bahuvrīheriti|
nityamiti| asya vikalpasyāpravṛteḥ "pratyayasthāt" ityutsarga eva
pravartata ityarthaḥ| paraviśeṣaṇatve tvayaṃ vikalpaḥ syādeveti bhāvaḥ|
bahuvrīherityupasarjanopalakṣaṇam| tenā'tikhaṭvikā
niḥkhaṭviketyādāvapi nityameva| śaiṣike kapīti|

abhivyāpyeti| ata eva bahuyuvā śāletyatra "yūnastiḥ"
upasarjanatvānneti prāñca āhuḥ|
prau.ma.- ṭiḍḍhāṇañdvayasajdaghnañmātractayapṭhakṭhañkhañkvarapaḥ
(pā.sū.4-1->
"ṭiḍḍhā"|| iha vṛtikṛtā sūtre khyun prakṣiptaḥ| āṬtsīti| utse
bhavā, "utsāgadibhyo'ñ"| na ca "śārṅgaravādyaña" iti ṅīnā
siddhamidamiti vācyam, tatra jāteḥ ityanuvartate| anyathā
puṃyoge'pi syāt| etena iha añgrahaṇaṃ spaṣṭārthamiti prāco
granthaḥ pratyuktaḥ| ihānekaṃ vākyaṃ, tatra anupasarjanamaṇ
yo'kārastadantāditi vyakhyānānneha--āpiśalamadhīte āpiśalā
brāhmaṇīti| atra hi proktārthe'ṇ upasarjanam,adhyetraṇ tu luptaḥ,
varṇaprādhānyānna pratyayalakṣaṇam, ataṣṭābeva bhavati| tācchīlike iti|
ayaṃ bhavaḥ-- "śīlam" "cchatrādibhyo ṇaḥ" iti yo ṇastasmin aṇkāryaṃ
bhavati , "kārmastācchīlye" iti jñāvakāt| anena hi "an" iti aṇi
vihitaṃ prakṛtibhāvaṃ bādhituṃ ṭilopo nipātyate| caurīti|
curā śīlamasyāḥ|
prau.ma.- halastaddhitasya (pā.sū.-->

yaño'kāralope kṛte yakārasya īkāraparatvamastyeva
kimupadhāgrahaṇānuvṛtyā ? na cāllopasya sthānivadbhāvaḥ, yalope
tanniṣedhāt; maivam, yalope kārye
allopasyā''bhīyatvenā'siddhatvāt| anapatyeti| yadyapi ṭhāpatyagrahaṇaṃ
kartavyam' iti vārtikantathā'pi apatyādhikāravihitaparaṃ tat|

prau.ma.- prācāṃ ṣpha taddhitaḥ (pā.sū. 4-1->

prau.ma.- kauravyamāṇḍūakābhyāṃ ca (pā.sū.4-1->

ityaṇ|

prau.ma.- kāṇḍāntātkṣetre (pā.sū.4-1->
"kāṇḍāntāt kṣetre"|| ṣoḍaśahastapramāṇo daṇḍaḥ kāṇḍam| yadyapi
viśeṣaṇena tadantavidhiḥ siddhastathā'pi antagrahaṇe asati kṣetre
ityetat śrutatvātkāṇḍasyaiva viśeṣaṇaṃ syānna tadantasya| tataścehāpi
syāt| dvābhyāṃ kāṇḍābhyāṃ -- kāṇḍapramitābhyāṃ kṣetrābhyāṃ krītā
dvikāṇḍī vaḍaveti| iha tu na syāt--dvikāṇḍā kṣetrabhaktiriti|
ato'ntagrahaṇam| mātraca iti| yatu prācoktaṃ dvayasaco lugiti; tanna,
prathamaśca dvitīyaśca ūrdhvamāne matau mama|
iti siddhāntāt| dvikāṇḍī rajjuriti| nanviha parimāṇāntāditi
pūrveṇa niṣedhaḥ syāt, kāṇḍaśabdasyā''yāmaparatayā
aparimāṇārthatvāt| paritaḥ-sarvataḥ ārohataḥ pariṇāhataśca yena
mīyate taddhi parimāṇamāḍhakakuḍavādi| na tu
yathākathañcitparicchedakamātram| yadāhuḥ-
ūrdhvamānaṃ kilonmānaṃ parimāṇaṃ tu sarvataḥ|
āyāmastu pramāṇaṃ syāt saṃkhyā bāhyā tu sarvataḥ||iti||
ata eva dvihastā bhitirityatra ṅīb na| atrāhuḥ-niyamārthamidam|
kṣetre eva niṣedho yathā syāditi| ato dvikāṇḍī rajjurityatra
ṅībeva sūtrasya phalamiti|
prau.ma.- puruṣātpramāṇe'nyatarasyām (pā.sū.-->
"puruṣātpramāṇe"|| yadyapi puruṣaśabdo jātivacanastathāpi dvau
puruṣau pramāṇamasyā iti vākye pramāṇe vartata eva,
pramāṇaśabdasāmānādhikaraṇyāt| vṛtau tu tatsvabhāvādeva| tathā ca
śulbasūtram--"pañcāratniḥ puruṣaḥ" iti| dvipuruṣīti| atra
nyāsakāraḥ ṭhpramāṇe lo dvigo nityam' iti dvayasaco lugityāha ;
tanna, puruṣaśabdasya pramāṇavācitvābhāvena ṭhpramāṇe lo' iti
ślokavārtikasya tatrāpravṛteḥ| ye hi śamādivat pramāṇatvena
prasiddhāstatraiva tatpravartate na tu puruṣaśabde'pi| anyathā
puruṣadvayasamityatrāpi luk syāt| spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ
"puruṣahastibhyāmaṇ ca" iti sūtre padamañjaryām| na caivaṃ
dvayasajdaghnacāvapi nāsmātsyātāmiti vācyam ? ṭhūrdhvamāne matau
mama' iti vacanāt| evaṃ "puruṣātpramāṇe" ityasyāpi sāmarthyādeva
pravṛtiḥ| yatu tatraiva sūtre ṭhdvigornityaṃ luk' iti
vṛtigranthamupādāya haradatenoktaṃ ṭhtasmādaprāpta evātra lugvidhīyate'
iti; tadapi cintyama, munitrayānuktasya vidhānoktervyāhatatvāt|
yadapi prācacīnaprakriyaṃ vyācakṣāṇairuktaṃ "puruṣahastibhyām" ityatra
paṭhaḷmānena vārtikāntareṇa lugiti ;tadapi rabhasoktimātramÂ,
udāhṛtasūtrasya bhāṣyavārtikakārābhyāmaspṛṣṭatvādityāstāṃ tāvat|
vastutastu "puruṣātpramāṇe" iti sūtre dvagostaddhitaluki
ityanuvartate| tatsāmarthyāllugapyākṣipyate| haradatoktirapi
kathaṃcidevaṃparatayaiva neyeti sahṛdayairākalanīyam| pramāṇe kim ?
dvābhyāṃ puruṣābhyāṃ krītā dvipuruṣā gauḥ| taddhitalukītyeva|
samāhāre pañcapuruṣī| 
prau.ma.-ūdhaso'naṅ (pā.sū.-->
"ūdhaso'naṅ"|| nādeṣenaiva sidhde'naṅkaraṇaṃ "dhanuṣaśca"
ityetadartham|
prau.ma.- dāmahāyanāntācca (pā.sū.4-1>
"dāma"|| bahuvrīhiviśeṣaṇatvādeva siddhe antagrahaṇaṃ spaṣṭārtham|
saṃkhyāderiti| svaritatvabalādekadeśa evānuvartata iti bhāvaḥ|
prau.ma.-
kevalamāmakabhāgadheyapāpāparasamānāryakṛtasumaṅgalabheṣajācca
(pā.sū.4-1->
"kevalamāmaka"|| ityādīti| addhyasyāṃ māmakī tanū|
mitrāvaruṇayorbhāgadheyī| aniṣevyāstanvaḥ santu pāpīḥ|
utāparibhyo maghavā vijigye| samānīva ākūtiḥ | āryakṛtī|
sumaṅgalī| sumaṅgalīriyaṃ vadhūḥ| śivā rudrasya bheṣajī|
pratyudāharaṇaṃ tviti| yatu prācā'ntarvatī pativatīti pratyudāhṛtaṃ,
tadayuktamiti bhāvaḥ| vyaste kathamiti cediti| yatu prācā
samaste'pyupacāra iti varṇitaṃ, tanmudheti bhāvaḥ|
prau.ma.- pūtakratorai ca (pā.sū.4-1->
"pūtakratorai ca"|| aikāra ādeśo na tu pratyayaḥ, utarasūtre udāta
utyukteḥ| na tviti| anyathā-- vṛṣākapiśabdo ṭhlaghāvante dvayośca
bahvaṣo guruḥ' iti madhyodāta udātatvaṃ prayojayati| agnyādiṣu
triṣu "sthāne'ntaratama" ityeva sidÂdhamiti prācāṃ granthā
virudhyeranniti bhāvaḥ|
udātaikāra iti| manuśabdo hyādyaudātaḥ ṭhśṛÃsvṛsnihi' ityādau
manerupratyayavidhāyake sūtre ṭhdhānye nit' ityadhikārāt|
samāsanirdeaśanaitaddarśayati--udāta ityanuvartamānaṃ
sambandhānuvṛteraikāreṇaiva sambadhyate na tvaukareṇāpīti| tataśca
yaduktaṃ prācā aikāraukārāvudātau sta iti, tadupekṣyaṃ
vṛtipadamañjaryādivirodhāditi bhāvaḥ| manāyīti| madhyodātam|
itaraddvayamādyaudātam|
etā-karburā| varṇānāmiti| etaḥ, śoṇaḥ, śitiḥ, pṛśniḥ, pṛṣat
ityudāharaṇāni| tryeṇyeti| atra utarapadasyopasarjanatve'pi samastasya
anupasarjanatvamiti bhāvaḥ| saṃjñātvāṇṇatvaṃ chāndasatvādvā| knameke
iti| atra cchandasīti bhāṣyokterloke ṭhgato gaṇastūrṇamasiknikānām'
ityādiprayogāḥ prāmādikā iti prāñcaḥ| vastutastu cchandasyeke
ityanvayāt bhāṣāyāmapi iti bhāṣyābhipretam| anyathā ekagrahaṇaṃ na
kuryāt| na hi cchandasi ṭhpaliknīridyauvataya' ityādiprayogeṣu
kaścidvapratipadyate| viśuddhavācīti| daip śodhane iti smaraṇāt|
ṭhavadātaḥ sito gaura' iti kośe tu viśuddhatvasādharmyāt prayoga
upanibaddha iti bhāvaḥ|
laghāviti| ante ekasmin laghau, dvayośca laghvoḥ parato bahvaṣo-
bahvackasya gururudātaḥ syāt| aṣ iti acaḥ prācāṃ saṃjñā| kṛṣṇeti|
ṭhkṛṣeśca' iti napratyaye ṭhkapeśca' iti sautrāddhātorauṇādike ilaci
ca kṛṣṇakapilāvantodātau|
prau.ma.-ṣidgaurādibhyaśca (pā.sū.4-1->

prau.ma.- bahvādibhyaśca (pā.sū.4-1->
"bahvadibhyaśca"|| bahuśabdasya guṇavācitvāt pūrveṇa siddhe utarārthaṃ
grahaṇamityāhuḥ| vastutastu bahuśabdo "nityaṃ cchandasi" ityutarasūtraṃ
ceti dvayamapi vyarthameva, sarvavikalpānāṃ cchandasi vyavasthitatvāt|
puṃyogāditi| tathā ca gauṇī vṛtiriti bhāvaḥ| yadā tu
mukhyārthāt "tasyedam" ityaṇ tadā ṭhgopī'ityeva bhavati|
puṃyogaśceha dānpatyabhāva eva gṛhyata ityeke| vastutastu saṅkoce
mānābhāvājjanyajanakabhāvo'pi gṛhyate| tathā ca bhaṭṭiḥ--
kausalyayā'sāvi sukhena rāmaḥ prākkekayīto bharatastato'bhūt||
iti| kekayasya duhitā kekayī| "janapadaśabdāt" ityapatyapratyaye añi
tu kaikeyīti syāt| na ca tasya "ataśca" iti luk, "na
prācyabhargādi" iti niṣedhāt| tathā ca kālidāsaḥ- "kaikeyi kāmāḥ
phalitāstava" iti| etena devakī vyākhyātā|

"pūrvapadātsaṃjñāyām" iti ṇatvam|

tena bahviti| ayaṃ bhāvaḥ-- "bahornañvadutarapadabhūmni"
"nañsubhyām"
"jātikālasukhādibhyo'nācchādanātktokṛtamitapratipannāḥ" iti
hyantodātatāvidhāyakāni| tatra

udāto na tu nitya iti bhāvaḥ|
sumukhā śāleti| evaṃ ca phalamukhī kāraṇamukhī
cāvanasthetyādiprayogāḥ prāmādikā iti bodhyam|
uraḥ kroḍeti| strīliṅgo'yamiti haradatādayaḥ|
tatropasarjanahrasvatve kṛte'dantatvād ṅīṣaḥ prāptiḥ| amarastu-- ṭhna

tarhi prāggulkādāvapi prasajyeta| tatparihārāya pūrvoktaparyudāsasya
prasajyapratiṣedhānāṃ vā'nuvṛtau gauravamiti bhāvaḥ|
vṛṣalīti| ekasyāṃ vyaktau vṛṣalatve kathite tadapatyatatsahodarādau

aṇṇantalakṣaṇaṃ ṅīpaṃ paratvādayaṃ bādhate| evaṃ cāpatyādhikāre

upekṣyā iti bhāvaḥ| brāhnaṇītyatreti| evaṃ ca prācī ṅīṣudāharaṇaṃ
prāmādikamiti bhāvaḥ|
prau.ma. ūRutaḥ (pā.sū.-->
kurūriti| liṅgaviśiṣṭaparibhāṣayā prātipadikatvātsvādayaḥ| prācā
tu ṭhantādivacca' iti sūtramupanyasyā''divadbhāvatsvādaya ityuktam|
atredaṃ vaktavyam- paraṃ pratyādivadbhāvo vidhīyate| tathā
cehā'pratyaya iti niṣedha eva syānna tu svādayaḥ| yatu
vyācakhyuḥ--ādiḥ- ūṅpekṣayā pūrvaḥ, prātipadikaṃ, tadvadbhāvāt
tadavayavatvena grahāt pūrvantavadbhāvāditi yāvaditi; tadapi
kliṣṭaṃ svaparagranthavirudhdaṃ ca| uktarītyā hi kāṇḍe kuḍaḷe
ityādāvapi ṭhekavacanamutsargataḥ' iti suḥ pravarteta| tadvāraṇāya
cā'pratyaya iti prasajyapratiṣedhābhyupagame prakṛte doṣaḥ|
śvaśrūrityatraikādeśā'bhāvena liṅgaviśiṣṭaparibhāṣāyā
āvaśyakatvācca| yatu- tatra "śvaśuraḥ śvaśrvā" iti nirdeaśātsvādaya iti;
tanna, nirdeaśasya śabdaparatvāt| "viparābhyāṃ jeḥ" "ReĀryaḥ" itivat|
śvaśurasyeti| puṃyogalakṣaṇe ṅīṣi prāpte ūṅ| tatsanniyogena ca
vidhīyamāno lopaḥ sannihitatvādantyasyaivā'kārasya, na tvādeḥ|
etacca vacanaṃ "śvaśuraḥ śvaśrvā"iti nirdeaśasiddhārthakathanaparam| ato'pi
prathamā'kārasya lopo na śaṅkyaḥ| 
upamānavācipūrvapadamiti| ṭhdhātrīkarābhyāṃ karabhopamoruḥ' ityatra tu
na, karabhaśabdasyopamānavācitve'pi pūrvapadatvābhāvāt|
śaphau khurāviti| yadyapi ṭhśaphaṃ klībe khuraḥ pumān' ityamarastathāpi
śaphaśabdasya puṃstvamapi bahusammatama, śaphāviva jarbhurāṇeti śrutau
"grāmyapaśaṣusaṅgheṣu" iti sūtrasya vyākhyāvasare
nyāsaharadatādigrantheṣu vācaspatyādinibandheṣu ca pracuraprayogāt,
"liṅgamaśiṣyaṃ lokāśrayatvālliṅgasya" iti bhāṣyācca, ṭhśapho
bhujo gudaṃ ghoṇā' iti gaṇaratnamahodadhau kroḍādigaṇe pāṭhācca,
ṭhśaphaḥ khure gavādīnāṃ mūle viṭapināmapi' iti hemacandrakośācca|
viśvakośe ca punnapuṃsakatoktā| vāmau sundarau ūrū yasyā iti
vigrahaḥ| iha sarvatra upamānābhāvādaprāpte vidhiḥ| kathaṃ tarhi
ṭhpīvaroru pibatīva barhiṇa' iti kumāraḥ ? pramāda evāyam|
saṃjñāpūrvakatvātsambuddhiguṇo netyeke| hitena saheti| ṭhsamo vā
hitatatayoḥ' iti vyutpāditaḥ sahitaśabdastu neha gṛhyate,
ekadeśavikṛtatayā saṃhitagrahaṇādeva siddheriti bhāvaḥ| 
año yokāra iti| añā akāraviśeṣaṇaṃ kim ? śūrasenī|
"janapadaśabdāt" ityaño "ataśca" iti luki jātilakṣaṇo ṅīṣ|
śārṅgarani#īti| śṛṅgaruśabdādapatyārthe aṇ| nṛnarayoriti|
nṛśabdād "ṛnnebhya" iti ṅīpi, naraśabdājjātilakṣaṇe ṅīṣi prāpte
vacanam| nanu naraśabde alo'ntyasya vṛddhiḥ syāt| yatvāhuḥ--
antyasya "yasyeti" lopenāpahārādanantyasyāpyakārasya
vṛddhirbhavatyāntaratamyāt| ṭhguṇe tvanyāyyakalpanā'
ityalontyaparibhāṣābādhasyaiva nyāyyatvāditi; tanna,
pratyayasanniyogaśiṣṭāyā vṛddherantaraṅgatvena paramapi "yasyeti"
lopaṃ bādhitvā pravṛtyaucityāt | kiṃ cāstu kathaṃcillopastathā'pi
rephasyaiva vṛddhiḥ syānna tvakārasya,
antaratamaparibhāṣāpekṣayā'lontyaparibhāṣāyāḥ paratvāt| api
cāntaratamaparibhāṣā'sadṛśamādeśaṃ vyāvartayati na tu sthāninaṃ
niyaccchati, tatkathaṃ rephasya sthānitvavyavṛtiḥ| kiṃ ca ṭhguṇe tvanyāyya'
iti nyāyopanyasanaṃ sthavīya eva, paribhāṣayoḥ parasparaṃ
guṇaguṇibhāvāyogāt|
atredaṃ tatvam| "nara" iti rāntaluptākārānukaraṇaṃ naraḥ aḥ naraḥ| nā
ca naraśca tayoriti vyākhyeyam| yadvā narasya aḥ naraḥ, katantavat
pararūpam| nā ca naraśca tayornṛnarayoḥ| narasya ca prathama eva akāro
gṛhyate na tu dvitīyaḥ praśleṣasāmarthyāt| adhikaṃ śabdakaustubhe
bodhyam| putraśabdo'tra paṭhaḷte| śailaputrī| ṭhputrīva harṣaṃ hṛdaye
tanoti' ṭhkurve tadurvīpatiputri sarvam' | na ca putraśabdaḥ kanyāyāṃ
nāstīti bhramitavyam,
ātmajastanayaḥ sūnuḥ sutaḥ putraḥ striyaṃ tvamī| 
āhurduhitaraṃ sarve, ityamarokteḥ|
ata eva "pratyayasthāt" itītvaprakaraṇe ṭhvā
sūtakāputrikāvṛndārakāṇām' iti vārtikaṃ tadbhāṣyādikaṃ ca
saṅgaccchate| yatvatra haradatenoktam- kevalaputraśabdaḥ striyaṃ na
vartate iti; tadbhāṣyādivirodhādupekṣyam,
itvaprakaraṇasthasvagranthavirodhācca|
idaṃ tu cintyam--"kāre satyāgadasya" iti sūtre

kimarthamiti| yatu vadanti svarārthamiti, tanna, ṭitvān ṅīpi kṛte

āmbaṣṭhaḷeti| "vṛddhetkosalāt" iti yaṅ| kārīṣagandhyeti|
karīṣasyeva gandho'sya karīṣagandhiḥ| "upamānācca" iti

ṣyaṅadeśaḥ| sa ca yadyapi striyāmeva vihitastathā'pi

mātraśabdasyeti| tasya cāvadhāraṇamarthaḥ| ṭhmātraṃ kārtsnye'vadhāraṇe'
ityamaraḥ|prātipadikārthādīnyeva
tanmātramityevakāreṇāsvapadavigrahaḥ| mayūravyaṃsakāditvātsamāsaḥ|
kṛṣṇa iti| yadyapi nīlarūpavatparo'yamaniyataliṅgastathā'pi
vāsudeve bhagavati niyataliṅga eveti bhāvaḥ| jñānamiti| bhāve
lyuṭ| droṇa iti| na ca prātipadikamātre prathamehā'stviti vācyam ?
tathā sati paricchedyaparicchedakabhāvasya saṃsargavidhayā
bhānāyogānnāmārthayorabhedasaṃsarga eveti vyutpateḥ| na
cāṣaṣṭhaḷrthabahuvrīhau vyabhicāraḥ, ekārthībhāvābhyupagamāt| nāpi
nipātārthe, teṣāṃ dyotakatā'bhyupagamāt| na ca siddhānte'pi
prakṛtipratyayārthayorabhedānvayo durlabhaḥ, anyatrādṛṣṭatvāditi
vācyam ? devatārthakataddhitādau dṛṣṭatvāditi bhāvaḥ| vacanaṃ
saṃkhyeti| tathaiva prācāṃ vyavahārāt|
iha sūtre'rthe liṅge ca prathametyetāvadeva suvacam| itaratsarvaṃ
tyaktuaṃ śakyam| tathā hi--arthe prathametyukte kasyārthe ityākāṅkṣāyāṃ
yasmātsvādividhistasyeti labhyata eva| svādayaśca
prātipadikālliṅgapratyayaviśiṣṭācca vidhīyante| tadarthatvañca tato
niyatopasthitikatvameva| evaṃ ca mṛḍānītyādau
puṃyogamahatvāderapi ṅyantānniyatabhānena prathamā
siddhyatītyaparamanukūlam| yathānyāse tu sā na syāt| yadi tu--
arthaśabdo vṛtiviṣayaparaḥ, vṛtistu śaktirlakṣaṇā ca, gauṇī tu
lakṣaṇāntarbhūtā pṛthagvāstu; trikaṃ prātipadikārthaḥ- tadā
liṅgetyapi māstu| lakṣyāṇāṃ vicitratve'pi na kṣatiḥ,
niyamāṃśatyāgāt| tathā ca arthe prathametyeva sāram| droṇo
vrīhirityapi droṇaśabdasya tatparimite upacārātsidhdam,
gaurvāhīka itivat| eko dvāvityādyapi ṭhna kevalā prakṛtiḥ
prayoktavyā' iti niṣedhādeva siddham, ananvitārthakaprayogāpekṣayā
anuvādakamātraprayogasya nyāyyatvāt| evaṃ mātragrahaṇamapi vyartham,
"sambodhane ca" iti jñāpakena karmādyādhikye prathamāyā apravṛteḥ|
yatu prācoktam--prātipadikārthaḥ sateti| yacca vyākhyānaṃ yo'rthaḥ
sannityeva kevalaṃ bhāsate na jātyādirūpe na
liṅgasaṃkhyākārakayogī, sa vivakṣitaḥ| yathāvyayārthaḥ|
yathā'vyayārtho hi kevalamastīti śakyate vaktuaṃ na punarīdṛśastādṛśa
itīti cet ? na, uccairnīcairityādiśabdānāṃ
sannityākārakapratītijanakatvasya sarvatantraviruddhatvāt,
astyādisāmānadhikaraṇyasyāpi asatvārthakeṣu durlabhatvāt, na
liṅgasaṃkhyākārakayogiti tvaduktavirodhācca
padāntaropasthāpitasya astisāmānādhikaraṇyamastīti cet ? na, tasya
avyāvartakatvāt, na punarityādivākyaśeṣavirodhācca, kṛṣṇaḥ
śrīrityādāvavyāpteśca| na ca liṅgagrahaṇātatsiddhiḥ,
mātraśabdenā'dhikavyavacchedāt|
yadapi prācā vyākhyātaṃ-yatrārthāntārānapekṣayā śabdapravṛtiḥ sa sarvaḥ
prātipadikārthaḥ, śabdapravṛtinimitamiti yāvat| yathā
uccairnīcairiti| aliṅgasaṃkhyāvetau kevalaṃ jātyādi nirapekṣaṃ
svārthamāhaturiti; tadapi na, pravṛtinimitamiha prakārībhūya
bhāsate| tathā ca tanmātrabodho'sambhavī| yadi so'pīṣṭastarhi
ghaṭādiśabdebhyo'pi syāditi saṃkhyākārakādyananvaya eva syāt|
yadapi droṇaparimite vrīhyādau droṇaśabdaḥ
so'yamityabhisambandhātpravartate| tatra gauṇamukhyanyāyānna prāpnotīti
parimāṇagrahaṇamiti; tanna, padakāryeṣvayaṃ nyāyo na
prātipadikakāryeṣviti siddhāntāt, anyathā siṃho māṇavaka
ityādyasiddhiprasaṅagācca|
yadapyāhuḥ--droṇādayaḥ śabdā lauhaṃ dārumayaṃ vā parimāṇameva na
vyabhicaranti, parimeyaṃ tu vyabhicarantyeva| atastasya
prātipadikārthatvābhāvādaprāpteḥ parimāṇagrahaṇamiti; tadapi na,
gauṇalākṣaṇikeṣu ṭhsiṃho māṇavakaḥ' ṭhmañcāḥ krośanti'
ityādiṣvavyāptyāpateḥ| tatra viśeṣyasya māṇavakāderbhede'pi
siṃhasadṛśa ityartho gauṇyāṃ niyatopasthitika eveti yadi, tarhi
vrīhyāderbhede'pi droṇaparimito niyatopasthitika eveti tulyam|
prau.ma.-saṃbodhane ca (pā.sū.-->
"sambodhane ca"|| mātragrahaṇātsaṃbodhanādhikye na prāpnotītyārambhaḥ|
iha saṃbodhanaṃ pra kṛtyarthaṃ prati viśeṣyaṃ, kriyāṃ prati viśeṣaṇamiti
sidhdāntaḥ| uktaṃ ca --
sambodhanapadaṃ yacca tat kriyāyāṃ viśeṣaṇam|
vrajāni devadateti nighāto'tra tathā sati|| iti.
evaṃ ca ṭhrāma māṃ pāhi' iti vākyasya rāmasaṃbandhisaṃbodhanaviṣayo
matkarmakaṃ rakṣaṇārthaḥ||
iti prathamā ||
prau.ma.- karturīpsitatamaṃ karma (pā.sū.-->
"karturīpsitatamaṃ karma"|| karturiti "ktasya ca vartamāne" iti
kartari ṣaṣṭhī| āpnoteḥ sannantāt "matibuddhipūjārthebhyaśca" iti
vartamāne ktaḥ| matiricchā na tu buddhiḥ ,punarbuddhigrahaṇāt| evaṃ ca
kartrā āptumiṣyamāṇaṃ karmetyarthaḥ| kartā ca dhātūpātavyāpārāśrayaḥ|
sa ca kenā''ptumiccchatītyākāṅkṣāyāṃ viśeṣaṇībhūtena
vyāpāreṇetyarthāllabhyate| tadetadāha--kriyayeti|
ktapratyayenopasthitaṃ vartamānatvaṃ neha vivakṣitam| tena ṭhkaṭṃa
kṛtavān' ṭhkaṭṃa kariṣyati' ityādisiddhyati| kārakamiti| etacca
"kārake" ityadhikārāllabhyate| tatra hi vyatyayena prathamārtheta saptamī|
pratisūtraṃ vākyaṃ bhitvā kārakasaṃjñā vidhīyate| tadyathā-apāye dhruvaṃ
kārakasaṃjñaṃ syāt| tataḥ "apādānam" uktaṃ kārakamapādānasaṃjñaṃ
syāt| punaḥ kārakaśabdānuvṛtisāmarthyāt viśeṣasaṃjñābhiḥ saha
samāveśaḥ| anvarthā ceyaṃ saṃjñā--karotīti kārakamiti|tena
kriyā'nanvayino na bhavati--brāhmaṇasya putraṃ panthānaṃ pṛccchatīti| iha
hi brāhmaṇaḥ putraviśeṣaṇaṃ na tu kriyānvayi|
yadvā kārakaśabdaḥ kriyāparaḥ, karoti kartṛkarmādivyapadeśāniti
vyutpateḥ| tathā ca apādānādisaṃjñāvidhau kriyayāmityasyopasthityā
kriyānvayināmeva tatatsaṃjñā vidhīyante| "kārakāddataśrutayoḥ"
ityādau tu kārakaśabdaḥ svaryate| tenaitadadhikāroktaṃ
kartrādiṣaṭkameva gṛhyate| asmin pakṣe vṛtāvapi kārakamityasya
kriyānvayītyarthaḥ| ādyapakṣe tu kārakasaṃjñā syāt| tacca kārakaṃ
karmasaṃjñaṃ syāditi vyākhyeyameva|
prau.ma.--anabhihite (pā.sū.2-3-1>
"anabhihite" || nanu bahupaṭuarityādau bahucā uktārthatvādyathā
kalpabādayo na, evaṃ kriyate kaṭaḥ ityādāvapi
tiṅadibhiruktārthatvād dvitīyā na bhaviṣyati, kiṃ ca kaṭṃa
karotītyādau sāvakāśā dvitīyā ṭhkṛtaḥ kaṭaḥ' ityādiṣu prathamayā
bādhiṣyate, niravakāśatvāt| na ca vṛkṣa ityādiravakāśaḥ, tatrāpi
pratīyamānāmastikriyāṃ prati kartṛtvena tṛtīyāprasaṅgāt| atha
nīlamidaṃ na tu raktamityādau viśeṣaṇāntaranivṛtitātparyake
astikriyāyā anāvaśyakatvāt prathamāyā avakāśaṃ brūṣe, tarhi
vipratiṣedhāt prathamāstu| evaṃ cānabhihitādhikāro vṛtheti cet? 
maivam, saṃkhyā vibhaktyartha iti pakṣe sūtrārambhāt| tathā
cā'nabhihite karmaṇi yadekatvaṃ tatra dvitīyaikavacanamamityarthaḥ|
sūtrānārambhe ca karmaṇi yadekatvaṃ tatra3
dvitīyaikavacanamam iti hi vākyārthaḥ| tathā sati ṭhkṛtaḥ kaṭaḥ'
ityādau ca ktena karmamātroktāvapi tadekatvasyānuktatayā am
durvāraḥ| paratvātprathametyapi na, ṭhkartavyaḥ kaṭaḥ' ityādau tato'pi
paratvena kṛdyogalakṣaṇaṣaṣṭhīprasaṅgāt| kārakaṃ vibhaktyartha iti pakṣe
sūtraṃ pratyākhyātamākare|
hariṃ bhajatīti| bhajanakriyayā harau prīterutpādāt prītiviśiṣṭatayā
kriyāvyāpyatvena hariḥ karma| tadviśiṣṭaṃ bhajanaṃ vākyārthaḥ| ṭhhariḥ
sevyate' ityatrāpyevameva, ṭhkriyāpradhānamākhyātam' iti siddhāntāt|
dhātūpasthitā kriyā tirṅthaṃ prati viśeṣyā na tu kṛdarthaṃ pratīva
viśeṣaṇamiti hi tasyārthaḥ|ata eva pācako vrajati itivat pacati
vrajatīti naikaṃ vākyam| pākakartṛkartṛkaṃ gamanaṃ hi pācaka
ityāderarthaḥ| ekakartṛkā pacikriyā gamikriyā ca pacativrajatyoḥ
pṛthagevārthaḥ| evaṃ ca prathamāntaviśeṣyako bodha iti
naiyāyikodghoṣo bhāṣyādyanusāribhirnādartavyaḥ|
yāsko'pyāha-ṭhbhāvapradhānamākhyātaṃ, satvapradhānāni nāmāni'| paśya
mṛgo dhāvatītyādau tu mṛgakartṛkaṃ gamanaṃ dṛśikriyāyāṃ karma,
pradhānaṃ dṛśikriyaiva| uktaṃ ca--
subantaṃ hi yathā'nekaṃ tiṅntasya viśeṣaṇam|
tathā tiṅntamapyāhustiṅntasya viśeṣaṇam|| iti|
evaṃ pacati bhavatītyasya pacikriyā bhavatītyarthaḥ| 
ayaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ--tiṅntopasthitāyāḥ kriyāyāḥ kriyāntaraṃ prati
viśeṣaṇatve'pi śabdādhyāhāravāde karmatvaṃ saṃsarga eva, na tu
prakāraḥ; apadārthatvāt| arthādhyāhāravāde tu prakāratve'pīṣṭāpatiḥ|
pacatītyādau dhātūpātaphalasyāpītthameva matabhedena vyavasthā| ata eva
tatsamānādhikaraṇe ṭhśobhanaṃ pacati' ityādau dvitīyaikacanotpatiḥ,
ṭhagniṣṭomena yajeta' ityādau tṛtīyotpatiprasaṅgaśca saṅgaccchate|
na ca dṛṣṭānte viśeṣyasamarpakāddārṣṭāntike viśeṣaṇasamarpakācca
karmatvādivacikāyāḥ subvibhakte- prayogo'stviti vācyam ?
dhātutiṅntayoraprātipadikatvāt| naiyāyikānāṃ tu syādeṣa
doṣaḥ| tathā hi dhāvantaṃ mṛgaṃ paśyetivat dhāvati mṛgamityapi
syāt| na cāprathamāsāmānādhikaraṇye śatṛśānacornityatvānnaivaṃ
prayoga iti vācyam? evamapi dvitīyānutpaterdurupapādatvāt|
nanutra vākyābheda eva, paśyetyatra tamiti karmādhyāhārāt| pacati
bhavatītyādi tu pāko bhavatītyādyarthe'niṣṭameveti cet? 
maivam, adhyāhāre gauravāt, tasya ca
munivacanavirodhenāprāmāṇikatvāt,
tvadīyānicÂchāmātrasyākiñcitkaratvāt| api ca
ṭhapakṣīddevadato'vehi' ityādau śatrādeḥ prasaṅga eva nāsti|
ekavākyatvaṃ ca bhāṣyavārtikādisakalasammatam| na ca tiṅntasthale
kriyāviśiṣṭakriyāntarabodhe jāyata eveti vācyam ? tathaiva
vyutpannānāṃ tādṛśabodhasya durvāratvāt| īdṛśe'rthe sādhutvaṃ
neṣṭamityapi na, sādhutvānuśāsanārthaṃ pravṛtaireva tasya svīkārāt,
dhātusambandhādhikaravyutpādite ṭhpurīmavaskanda' ityādau
tasyāvaśyakatvācca| nanu sidhdānte ākhyātavācyakriyāyā api
kartṛtvakarmatvasvīkāre tasyā asatvarūpatvaṃ bhajyeteti cet ? na,
liṅgasaṃkhyayoḥ kartṛkarmetarakārakasya cābhāvamātreṇa
tathātvopapateḥ, karmatvasya prakāratayā'nabhyupagamādvetyanyatra
vistaraḥ| abhihite tviti| nanvevaṃ ṭhpakvamodanaṃ bhuṅkte' ityatrāpi
dvitīyā nasyāditi cet ? maivam, iha hi pacibhujinirūpite dve
karmatvaśaktī, tatra pradhānakriyānirūpitāmanabhihitāṃ śaktimādāya
dvitīyotpateḥ| uktaṃ ca hariṇā--
pradhānetarayoryatra dravyasya kriyayoḥ pṛthak|
śaktirguṇāśrayā tatra pradhānamanurudhyate||
pradhānaviṣayā śaktiḥ pratyayonābhidhīyate|
yadā guṇe tadā tadvadanuktā'pi pratīyate|| iti|
ata eva ṭhpaktvaudano bhujyate' ityatra bhujipratyayenābhihite
prathamaiva, paceraprādhānyāt|
vastutastu ṭhpaktvā bhujyate' ityādau karmaṇaḥ pacibhujibhyāṃ
yugapacchābdo'nvayaḥ, kiṃ tvantareṇa śābdo'pareṇārthaḥ| tatra ca
prathamādvitīye vyavasthite eva| tadyathā--paktvā bhujyate odanaḥ| kiṃ
paktvā ? arthādodanam| yadā tu odanaṃ paktvā bhujyate iti
prayujyate tadā bhujyata ityatrārthādodana iti gamyate| evaṃ ca
vivakṣābhedena ṭhpūjayituṃ śakyate harirhariṃ vā' ityubhayaṃ sādhu|
prāptānanda iti| ānandakartṛkaprāptikarmībhūta ityarthaḥ| yaduktaṃ
prāyeṇeti, tasya phalamāha--kvaciditi| viṣavṛkṣa iti| yadu tu
vardhiccchidyoranyatareṇa saha karmatayā'nvayo vivakṣyate, tadā dvitīyā
syādeveti bodhyam|
tṛṇaṃ spṛśatīti| yathā spṛśyamānasya gaṅgāderīpsitasya kriyayā
yogastajjanyasaṃyogādiphalādhāratvāt tathaivānīpsitasya
tṛṇāderapīti bhāvaḥ| syādetat
dhātūpasthāpyaphalaśālikarmetyevā'stu, kimīpsitānīpsitayoḥ
pṛthakkathaneneti cet ? maivam, agnermāṇavakaṃ vārayatītyatra
"vāraṇārthānām" iti sūtreṇa māṇavakasyāpādānatvaṃ prāptaṃ
tadbādhanāya "karturīpsitatamam" iti vaktavyameva| evaṃ ca
dveṣyodāsīnayoḥ saṃgrahārthaṃ "tathā yuktam" ityasyāpyāvaśyakatvāt|
anīpsitagrahaṇaṃ tu spaṣṭārthameva na tvāvaśyakam|
idaṃ tvavadheyam| īpsitānīpsitayoḥ śābdabodha ekarūpa eva|
īpsānīpsāderadhiśīṅadivatsaṃjñāpravṛtāvupayoge'pi
dvitīyāvācyakoṭāvapraveśāt| ananyalabhyasyaiva śabdārthatvāt|
"karmaṇi dvitīyā" iti sūtre hi tatatsūtrairyāvatāṃ karmasaṃjñā
vihitā, te sarve tena rūpeṇopasthitāsteṣu ca vidhīyamānā
dvitīyā ādhāravācikā, lāghavāt, kriyāyā dhātunā kartuśca
devadatādiśabdairlābhāt, iṣṭatamatvādestu bhānābhāvāt|
prakṛtipratyayārthayoścābhedena iha saṃsargaḥ| tathā ca haryādhārikā yā
prītistadanukūlo vyāpāro vākyārthaḥ| yadyapi karmaṇi kartari
adhikaraṇe ca vihitānāṃ dvitīyādīnāmādhārārtha iti tulyam;
tathā'pi tannirūpakaṃ phalaṃ, vyāpāraḥ, svāśrayadvārā tadubhayaṃ ceti
phalūbhūte śābdabodhe vailakṣaṇyaṃ saṅgaccchate| na cādhārataiva
vācyeti mantavyam, tanniṣṭhadharmasya
vācyatāvacchedakatāpatyā'tigauravāt "karmaṇi dvitīyā"
ityādisūtrasvarasabhaṅgāpateśca| ata eva bhāṣyakāra āha--
supāṃ karmādayo'pyarthāḥ,saṃkhyā caiva tathā tiṅam| iti|
na ca tiṅe'pi dharmamātramarthaḥ, "kartari kṛt" "laḥ karmaṇi ca"
"tayorevakṛtyaktakhalarthāḥ" iti sūtrāṇāṃ vairūpyāpateḥ, pācako
devadataḥ, pakva odana ityādau sāmānādhikaraṇyānurodhena
dharmivācakatāsvīkārācca| tasmādiha ghaṭādiśabdānāmiva viśiṣṭameva
vācyam| ākṛtyadhikaraṇābhyupagame tu kṛtsvapi dharmamātraṃ,
sarvathā'pi naiyāyikamīmāṃsakādyabhyupetamardhajaratīyaṃ heyameva| na
hi tiṅkṛtorvailakṣaṇyaṃ sūpapādam|
prapañcitañcaitaccchabdakaustubhe'smābhirityalamiyatā| 
parigaṇanamiti| tena naṭasya śrṛṇoti ityādau nātiprasaṅga iti
bhāvaḥ| gāṃ dogdhīti| gosambandhi payaḥkarmakaṃ dohanamarthaḥ|
syādetat, "karturīpsitatamaṃ karma" "tathā yuktaṃ cānīpsitam" iti
sūtrābhyāṃ gatārthametat| tathā hi, tyajanopasarjanaṃ tyājanaṃ
duherarthaḥ, dāpanamaṅgīkaraṇaṃ ca yāce, vikledanaṃ nivartanaṃ ca
pacerityādi| evaṃ ca dhātūpātavyāpāraviṣayībhūtaṃ yat
tyajanadānāṅgīkārādi,tadāśrayatvaṃ gavādīnāṃ spaṣṭameva| ata eva
hi aṇau karturṇau karmatvasya nyāyasiddhatayā gatyādisūtraṃ
niyamārthamiti prācāṃ siddhāntaḥ| ata eva cākaḍārasūtre
"apādānamutarāṇi gāṃ dogdhi paya ityatra paratvātkarmasaṃjñā" iti
bhāṣyaṃ saṅgaccchate| pañcakaṃ prātipadikārtha iti pakṣe
tvavadhitvaphalāśrayatvayoryugapad vivakṣāyāṃ cedam, avadhibhūtā yā
gaustanniṣṭho yaḥ payaḥkṣaraṇānukūlo vyāpārastadviṣayiṇī
gopaniṣṭhā preraṇetyarthāt| tathā ca "akathitaṃ ca" iti sūtraṃ
vyarthamiti cet ?
atrocyate, karmaṇaḥ śeṣatvena vivakṣāyāṃ ṭhna māṣāṇāmaśnīyāt'
itivat ṣaṣṭhī prāptā, tāṃ bādhitumidaṃ sūtram| tathāceha
ṣaṣṭhīvatsambandhamātraṃ dvitīyārtha iti sthitam|
apādānatvamātravivakṣāyāṃ tu pañcamyeva| goḥ kṣīraviśeṣaṇatve tu
ṣaṣṭhī| tena goḥ payo dogdhītyapi prayogaḥ sādhureva|
evamagre'pyūhyam| yatu prācoktam-

prau.ma.-gatibuddhipratyavasānārthaśabdakarmākarmakāṇāmaṇikartā sa ṇau
(pā.sū.-->
"gatibuddhi"|| pratyavasānaṃ bhakṣaṇam| śabdakarmakāṇāmiti| śabdaḥ
karmakārakaṃ yeṣāṃ, teṣāmityarthaḥ| na tviha "kartari karmavyatihāre"

karmagrahaṇasāmarthyācca| anyathā

caitatsūtramiti prāñcaḥ| ṇijarthenāpyamānasya yadi bhavati tarhi

kartuḥ svadharmeṇa tṛtīyayetyarthaḥ| nanu ṇijarthaṃ prati karmatvaṃ

antaraṅgatvācca| svakārakaviśiṣṭā hi kriya ṇijarthena sambaddhyate

evopajīvyā'pi kartṛsaṃjñā| evaṃ ca--
paratvādantaraṅgatvādupajīvyatayā'pi ca| 
prayojyasyāstu kartṛtvaṃ; gatyādervidhitocitā || iti cet ?
astvevaṃ, lakṣyasya nirbādhatvāt| niyamasūtramidamiti granthānāṃ kā
gatiriti cet ? śṛṇu, ṇijarthasya śābdaṃ prādhānyaṃ puraskṛtya

ityāśayena granthānāmuktisambhavaḥ| arthaprādhānyapuraskāre tu

gatyādisūtreṇa karmatvāditi cet ? maivam, karmasaṃjñāyāṃ hi

dhātūpātavyāpārāśrayatvameva| tacca prayojakasannidhāne'pi

pradhānakarmaṇyākhyeye lādīnāhurdvikarmaṇām|
apradhāne duhādīnāṃ| ityuktyā ṭhṇyante kartuśca karmaṇaḥ' iti vadato
bhāṣyakṛta evatatra pramāṇatvāt| na hi lakṣaṇāṃ vinā ṇijantānāṃ
dvikarmakatā labhyate, yena ṭhṇyante kartuḥ' iti vacanaṃ sāvakāśaṃ
syāt| nanu svatantre saṅketitasya tatraiva lakṣaṇā kathamiti cet ?

setyavadhehi|
nīvahyaeriti| yadyapyanayoḥ prāpaṇamartho na datistathā'pi gatirapi

prayojyaḥ karmeti vaktavyamiti phalito'rthaḥ| sūta iti|
niyantā prājitā yantā sūtaḥ kṣatā ca sārathiḥ|

prau.ma.-abhiniviśaśca (pā.sū.-->

prau.ma.- lakṣaṇetthambhūtākhyānabhāgavīpsāsupratiparyanavaḥ (pā.sū.-->

varjanārthenāpetyanena sāhacaryāt|

prau.ma.-svatantraḥ kartā (pā.sū.-->

āha ca-

prau.ma.- sādhakatamaṃ karaṇam (pā.sū.-->
"sādhakatamaṃ karaṇam"|| prakṛṣṭeti| yadvyāpārādanantaraṃ
kriyāniṣpatistatprakṛṣṭam| uktaṃ ca--
kriyāyāḥ pariniṣpatiḥ yadvyāpārādanantaram|
vivakṣyate yadā yatra karaṇaṃ tatadā smṛtam|| iti|
vivakṣyate ityanena sthālyādīnāmapi vaivakṣikakaraṇatvamastīti
dhvanayati|
āha ca--
vastutastadanirdeaśyaṃ na hi vastu vyavasthitam|
sthālyā pacyata ityeṣā vivakṣā dṛśyate yataḥ|| iti|
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ- kārakatvaṃ tadvyāpyakartṛtvādiṣaṭkaṃ ca vastuviśeṣe
viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvavanna vyavatiṣṭhate| na hi gaussarvaṃ prati gaureva
na tu kiṃcitpratyagauritivad viśeṣaṇaṃ viśeṣaṇameveti suvacam|
tathā ca kayācid dhātuvyaktyā upasthāpite'rthe kiṃ kartrādikamiti
praśene prakṛtavyaktyupātavyāpārāśrayatayā vivakṣitaḥ kartā,
vyāpāravyadhikaraṇaphalāśrayaḥ karma,
kartṛkarmadvārakasambandhenāśrayo'dhikaraṇam| pacyartho
vyāpāraścānekadhā| evaṃ ca
paceradhiśrayaṇataṇḍulāvapanaidhopakarṣaṇāditātparyakatve tadāśrayo
devadataḥ kartā, jvalanatātparyakatve edhāḥ kartāraḥ,
taṇḍuladhāraṇādiparatve sthālī kartrī, avayavavibāgādiparatve
taṇḍulāḥ kartāraḥ| sthālyā pacatītyatra tṛtīyopātavyāpārāśrayā'pi
sthālī karaṇameva, na tu kartrī, devadatādivyāpārasyaiva
taddhātūpātatvāt| tathā--ādikhādibhyāmupāte'rthe prayojyaḥ kartā|
tasminnevārthe bhakṣayatinopāte karma| adhipūrvaiḥ śīhādibhirupāte'rthe
ādhāraḥ karma, kevalairupāte adhikaraṇamityādi | etena jñānasya
svaprakāśatve kartṛkarmavirodha ityādi parāstam,

etena parasamavetakriyājanyaphalaśālitvaṃ karmatvaṃ ced
apādāne'tavyāptimāśaṅkya dhātvarthatāvacchedakaphalaśāli karmeti
pariṣkurvanto'pi pratyuktāḥ, grāmaṃ gamayati devadatamityādau
gantaryavyāpteḥ| tanmate gamanasya prakṛtyarthatve'pi
tatvānavacchedakatvāt| tatra ṇico vyāparārthakatvaṃ na prakṛteḥ| tatve
vā pācayatyādiprayojyakartaryativyāpteḥ|
pāribhāṣikamanuśāsanopayogi karmatvaṃ vacanabalāt
vyavasthitamiti ced ? apādāne'pi tarhi tannāstītyavadhehi,
ekasaṃjñādhikāre'navakāśayā sāvakāśāyā bādhāt| ata eva
ṭhātmānamātmanā hanti' ityādau parayā kartṛsaṃjñayā
karmasaṃjñābādhamāśaṅkyāhaṅkārādiviśiṣṭātmabhedamāśritya
samāhitaṃ bhāṣye|
rāmeṇeti| iha yadyapi vibhaktyupasthitānāṃ kārakāṇāṃ kriyāṃ prati
viśeṣaṇataiva tathā'pi kṛdupasthitānāṃ viśeśyataiva, 'satvapradhānāni
nāmāni"ityukteḥ| dhātūpasthāpyayoḥ phalavyāpārayorapi
karmakatsamabhivyāhāre viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvavyatyāsaḥ| tathā ca
rāmamiṣṭho yo
vyāpārastadviṣayībhūtabāṇavyāpārasādhyaprāṇaviyogāśrayo vālīti
vākyārthaḥ|
yatu vājapeyādhikaraṇe mīmāṃsakairuktaṃ--ṭhkāṣṭhṬaḥ pākaḥ' ityapi
syāditi, tadiṣṭāpatigrastam| "kartṛkaraṇe kṛtā bahulam"
"kartṛkarmaṇoḥ kṛti" ityādīsūtrāṇāṃ kṛtprakṛtyarthena karakānvayaṃ
vinoktisaṃbhavasyaivābhāvāditi sudhībhirākalanīyam|
anabhihite kim ? hariḥ karoti| jīvantyanena jīvanaḥ| śābdikaḥ|
"śabdarduraṃ karoti" iti ṭhak| kṛtaṃ viśvaṃ yena kṛtaviśvaḥ,
viśvakarmakakṛtyāśraya ityarthaḥ; aṣaṣaṭhaḷrthabahuvrīhau
vyutpatyantarasvīkārāt| yathā caitatathā vakṣyate| prakṛtyādibhya iti |
ākṛtigaṇo'yam| tena ṭhnāmnā sutīkṣṇaścaritena dāntaḥ' ityādi
bodhyam| dvayordroṇayoḥ samāhāro dvidroṇam|
pātrāditvātstrītvābhāvaḥ| droṇadvayasambandhi dhānyamityarthaḥ| 
cāditi| karaṇaśabdānuvṛtyā "parikrayaṇe sampradānam"
ityutarasūtrasthānyatarasyāṃgrahaṇāpakarṣaṇena vā saṃjñayoḥ paryāye
labdhe samāveśārthaścakāraḥ| tena ṭhmanasādevaḥ' ityatra karmaṇyaṇ,
karaṇe tṛtīyā cetyubhayaṃ siddhyati| "manasaḥ saṃjñāyām" ityaluk|
kiṃ cākṣerdeavayate yajñadatenetyatra sakarmakatvādaṇi kartarṇau karmatvaṃ
na, "aṇāvakarmakāt" iti parasmaipadaṃ ca neti siddhāntaḥ|
yatu prācā karmasaṃjñaṃ vā syaditi vyākhyātam,
tadākaravirodhādupekṣyam| syadetat, karaṇasaṃjñāyā avakaśaḥ devanā
akṣāḥ, karaṇe lyuṭ| karmasaṃjñāyāstu- dīvyante akṣāḥ, karmaṇi laṭ|
ṭhakṣairdīvyati' ityatra saṃjñādvayaprayuktakāryadvayaprasaṅge paratvāt
tṛtīyaiva syānna tu dvitīyeti cet ? atrāhuḥ- kāryakālapakṣe
"karmaṇi dvitīyā" ityatra yadasyopasthānaṃ tasyanavakāśatvād
dvitīyeti| nanu ṭhdīvyante akṣāḥ' ityatra karmaṇyabhihite'pi
karaṇatvasyānabhidhānāt tṛtīyā syāt tathā ṭhdevanā akṣāḥ' ityatra
lyuṭā karaṇasyābhidhāne'pi karmaṇo'nabhidhānāt dvitīyā syāt ?
maivam, ekaiva hyatra śaktiḥ saṃjñādvayayoginī| tathā ca
ubhayatrāpyabhidhānameva na tvanabhihitatvam|
prau.ma.--sahayukte'pradhāne (pā.sū.-->
"sahayukte"|| putreṇeti | puturatra kriyāsaṃbandhaḥ śābdaḥ, putrasya tu
ātha iti tasyāprādhānyam| apradhānagrahaṇaṃ śakyamakartuma| na caivaṃ
piturapi tṛtīyāpatiḥ| tatra prātipadikarthamātre
antaraṅgatvātprathamotpateḥ| ṭhpiturāgamanam' ityādau
kārakavibhakteḥ prābalyācca|
prau.ma.-yenāṅgavikāraḥ (pā.sū.-->
"yenāṅgavikāraḥ"|| aṅgaśabdo'rśa ādyajantastadāha--aṅgina iti|
yeneti sarvanāmnā prakṛtyarthabhūto'vayava eva gṛhyate, sannidhānāt|
sacārthādvikṛta eva, nahyavikṛtenāvayavena śarīrasya vikāraḥ
saṃbhavati| tadetadāha--yenāṅgena vikṛteneti| 
saṃjānīta iti| "sampratibhyāmanādhyāne" iti taṅ| kṛdyoge paratvāt
ṣaṣṭhī pituḥ saṃjñātā| yatu haradatenoktam- ādhyāne paratvād
"adhāgartha" iti ṣaṣṭhī ṭhmātuḥ saṃjānāti' iti ;tanna, tatra
śeṣādhikārāt|
dravyādīti| uktaṃ ca--
dravyādiviṣayo hetuḥ karaṇaṃ niyatakriyam|| iti|
anāśrite tu vyāpāre nimitaṃ heturiṣyate || iti ca|
phalamapīti| etacca ṭhpratyaya' iti sūtre kaiyaṭe spaṣṭam| evaṃ ca
tādarthyacaturthyā saheyaṃ tṛtīyā vikalpyate| adhyayanāya vasati| ayaṃ
tu viśeṣaḥ--adhyayanasya phalena sahābhedaḥ saṃsargaḥ, upakārakatvena saha
tu nirūpakateti| gamyamāneti| na tu śrūyamāṇaivetyāgraha iti bhāvaḥ|
sādhaneti| sādhyamityatra prakṛtibhāgasyārthaṃ pratīti bhāvaḥ| ṭhmā
cāpalāyeti gaṇān vyanaiṣīt' ityatra tu yatadhvamiti śeṣaḥ|
aśiṣṭeti| etacca ṭhdāṇaśca sā ceccaturthyarthe' ityanena jñāpyate||
|| iti tṛtīyā ||
anvarthasaṃjñābalenāha-dānasyeti| tena ṭhajāṃ nayati grāmam' ṭhhastaṃ
nidadhāti vṛkṣe' ityādau nātiprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ| dānaṃ
cāpunargahaṇāya svasvatvanivṛtipūrvakaṃ parasvatvotpādanam| ata eva
ṭhrajakasya vastraṃ dadāti' ityādau na bhavati ; tatra hi dadātirbhāktaḥ|
etacca vṛtikṛnmatam |bhāṣyamate tu nānvarthatāyāmāgrahaḥ,
ṭhkhaṇḍikopādhyāyaḥ śiṣyāya capeṭāṃ dadāti' iti prayogāt|
ṭhrajakasya vastraṃ dadāti' iti tu śeṣatvavivakṣāyāṃ bodhyam| na
caivam ṭhajāṃ nayati grāmam' ityatrāpi prasaṅgaḥ ṭhyamabhipraiti'
ityuktyā hi yamiti nirdiṣṭasya śeṣitvaṃ, karmaṇaśca śeṣatvaṃ labhyate|
grāmasya ajāṃ prati śeṣitvaṃ nāsti| dānīya iti| bāhulakāt
sampradāne anīyar|
prau.ma.-rucyarthānāṃ prīyamāṇaḥ (pā.sū.-->
"rucyarthānāṃ prīyamāṇaḥ"|| prīñ tarpaṇe, asmāt karmaṇi laṭ| haraye
rocate iti| hariṃ prīṇayatītyarthaḥ| karmasaṃjñāyāṃ prāptāyāṃ vacanam|
prīyamāṇaḥ kim ? devadatāya rocate modakaḥ pathi| atra patho mā
bhūt|
prau.ma. ślāghanhuṅsthāśapāṃ jñīpsyamānaḥ (pā.sū.-->
"ślāghanhuṅ"|| ślāghā stutiḥ| kṛṣṇāya ślāghate| kṛṣṇaṃ
stautītyarthaḥ| anye tvāhuḥ--kṛṣṇāyātmānaṃ paraṃ vā ślāghyaṃ
kathayatītyartha iti| tatrādye pakṣe karmatve prāpte dvitīye tu
kārakaśeṣatvāt ṣaṣṭhaḷaṃ prāptāyāṃ vacanam| hanute iti| sapatnībhyaḥ
kṛṣṇaṃ hanuvānā tamevārthaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ bodhayatītyarthaḥ| yasya kasyacit
hanute iti vā| tiṣṭhate iti| sthityā svābhiprāyaṃ bodhayatītyarthaḥ|
"prakāśanastheyakhyayośca" iti taṅ| 
prau.ma.-dhārerutamarṇaḥ( pā.sū.-->
"dhārerutamarṇaḥ" || ṣaṣṭhaḷaṃ prāptāyāṃ vacanam| 
prau.ma.-spṛherīpsitaḥ (pā.sū.-->
"spṛherīpsitaḥ"|| paratvāditi| tena ṭhparaspareṇa spṛhaṇīyaśobham'
ṭhspṛhaṇīyaguṇairmahātmabhiḥ' ityādi siddham| yadā tu śeṣatvavivakṣā
tadā ṣaṣṭhī| tena --
ṭhkumārya iva kāntasya trasyanti spṛhayanti ca'|
iti siddham| etacca sarvaṃ haradatādirītyoktam|
vākyapadīyahelārājagranthayostu "spṛhayatiyoge karmasaṃjñāyāḥ
śeṣaṣaṣṭhaḷaśca bādhikeyaṃ sampradānasaṃjñā iti sthitam| tanmate
spṛhaṇīyamityatra dānīya itivat sampradāne kṛtyaḥ| kumārya ivetyatra
tu vibhaktivipariṇāmena kāntāya spṛhayantīti vyākhyeyam|
prau.ma.-krudhadruherṣyāsūyārthānāṃ yaṃ prati kopaḥ (pā.sū.-->
"krudhadruha" || krudha kope, druha drohe, īrṣya īrṣyāyām| asūyeti
kaṇḍvādiyagantaḥ|eṣāmartha ivārtho yeṣāṃ dhātūnāmityarthaḥ| tatra
krodho'marṣaḥ, droho'pakāraḥ,īrṣyā asahanam, asūyā tu guṇeṣu
doṣāropaḥ| krudhadruhorakarmakatvātadyoge ṣaṣṭhī prāptā, anyayoḥ
sakarmakatvād dvitīyā praptā|
prau.ma.- rādhīkṣyoryasya vipraśnaḥ (pā.sū.-->
"rādhīkṣyoḥ"|| kārakamiti| karmakārakamiti tu prācaḥ pramādaḥ,
ekasaṃjñādhikārāt| na ca prāthamikaprāptimātrābhiprāyeṇa
karmatvavyapadeśaḥ, kṛṣṇasambandhi śubhāśubhaṃ
paryālocayatītyarthābhyupagamena ṣaṣṭhaḷa eva prāpteḥ|
prau.ma.- anupratigṛṇaśca (pā.sū.-->
"anupratigṛṇaśca"|| gṛṇa iti śnāntasyānukaraṇāt ṣaṣṭhī| hotre iti
| karmatve prāpte|
prau.ma.- parikrayaṇe sampradānamanyatarasyām (pā.sū.-->
"parikrayaṇe" || krayo--dhanādyarpaṇenā''tyantikasvīkaraṇam, tasya
samīpaṃ pariśabda ācaṣṭe|tacca niyatakālaṃ tato nyūnatvāditi
vyācaṣṭe--niyateti| bhṛtyā--vetanādinā| tādarthye iti| tasmai
kāryāyedaṃ tadartham--kāraṇam, tasya bhāvastādarthyam| ṣyañā
upakārakatvarūpaḥ sambandho'bhidhīyate, ṭhkṛtaddhitasamāsebhyaḥ
sambandhābhimānaṃ bhāvapratyayena' iti siddhāntāt| upakārakatvaṃ
cānekadhā| tena brāhmaṇāya dadhītyādāvapi bhavati| bhaktirjñānāyeti|
jñānātmanā pariṇamata ityarthaḥ| prakṛtivikṛtyorbhedavivakṣāyāṃ
vikṛtivācakāccaturthī| abhedavivakṣāyāṃ tu paratvātprathamaiva,
bhaktirjñānaṃ kalpata iti| yadā tu "janikartuḥ" iti bhakterapādānatvaṃ
vivakṣyate tadā jñānasyābhihitakartṛtvātprathamaiva, bhakterjñānaṃ kalpate|
ṭhklṛpī'tyarthagrahaṇamityāśayenāha--sampadyata ityādi| utpāteneti|
prāṇināṃ śubhāśubhasūcako bhūtavikāra utpātaḥ, tena jñāpite'rthe
vartamānācturthītyarthaḥ| vātāyeti| vātasya jñāpiketyarthaḥ|
hiteti| samāsavidhānājjñāpakāditi bhāvaḥ| evaṃ sukhayoge'pi
bodhyam| 
"tumuṇvulau kriyāyām" iti sūtramahimnā kriyārthakamupapadaṃ
kriyāvācyeva phalatītyāśayenāha-kriyeti| kriyāphalakaṃ
kriyāvācaprakṛtikamityarthaḥ| ṭhtumun' iti ṇvulo'pyupalakṣaṇam|
phalebhyo yātītyasya phalānyāharaka ityapi vivaraṇe bādhakābhāvāt|
karmaṇīti| tathā ca dvitīyāpavādo'yamiti bhāvaḥ| 
yatu prācā vyākhyānamaprayujyamānasyaiva karmaṇi yathā syāt,
prayujyamānasya mā bhūditi niyamāya sūtramiti, tanna, aprāptasya
niyamāyogāt| na ca ṭhtādarthya' iti prāptiḥ| na hi phalārthā
yānakriyā kiṃ tvāharaṇārthā| āharaṇaṃ tu phalakarmakamityanyadetat|
tumuno bhāva eva vidhānātadarthasya bhāvavacanatve siddhe
punarbhavavacanagrahaṇaṃ sūtraviśeṣaparigrahārthamityāha- iti sūtreṇeti|
na ca tādarthye ityeva gatārthatā śaṅkyā, bhāvavacanenaiva tumuneva
tādarthyasya dyotitatayā prātipadikārthamātre prathamāprasaṅgāt|
yatu prācoktam--bhāvavacanādeva yathā syāt pācako vrajatīti
ṇvulantāt mā bhūditi niyamārthamidaṃ sūtramiti; tanna, ṇvalante
kartuḥ prādhānyāt kartāraṃ prati ca tādarthyābhāvāt,
duṇībhūtayānasya ca vibhaktyarthānanvayāt|
nanu ṣaṣṭhaḷpīti kutaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḷevāstu, alaṃ śabdastu paryāptītarārthaka
eva gṛhyatāmityāśaṅkyāha--tasmai prabhavatīti|
na tvāṃ tṛṇamityādi| nanu tṛṇādivad yuṣmaccchabdādapi pakṣe
caturthyā bhāvyam| maivam, ṭhanādare' iti hi karmaṇo
viśeṣaṇam--anādaradyotakaṃ yatkarmeti| tādṛśaṃ tṛṇameva na tu
yuṣmadarthaḥ| 
|| iti caturthī ||
prau.ma.- dhruvamapāye'pādānam (pā.sū.-->
dhruvamiti|| dhru gatisthairyayoḥ, asmātkuṭādeḥ pacādyac| dhruva sthairye
iti pāṭhe tu igupadhalakṣaṇaḥ kaḥ| dhruvaṃ--sthiram,
prakṛtadhātūpātagatyanāviṣṭatve sati tajjanya vibhādāśrayaḥ|
taccārthādavadherevetyāha--avadhibhūtamiti| dhāvata iti| iha
kriyāviśiṣṭasyāpyaśvasya prakṛtadhātūpātakriyāṃ pratyavadhitvaṃ na
viruddhyate | ṭhparasparasmānmeṣāvapasarataḥ' ityatra tu sṛdhātunā
gatidvayasyāpyupādānād ekaniṣṭhāṃ gatiṃ pratītarasyāpādānatvaṃ na
virudhyate| uktaṃ ca--
apāye yadudāsīnaṃ calaṃ vā yadi vā'calam|
dhruvamevātadāveśātadapādānamucyate ||
patato dhruva evāśvo yasmādaśvātpatatyasau|
tasyāpyaśvasya patane kuḍaḷdi dhruvamiṣyate||
meṣāntarakriyāpekṣamavadhitvaṃ pṛthak pṛthak|
meṣayoḥ svakriyāpekṣaṃ kartṛtvaṃ ca pṛthak pṛthak||iti|
parvatātpatato'śvātpatatītyatra tu
parvatāvadhikapatanāśrayo'śvastadavadhikaṃ
devadatādiniṣṭhapatanamityarthaḥ| pañcamyarthe'vadhau abhedena saṃsargeṇa
prakṛtyartho viśeṣaṇam| pratyayārthastu kriyāyāṃ, sa ca dharmī na tu
dharmamātram, vibhaktyarthe vihitasya bahuvrīherdharmiparatāyā
nirvavādatvāt| uddhṛtaudanā sthālītyatra hi
odanakarmakoddharaṇāvadhibhūtetyarthaḥ| jugupseti| bhāṣyakārastu
jugupsādīnāṃ tatpūrvakanivṛtilakṣakatāmāśritya sūtreṇa
tatsiddhimāhuḥ| evamutarasūtreṣvapi |
prau.ma.-bhītrārthānāṃ bhayahetuḥ (pā.sū.-->
"bhītrā" || kathaṃ tarhi--
"kasya bibhyati devāśca jātaroṣasya saṃyuge"
iti rāmāyaṇam ? atrāhuḥ-kasya saṃyuge iti | na caivaṃ
saṃyugasyaivāpādānasañjñāpatiḥ, parayā'dhikaraṇasaṃjñayā bādhāt|
adhikaraṇatvāvivakṣāyāṃ tviṣṭāpatiḥ| adhyayanāditi|
addhyayanasambandhinī glānirityarthaḥ|
prau.ma.- janikartuḥ prakṛtiḥ (pā.sū.-->
"janikartuḥ prakṛtiḥ"|| jananaṃ janiḥ--utpatiḥ| ṭhiñādibhyaḥ' iti
janerbhāve iñ, ṭhjanighasibhyāmiṇ' ityuṇādisūtreṇa iṇ vā|
"janivadhyośca" iti vṛddhipratiṣedhaḥ| tasyāḥ karteti śeṣaṣaṣṭhaḷa
samāsaḥ| tathā ca dhātvantarayoge'pi bhavati| ṭhaṅgādaṅdātsambhavasi'
iti yathā| tadetadāha- jāyamānasyeti| etena ṭhikśtipau
dhātunirdeaśe' iti ikā nirdeaśo'yamityāśritya "gamahana"
ityupadhālopaprasaṅgamarthāsaṅgatiṃ samasānupapati codbhāvayanto
mīmāṃsāvārtikakārāḥ samāhitā iti bhāvaḥ| iha sūtre prakṛtigrahaṇaṃ
hetumātraparamiti vṛtikṛnmatam, putrātpramodo jāyate'
ityudāharaṇam| upādānamātraparamiti tu bhāṣyakaiyaṭamatam|
tadubhayasādhāraṇamudāharaṇamāha--brahmaṇa iti| brahmā- hiraṇyagarbhaḥ| sa ca
hetureva, na tūpādānam| kiṃ ca bra hma māyāśabalam, taddhi
sarvakāryyopādānam, iti vādāntasiddhāntaḥ|
prau.ma.-bhuvaḥ prabhavaḥ (pā.sū.-->
"bhuvaḥ"|| bhavanaṃ bhūriti| sampadāditvādbhāve kvip| pūrvasūtre
samāsanirdiṣṭamapi kartṛgrahaṇamanuvartate,
svaritatvādityāha--bhūkarturiti| prabhavatyasmāditi prabhavaḥ| lyablopa
iti| lyabantasya gamyamānārthakatvādaprayoge ityarthaḥ| yataśceti|
yadavadhitvenāśrityādhvanaḥ kālasya vā nirmāṇaṃ paricchedaḥ pratipādyate
tasmādityarthaḥ| tena pañcamyantārthenā'rthadvārā
prayuktānnirmīyamāṇā'dhvavācinaḥ prathamāsaptamyau staḥ| kalāditi|
tadyauktādityapekṣyate|
prapañcārthamiti| na ca "itarastvanyanīcayoḥ"
ityamarokternīcārthakasyedaṃ grahaṇamiti vācyam, "asmātāro mandro
vā" ityādāviva "pañcamī vibhakte' ityanenaiva siddhatvāt|
yatu itara iti nirdiśyamānapratiyogika eva prayujyate | tadyathā-- caitraḥ
śūraḥ, itaraḥ kātara iti| ato nāyamanyārtha iti; tanmandam,
pratiyogyā''kāṅkṣāyā ubhayatra tulyatvāt udāhṛtakośavirodhācca|
ṛte kṛṣṇāditi| kathantarhi ṭhpuruṣārādhanamṛte' iti
?pramādo'yamityeke| anye tu ṭhubhasarvatasoḥ' ityatra dṛśigrahaṇāt
ṭhcaitraṃ yāvacchītam' ityādāviva ṛtayoge dvitīyāpi
sādhurityāhuḥ| yaktaṃ caitat, ṭhṛte dvitīyā ca' iti cāndrasūtrāt|
prabhṛtyarthayoga iti| prabhṛtyarthairyoga ityarthaḥ| tathā ca "kārtikyāḥ
prabhṛti" iti bhāṣyaṃ vivṛṇvan kaiyaṭaḥ prāyuṅkta--tata ārabhyetyarthaḥ
iti|
vacanagrahaṇāditi| sāmarthyādavadhimātraṃ vivakṣyate| tena vineti
viśeṣaṇāṃśastu neti bhāvaḥ| yadvā-- maryādeti śabda
ucyate'smiṃstanmaryādāvacanam--"āṅmaryādābhividhyoḥ" iti
sūtram, tatra ya āṅ dṛṣṭaḥ sa uktasaṃjña ityarthaḥ|

dvirvacanābhāvaḥ| pariratreti| atara pañcamīvidhau varjanārthenāpena
sāhacaryāditi bhāvaḥ|
prau.ma.- pratinidhipratidāne ca yasmāt (pā.sū.-->
"pratinidhipratidāne ca"|| asmādeva nirdeaśādābhyāṃ yoge
pañcamītyāhuḥ| mukhyasyā'bhāve tatsadṛśa upādīyate sa pratinidhiḥ|
datasya pratiniryātanaṃ pratidānam|
prau.ma.-akartaryṛṇe pañcamī (pā.sū.-->
"akartari"|| pañcamī syāditi| tṛtīyāpavādo'yam| śateneti|
śatamiha utamarṇāya dhāryamāṇatvādṛṇam| "tatprayojako hetuśca" iti
cakāreṇa kartṛsaṃjñā ca|
yogavibhāgāditi| etacca "hetumanuṣyebhya" iti sūtre haradatagranthe
spaṣṭam| striyāṃ ceti| ṭhbāhulakaṃ prakṛtestanudṛṣṭeḥ' iti
vārtikanirdeaśo'pīha jñāpakaḥ|
prau.ma.- pṛthagvinānānābhistṛtīyānyatarasyām (pā.sū.-->
"pṛthagvinā"|| samuccayārthamiti| nipātānamanekārthatvāditi bhāvaḥ|
pañcamīti| tatra maṇḍūakaplutyā pañcamī| dvitīyā tu sannihitaiva|
dūraṃ, dūrāt, dūreṇa veti| iha saptamyapi vakṣyate| kiṃ ca
"dūrāntikārÂthebhya" iti
sūtrasyotaratrāpyanuvartanādadhikaraṇe'pyebhyo vibhakticatuṣṭayaṃ
bodhyam| tathā ca prayujyate--ṭhdūrādāvasathānmūtram' iti| āvasathasya
dūre ityarthaḥ| asatveti| satvavacanatvaṃ ca tadviśeṣyakatvam| evaṃ ca

dravyam' iti prācāṃ grantho vyākhyātaḥ| yatu--tamanūdya kriyāyā

kriyā na yujyate liṅgakriyānādhārakārakaiḥ|
asatvarūpatā tasyā iyamevāvadhāryatām||
|| iti pañcamī ||
rājñaḥ puruṣa iti| pratyayarthasya prakṛtyarthaṃ prati prādhānayādapradhānādeva
ṣaṣṭhī| tadarthaśca puruṣe viśeṣaṇam| pradhānātu na ṣaṣṭhī,
uktanyāyavirodhāt, śeṣatvābhāvācca| puruṣaśabdo hi yathāyathaṃ
prātipadikārthamātravṛtiḥ kārakārthako vā| yadā tu naivaṃ tadā
puruṣaśabdādapi ṣaṣṭhī bhavatyeva| yatu --apradhānaṃ śeṣa iti, tanna,
prātipadikārthamātre ṭhśuklo ghaṭaḥ' ityādau viśeṣaṇe
ṣaṣṭhīprasaṅgāt| na ca prathamayā bādhaḥ, tasyāḥ prādhānye
caritārthatvāt| karmadīnāmapīti| yathā viśeṣā'vivakṣāyāṃ
ṭhrūpavān' iti, tadvivakṣāyāṃ tu ṭhnīlaḥ pīta' ityādī prayujyate
tathedamapi nyāyasiddhamiti bhāvaḥ|
satāmiti| evaṃ ca ṭhnapuṃsake bhāve ktasya yoge śeṣatvavivakṣāyāṃ ca'
iti ṭhtṛptyarthānāṃ karaṇe ṣaṣṭhī vā' iti ca prācāṃ granthe paṭhaḷmānaṃ
nāpūrvaṃ vacanamiti bhāvaḥ| sarpiṣa iti| jño'vidarthasya karaṇe,
adhīgarthadayeśāṃ karmaṇi, kṛñaḥ pratiyatne , rujārthānāṃ
bhāvavacanānāmajvareḥ, āśiṣi nāthaḥ, jāsiniprahaṇanāṭakrāthapiṣāṃ
hiṃsāyām, vyavahṛpaṇoḥ samarthayoḥ, kṛtvo'rthaprayoge
kāle'dhikaraṇe ityaṣṭasūtrī tu neha vyākhyātā, tasyāḥ
samāsanivṛtiphalakatvāt| tathā hi--tatra ṭhśeṣa' ityanuvartate|
śeṣatvena vivakṣite ca karaṇādau ṣaṣṭhī siddhaiva| tadayamarthaḥ- iha
ṣaṣṭhaḷeva na tu talluk| tathā ca lukaḥ prayojakībhūtaḥ samāsa eva
neti| tathā ca vārtikam--ṭhpratipadavidhānā ṣaṣṭhī na ' ityādi|
hariścāha--
kārakairvyapadiṣṭe ca śrūyamāṇakriye punaḥ|
proktā pratipadaṃ ṣaṣṭhī samāsasya nivṛtaye|| iti|
tena śeṣatvavivakṣāyāṃ ṭhsarpiṣo jñānaṃ' ṭhmātuḥ smaraṇam'
ityādīnyasamastānyeva sādhūni| ṭhharismaraṇam' ityādīni tu
śeṣatvavivakṣāyāṃ kṛdyogaṣaṣṭhaḷa samāse bodhyāni| tatra ca
kārakapūrvakatvātkṛdutarapadaprakṛtisvaraḥ| śeṣaṣaṣṭhaḷa samāse
tvantodātatvaṃ syāt, taccāniṣṭam| tathā ca svarārtheyamaṣṭasūtrīti
paramaniṣkarṣaḥ| kiṃ ca ṭhmātuḥ smṛtam' ityādau samāsābhāvo'pi
phalam| na hi tatra kārakaṣaṣṭhī labhyate, "na loka" iti niṣedhāt|
āha ca--
niṣṭhāyāṃ karmaviṣayā ṣaṣṭhī ca pratiṣidhyate|
śeṣalakṣaṇayā ṣaṣṭhaḷa samāsastatra neṣyate|| iti|
evaṃ sthite vṛtyādibahugrantheṣu iha prakaraṇe udāharaṇaṃ
pratyudāharaṇaṃ ca yadyatiṅntaṃ dṛśyate tat sarvamupalakṣaṇatayā

prau.ma.-sarvanāmnastṛtīyā ca (pā|sū|-->

bhāvaḥ| nimitaparyāyeti| paryāyagrahaṇasya
phalamudāharati--evamityādinā| prācā tu ṭhkimartham' ityudāhṛtam|
tatra kimaḥ klībatvaṃ durlabhamÂ| sāmānye napuṃsakatāṃ samāsaṃ
cāśritya kathaṃcidvā neyam| asarvanāmna iti| ṭhnimitakāraṇahetuṣu
sarvāsāṃ prāyadarśanam' iti vārtikaṃ hi vṛtikāreṇa yadyapīhoktaṃ,
tathā'pi bhāṣyakṛtā "hetau" ityatra paṭhitam| tena sarvanāmā'prayoge
api pravartate| kiṃ tu prāyagrahaṇātatra tṛtīyadaya evetyarthaḥ|
varddhamānastu prathamāmapīccchati|

prau.ma.-dūrāntikārthaiḥ ṣaṣṭhaḷnyatarasyām (pā.sū.-->

prau.ma.-divastadarthasya (pā.sū.-->
"divastadarthasya"|| taccchabdena vyavahṛpaṇau parāmṛśyete| tau ca dyaūte
krayavikrayavyavahāre ca tulyārthau pūrvasūtre gṛhītau,
ityāśayenāha--dyaūte iti| karmaṇīti| iha ṭhśeṣe' iti na sambadhyate
utarasūtradvayārambhāditi bhāvaḥ| ata eveha trisūtryā
tiṅntamudāharati--śatasya dīvyatīti|
prau.ma.- preṣyabruvorhaviṣo devatāsampradāne (pā.sū-->

samāsasyā'prasaktatvāt|
prau.ma.- kartṛkarmaṇoḥ kṛti (pā.sū.->

bhaṭṭiḥ ? atrāhuḥ--anityamidam, "tadarham" iti nirdeaśāt|

prau.ma.- gostriyorupasarjanasya (pā.sū.-->

prācā--go'ntastrīpratyayāntayorupasarjanayorityuktam; tanna,

atiprasaṅgāt| yatu tatpautreṇa

parigaṇanādadhinā'bhihite'pi saptamīti, tanna, parigaṇanasyākare

tathaivoktatvāt ṭhkramādamuṃ nārada ityabodhi saḥ' ityādi
prayogavirodhācca|
dvitīyāpañcamyoriti| etacca rakṣigranthe spaṣṭam| yatu
vadanti--caitrasya vanādārādityādau sāpekṣatvādasamāse
caritārthatvādvidhānasāmarthyaṃ nāstīti; taccintyam, nityasamāsasya
nyāyyatve sati caitrasyetyādiviśeṣaṇaprayogasyaivāsambhavāt| uktaṃ
hi bhāṣye--"vṛtasya viśeṣaṇayogo na" iti| nanu "saviśeṣaṇānāṃ
vṛtirna" ityapi bhāṣye uktamiti cet ? satyam, anityasamāsaviṣayaṃ
tat| nityasamāsasyāpi saviśeṣaṇeṣvapravṛtau mahataḥ kumbhasyopa
ityādyāpadyeta| yatu prācā kṛṣṇasyopetyuktam, yacca vyācakhyurīdṛśe
prakriyāvākye sthite iti, tadubhayamapi prāguktarītyā prāmādikameva|
samprati neti| sampratītyavyayamidānīmityarthe|
etarhi sampratīdānīmadhunā sāmprataṃ tathā|| ityamaraḥ|
taccā'dhikaraṇaśaktipradhānatvātkriyāpadenaivānvayārham| niṣedho'pi
kriyāyā eva|
tadātadāha--yujyate iti| asampratīti| samāsavṛtau tu
yujikriyā'ntarbhavati ṭhdadhyayodana' ityatra sekakriyā yathā | yatu
prācoktam--asampratīti sampratyabhāva ityarthaḥ| anenopabhogyavastuno
yo vartamānaḥ kālastasyaiva niṣedhaḥ kriyate iti; tanna, adhikaraṇasya
niṣedhaṃ prati pratiyogitvenānvayasya sarvatantraviruddhatvāt| na hi
bhūtale ghaṭo nāstītyanena bhūtalaṃ niśidhyata iti kaścidabhyupaiti,
na vā yuktisahamityastāṃ tāvat| itiharīti| svarūpapareṇa
ṣaṣṭhaḷntena hariśabdena saha prakāśārthasyetiśabdasya samāsaḥ|
pratiśabdasyeti| yatvāhuḥ--grāmasya vṛkṣaṃ pratītyatra sāpekṣatvena
samāsābhāve dvitīyāvidhānaṃ sāvakāśamiti tatprāgeva dūṣitam|
haradatastvāha- arthamarthaṃ pratīti bhāṣyakāraprayogād vākyamapi sādhu|
yadvā nātrāvyayaṃ vīpsāvṛti kiṃ tarhi karmapravacanīyatvāt
sambandhamavaccchinati| vīpsā tu dvirvacanadyotyeti|
sahapūrvāhṇamiti| sākalye'vyayībhāvaḥ| guṇabhūte'pīti| yadi
sādṛśya iti nocyeta tato yatra sādṛśyaṃ pradhānaṃ tatraiva syāt,
avyayārthaprādhānyasyāvyayībhāve āṬtsargikatvāditi bhāvaḥ| anta iti|
idānīmetāvānpradeśe'dhyetavya iti yāvataḥ pradeśasya parigrahaḥ

tṛtīyāntaḥ samasyate| yadyapyagninā saheti prayogārhameva
tathā'pisāhityamātre tathā| antatvavivakṣāyāṃ tu samaso nityaḥ| ata

thamāntenāgnerantatvamiti ṣaṣṭhaḷntena vā vigraha iti prathamāntena

prau.ma.- vibhāṣā (pā.sū.-->
"vibhāṣā" || etaditi| nyāyasiddhe'rthe liṅgadarśanamidam|
ṭighubhādisaṃjñānāmiva samasasaṃjñāyā api nityaprāptatvādvacanaṃ vinā
viddhyantarāṇāmiva saṃjñānāmapi vikalpā'lābhāt|
syādetat| "supsupā" ityapi tarhi nityaṃ syāt| na ceṣṭāpatiḥ,
apasiddhāntāt| tathā hi--caraṭsūtre vṛtikṛtoktaṃ pūrvaṃ bhūto
bhūtapūrvaḥ| supsupeti samāsa iti| tathā śākalasūtre
"sinnityasamāsayoḥ śākalapratiṣedha ityatra nityagrahaṇena nārtha idamapi
siddhaṃ bhavati vāpyāmaśvo vāpyaśva" iti bhāṣyaṃ kaiyaṭo
vyākhyāt--ṭhvāpyaśva iti supsupeti samāsaḥ| "saṃjñāyām" iti
samāsasya nityatvātsiddhaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ' iti| tathā "anudātaṃ
padamekavarjam" "asiddhavadatra" ityādisūtreśaṣvapi kaiyaṭe spaṣṭam|
ata āha--supsupetīti samāseti| yogavibhāgenaiva siddhe samāse
utarasūtraiḥ punarvidhānaṃ prācīnasya kvācitkatāṃ jñāpayati| evaṃ ca
ivena samāsasyāpi kvācitkatvādudbāhuriva vāmana ityādi
siddham| iveneti vārtikaṃ tu yadyapi "kugati" ityatra paṭhaḷte tathā'pi
supsupetyatratyameva nityādhikāre smāritamita kaiyaṭaḥ| evaṃ sthite
ivena samāsasya chāndasatvaṃ kalpayantaḥ ptyuktāḥ| jīmūtasyevetyatra
bahvṛcaiḥ samāsābhyupagame'pi taitirīyairvyastasyaiva pāṭhāditi
dik|
prau.ma.-apaparibahirañcavaḥ pañcamyā (pā.sū.-->
"apaparibahiḥ"|| ata eva jñāpakādbahiryoge pañcamī| jñāpakasiddhaṃ na
sarvatra| tena ṭhkarasya karabho bahiḥ' iti siddham|

ityudāhāryam|

prau.ma.- nadībhiśca (pā.sū.-->
"nadībhiśca"||svarūpasya saṃjñāyā nadyāśca neha grahaṇaṃ
bahuvacananirddeśāt kiṃ tvarthasya| na ca tasya samāsaḥ saṃjñā bhavati|
atastadvācināmayaṃ samāsaḥ| te ca na kevalaṃ viśeṣaśabdā eva kiṃ tu
sāmānyaśabdo'pi|
nityasamāsa iti| etacca vṛtau spaṣṭam| śākalasūtre
kaiyaṭasvaraso'pyevam| nanvevaṃ nyāyasāmyāt bahuvrīherapi
nityasamāsatvaṃ syāditi cenmaivam,
yasyetyādipadāntarasamabhivyāhareṇānyapadārthapratīteḥ| ata eva
"saṃjñānavagamāt" ityuktaṃ na tvanyapadārthā'navagamāditi| saṃjñāyāṃ
kim ? śīghragaṅgo deśaḥ| 
athāvyayībhāve'sādhāraṇān samāsāntānāha--"avyayī"| samāsānta iti|

tenopaśaradamityatra "nāvyayībhāvāt" ityam, dvipurītyādau

indriyamātraparaḥ| samakṣamiti| akṣṇo yogyam|

paurṇamāsyāgrahāyaṇyoḥ pṛthaggrahaṇāt| upanadīti| ṭajabhāve
napuṃsakahrasvaḥ| idaṃ tvavadheyam| "nadībhiśca" iti sūtre

mānābhāvādityuktam| tena pañcanadamiti siddhyati| atra ca
nityo'c| taduktam--

ityamaraḥ|

prau.ma.- ṭābṛci (pā.sū.4-1-9>

prau.ma.- ḍābubhābhyāmanyatarasyām (pā.sū.4-1-13>

siddhaḥ, satyam| "anyatarasyām" iti yogo vibhajyate| tatra "manaḥ"

ityeva ṅīp|tena bahudhīvarīti siddhyati| "ṛnnebhya" iti hi

prau.ma.- pratyayasthātkāt pūrvasyāta idāpyasupaḥ (pā.sū.-->

sāṅkāśyiketyatrāpi prasajyeteti|

prau.ma.- bhastraiṣājājñādvāsvānañpūrvāṇāmapi (pā.sū.-->
"bhastraiṣā" || tadanteti| āṅgatvāditi bhāvaḥ| evaṃ ceti| uktaṃ ca
haradatena--
atrātaḥ sthāna ityetatsvaśabdasya viśeṣaṇam|
sambhavavyabhicārau hi tatra staḥ kākacoḥ satoḥ||

iha svaśabdasyetyasyā'nantaramata iti śeṣo bodhyaḥ| etacca iha

munermanovṛtimiva svikāyām|
iti śrīharṣaśloke "bhastraiṣā" iti vaikalpikamitvamiti vyācakṣāṇā

rūpadvayamākare sthitamityāhuḥ; taccintyam|
tādṛśasyā''karasyā'prasiddheḥ, liṅgānuśāsanavirodhācca| yatu-
svakā, svikā, iti kāśikāyāmudāhṛtaṃ; tatsaṃjñāviṣayakaṃ, na tu
jñātiviṣayakam| yadapi ṭhsvārthikāḥ kvacitprakṛtito

ityaṃśasya durupapādatvāt| pratyudāharaṇānyuktvā udāharaṇanyāha-
nirbhastriketyādi| niṣkrāntā bhastrāyā nirbhastrā| upasarjanahrasvaḥ,

vivakṣitaviṣayo na tu "na yāsayoḥ" itivadupalakṣaṇamiti bhāvaḥ|
strīliṅganirdeaśādajādayaḥ strīliṅgā eva gṛhyante| teneha na,
śubhro'jo'syāḥ śubhrājikā| jānātīti jñaḥ| priyo jño'syāḥ
priyajñikā iti|

tarhi prāggulkādāvapi prasajyeta| tatparihārāya pūrvoktaparyudāsasya
prasajyapratiṣedhānāṃ vā'nuvṛtau gauravamiti bhāvaḥ|
vṛṣalīti| ekasyāṃ vyaktau vṛṣalatve kathite tadapatyatatsahodarādau
kathanaṃ vinā'pi tasya sugrahatvāditi bhāvaḥ| āṬpagavīti|
aṇṇantalakṣaṇaṃ ṅīpaṃ paratvādayaṃ bādhate| evaṃ cāpatyādhikāre
āṬpagavīti pratīkamupādāya "ṭiḍḍhāṇañ" iti ṅībiti vyācakṣāṇā
upekṣyā iti bhāvaḥ| brāhnaṇītyatreti| evaṃ ca prācī ṅīṣudāharaṇaṃ
prāmādikamiti bhāvaḥ|
prau.ma. ūRutaḥ (pā.sū.-->
kurūriti| liṅgaviśiṣṭaparibhāṣayā prātipadikatvātsvādayaḥ| prācā
tu ṭhantādivacca' iti sūtramupanyasyā''divadbhāvatsvādaya ityuktam|
atredaṃ vaktavyam- paraṃ pratyādivadbhāvo vidhīyate| tathā
cehā'pratyaya iti niṣedha eva syānna tu svādayaḥ| yatu
vyācakhyuḥ--ādiḥ- ūṅpekṣayā pūrvaḥ, prātipadikaṃ, tadvadbhāvāt
tadavayavatvena grahāt pūrvantavadbhāvāditi yāvaditi; tadapi
kliṣṭaṃ svaparagranthavirudhdaṃ ca| uktarītyā hi kāṇḍe kuḍaḷe
ityādāvapi ṭhekavacanamutsargataḥ' iti suḥ pravarteta| tadvāraṇāya
cā'pratyaya iti prasajyapratiṣedhābhyupagame prakṛte doṣaḥ|
śvaśrūrityatraikādeśā'bhāvena liṅgaviśiṣṭaparibhāṣāyā
āvaśyakatvācca| yatu- tatra "śvaśuraḥ śvaśrvā" iti nirdeaśātsvādaya iti;
tanna, nirdeaśasya śabdaparatvāt| "viparābhyāṃ jeḥ" "ReĀryaḥ" itivat|
śvaśurasyeti| puṃyogalakṣaṇe ṅīṣi prāpte ūṅ| tatsanniyogena ca
vidhīyamāno lopaḥ sannihitatvādantyasyaivā'kārasya, na tvādeḥ|
etacca vacanaṃ "śvaśuraḥ śvaśrvā"iti nirdeaśasiddhārthakathanaparam| ato'pi
prathamā'kārasya lopo na śaṅkyaḥ| 
upamānavācipūrvapadamiti| ṭhdhātrīkarābhyāṃ karabhopamoruḥ' ityatra tu
na, karabhaśabdasyopamānavācitve'pi pūrvapadatvābhāvāt|
śaphau khurāviti| yadyapi ṭhśaphaṃ klībe khuraḥ pumān' ityamarastathāpi
śaphaśabdasya puṃstvamapi bahusammatama, śaphāviva jarbhurāṇeti śrutau
"grāmyapaśaṣusaṅgheṣu" iti sūtrasya vyākhyāvasare
nyāsaharadatādigrantheṣu vācaspatyādinibandheṣu ca pracuraprayogāt,
"liṅgamaśiṣyaṃ lokāśrayatvālliṅgasya" iti bhāṣyācca, ṭhśapho
bhujo gudaṃ ghoṇā' iti gaṇaratnamahodadhau kroḍādigaṇe pāṭhācca,
ṭhśaphaḥ khure gavādīnāṃ mūle viṭapināmapi' iti hemacandrakośācca|
viśvakośe ca punnapuṃsakatoktā| vāmau sundarau ūrū yasyā iti
vigrahaḥ| iha sarvatra upamānābhāvādaprāpte vidhiḥ| kathaṃ tarhi
ṭhpīvaroru pibatīva barhiṇa' iti kumāraḥ ? pramāda evāyam|
saṃjñāpūrvakatvātsambuddhiguṇo netyeke| hitena saheti| ṭhsamo vā
hitatatayoḥ' iti vyutpāditaḥ sahitaśabdastu neha gṛhyate,
ekadeśavikṛtatayā saṃhitagrahaṇādeva siddheriti bhāvaḥ| 
año yokāra iti| añā akāraviśeṣaṇaṃ kim ? śūrasenī|
"janapadaśabdāt" ityaño "ataśca" iti luki jātilakṣaṇo ṅīṣ|
śārṅgarani#īti| śṛṅgaruśabdādapatyārthe aṇ| nṛnarayoriti|
nṛśabdād "ṛnnebhya" iti ṅīpi, naraśabdājjātilakṣaṇe ṅīṣi prāpte
vacanam| nanu naraśabde alo'ntyasya vṛddhiḥ syāt| yatvāhuḥ--
antyasya "yasyeti" lopenāpahārādanantyasyāpyakārasya
vṛddhirbhavatyāntaratamyāt| ṭhguṇe tvanyāyyakalpanā'
ityalontyaparibhāṣābādhasyaiva nyāyyatvāditi; tanna,
pratyayasanniyogaśiṣṭāyā vṛddherantaraṅgatvena paramapi "yasyeti"
lopaṃ bādhitvā pravṛtyaucityāt | kiṃ cāstu kathaṃcillopastathā'pi
rephasyaiva vṛddhiḥ syānna tvakārasya,
antaratamaparibhāṣāpekṣayā'lontyaparibhāṣāyāḥ paratvāt| api
cāntaratamaparibhāṣā'sadṛśamādeśaṃ vyāvartayati na tu sthāninaṃ
niyaccchati, tatkathaṃ rephasya sthānitvavyavṛtiḥ| kiṃ ca ṭhguṇe tvanyāyya'
iti nyāyopanyasanaṃ sthavīya eva, paribhāṣayoḥ parasparaṃ
guṇaguṇibhāvāyogāt|
atredaṃ tatvam| "nara" iti rāntaluptākārānukaraṇaṃ naraḥ aḥ naraḥ| nā
ca naraśca tayoriti vyākhyeyam| yadvā narasya aḥ naraḥ, katantavat
pararūpam| nā ca naraśca tayornṛnarayoḥ| narasya ca prathama eva akāro
gṛhyate na tu dvitīyaḥ praśleṣasāmarthyāt| adhikaṃ śabdakaustubhe
bodhyam| putraśabdo'tra paṭhaḷte| śailaputrī| ṭhputrīva harṣaṃ hṛdaye
tanoti' ṭhkurve tadurvīpatiputri sarvam' | na ca putraśabdaḥ kanyāyāṃ
nāstīti bhramitavyam,
ātmajastanayaḥ sūnuḥ sutaḥ putraḥ striyaṃ tvamī| 
āhurduhitaraṃ sarve, ityamarokteḥ|
ata eva "pratyayasthāt" itītvaprakaraṇe ṭhvā
sūtakāputrikāvṛndārakāṇām' iti vārtikaṃ tadbhāṣyādikaṃ ca
saṅgaccchate| yatvatra haradatenoktam- kevalaputraśabdaḥ striyaṃ na
vartate iti; tadbhāṣyādivirodhādupekṣyam,
itvaprakaraṇasthasvagranthavirodhācca|
idaṃ tu cintyam--"kāre satyāgadasya" iti sūtre
ṭhsūtograrājabhojakulamerubhyo duhituḥ putraḍvā' iti vārtikaṃ
kimarthamiti| yatu vadanti svarārthamiti, tanna, ṭitvān ṅīpi kṛte
udātanivṛtisvareṇamodātaṅīnantena tatpuruṣe'pi
samāsasvareṇāntodāta eveti svare bhedābhāvāt| yadapi
śārṅgaravādau putraśabdaḥ prakṣipto na tu sāmpradāyika iti; tadapi
na, udāhṛtaprayodavirodhāt, ṭhvā sūtakā' ityādivārtike
kaiyaṭaharadatādikṛtā'kārapraśleṣavirodhāccetyāstāṃ tāvat| 
āmbaṣṭhaḷeti| "vṛddhetkosalāt" iti yaṅ| kārīṣagandhyeti|
karīṣasyeva gandho'sya karīṣagandhiḥ| "upamānācca" iti
gandhasyedantādeśaḥ| tasya gotrāpatyaṃ strī, aṇ| "aṇiñoḥ" iti
ṣyaṅadeśaḥ| sa ca yadyapi striyāmeva vihitastathā'pi
ṅtkiraṇasāmarthyātadantādapyayaṃ cāp| pautimāṣyeti|
pūtimāṣaśabdo gargādiḥ| 
prau.ma.- taddhitāḥ (pā.sū.->
"taddhitāḥ" || bahuvacanamanuktānāmupasaṃkhyeyānāṃ saṅgrahārtham|
mahāsaṃjñākaraṇaṃ ca tebhyaḥ prayogebhyo hitāstaddhitā ityanvarthatvāya|
tena yathā'bhidhānameva syuḥ| syādetat| ṭāpaḥ prāgayamadhikāro'stu
ṣphavidhau taddhitagrahaṇaṃ yasyeti lope īdgrahaṇaṃ ca māstviti cet
? maivam, paṭvītyatra orguṇa prasaṅgāt| 
śatrantāditi| yauti miśrīkaroti patyeti vyutpateḥ| kecitu
bāhulakāt yauterauṇādikaṃ katipratyayaṃ vadanti| ubhayathā'pi
yuvatīnāṃ samūho yauvatam| tyantātu aṇi puṃvadbhāvāt
yauvanamityeva| bhikṣādipāṭhasāmarthyānna puṃvaditi vṛtistu
bhāṣyaviruddhā, bhāṣye tatpāṭhasya pratyākhyānāditi dik||
|| iti strīpratyayāḥ ||


 |||||| atha svādisandhiḥ |||||| 
prau.ma.-sasajuṣo ruḥ (pā.sū.8-2-66>
sasajuṣo ruḥ|| padasyetyanuvṛtaṃ sasajūrbhyāṃ viśeṣyate, vaśeṣaṇena
tadantavidhiḥ| na ca sajūḥ śabdāṃśe "grahaṇavatā prātipadikena
tadantavidhirnāsti" iti niṣedhaḥ śaṅkyaḥ| tasya
pratyayavidhivaṣayatvāt| sāntaṃ sajuṣśabdāntaṃ ca yatpadaṃ tasya ruḥ
syāt, sa cālontyasya| evaṃ sthite phalitamāha-- padāntasyeti|
sajuṣśabdasyeti| tadantasya padasyetyarthaḥ, tena sajuṣa ityādau
nātiprasaṅgaḥ| na ca sajūrityatrāvyāptiḥ
"vyapadeśivadbhāvo'prātipadikena" iti niṣedhāditi vācyam|
"yena vidhiḥ" iti sūtre śabdakaustubhe'sya pratyākhyātatvāt|
"vyapadeśivapadbhāvo'prātipadikena" iti, "grahaṇavatā--" iti ca
paribhāṣādvayamapi pratyayavidhiviṣayakamiti diva utsūtre
kaiyaṭaharadatābhyāmuktatvācca| evaṃ ca paramasajūrbhyāmityādyapi
nirbādham| evaṃ sthite iha sajuṣśabdena tadantavidhirneti
prācāmuktirāpātata ityavadheyam||
prau.ma.- bhobhagoago'pūrvasya yo'ci(pā.sū.8-3-17>
bhobhago || asandhiḥ sautra iti| bhagoaghośabdayorokārasyākārasya
ca pūrvarūpaṃ sautratvānnetyarthaḥ| yadi tu bhos, bhagos,
aghos,iti sāntaṃ rāntaṃ vā'nukṛtya bho ityādīnāṃ
trayāṇāmalo'ntyasya yaḥ syādapūrvasya rośceti vyākhāyate, tarhi
asandhirnyāyya eva| yuktaṃ caitat| anyathā vibhoridaṃ, suprabhā
gauryasya sa suprabhagustasya suprabhagoridaṃ, raghoridamityatrāpi
yatvāpateḥ| bhavatihyayaṃ bheśabdādipūrvo ruḥ| na cātra
bhośabdāderlākṣaṇikatvādagrahaṇam| "vibhāṣā
bhavadbhagavadaghavatāmoccāvasya" iti vārtikena niṣpannānāṃ
bhavadādiprakṛtikānāṃ bhośabdādīnāmapi lākṣaṇikatvāviśeṣāt|
anarthakatvamapyubhayatra tulyam| ruviśiṣṭānāmevārthavatvāt|
nanu rorukārasyānubandhatvādrephamātraṃ visargasya sthāni, tathā ca
"analvidhau"iti sthānivadbhāvo na syādityāśaṅkyāha-- na hyayamiti|
yathā'grahīdityatra "graho'liṭi"(pā.sū.7-2-37)iti dīrghasya
sthānivadbhāveneṭtvāt "iṭa īṭi" (pā.sū.7-2-28)iti pravartate,
evamihāpi visargasya rutvādyatvaṃ pravarteteti bhāvaḥ| tathā ca
sthānivatsūtre bhāṣyam "viśiṣṭaṃ hyaeṣo'nalamāśrayate iṭṃa nāma" iti|
ito'pi hetordhātvaṅgetyādiparigaṇanamaśudhdamityavadheyam|
nipātā iti| "bhobhago--"(pā.sū.8-3-17)iti sūtre
nirdiṣṭāścāderākṛtigaṇatvātatra bodhyāḥ| tathā ca "vibhāṣā
bhavadbhagavat--"iti vārtikaṃ nāśrayaṇīyam| anyathā
pulliṅgaikavacanamātre bho hare ityādisidhdāvapi bho hariharau,
bho vadvadvṛnda, bho gaṅge, ityādi na sidhyediti bhāvaḥ| etacca
vṛtiharadatādimatenoktam| bhāṣyasvarasarītyā tu "vabhāṣā--"ityādi
vārtikamārabdhavyameva| kiṃ tu nipāto'pi bhoḥ śabdo'sti, tena
dvavacanādau strīnapuṃsakayośca na doṣaḥ| yaktataraṃ caitat| he
bhavannitivat he bho iti prayogasyāpīṣṭatvāt, tasya ca
vārtikārambha eva sidhdeḥ| "syuḥ pyāṭpāḍaṅgahaihebhoḥ"
ityamaroktānāṃ sambodhana#arthānāṃ nipātānāṃ
sahaprayogāyayogāt| kiṃ cātra bhavān haristatrabhavān itivat tatra
bho ityādyapi vārtike satyeva sidhyati| "itarābhyo'pi
dṛśyante"(pā.sū.5-3-14)iti sarvavibhaktyantātratasorbhavadādiyoga
eveṣṭatvāt| kiṃ cāmantritatve padātparasyāṣṭamikanaghātaḥ, "āmantrataṃ
pūrvam--"(pā.sū.8-1-72)ityavidyamānavadbhāvaḥ
"āmaekāntaram--"(pā.sū.8-1-55)iti vidhiḥ,
"vākyāderāmantritasya--"(pā.sū.8-1-8)iti dvirbhāvaḥ,
"subāmantrite--"(pā.sū.2-1-2)iti parāṅgavadbhāvaścetyādi sarvaṃ
sidhyati, na tvanyatheti dik| bhago, agho iti nipātāvapi yadi
prāmāṇikau tarhi stāṃ nāma| āmantritakāryasidhdistu
vārtikāyataiveti sudhībhirākalanīyam| yatu
vadanti--sakārāntānevaitān nipātānāsthāya rutvautve bhāṣye
pratyākhyāte iti, tadatirabhasāt| syādetat| nipātānāmabhyupagame
yatvavidhau ta eva gṛhyaeran pratipadoktatvāt, na tu sambuddhyantāḥ
lākṣaṇikatvāt, iti cet ? maivam,
"svaritenādhikāraḥ"(pā.sū.1-3-11)ityatrādhikaḥ kāro'dhikāra iti
vyākhyānamāśritya lakṣyānurodhena svaritatvamāśrityobhayagrahaṇāt,
"bhuvaśca mahāvyāhṛteḥ"(pā.sū.8-2-71)iti sūtre mahāvyāhṛtigrahaṇaṃ
lakṣaṇapratipadoktaparibhāṣayā anityatvajñāpanārthamiti haradatādyaukteśca|
na caivamapi prabhorityādāvativyāptiḥ| parasparasāhacaryeṇa
narapātānāṃ vārtikoktānāṃ ca grahaṇe'pyanyeṣāmagrahaṇāt|| 
prau.ma.- vyorlaghuprayatnataraḥ śākaṭāyanasyayaya (pā.sū.8-3-11>

prau.ma.-śarpare visarjanīyaḥ (pā.sū.8-3-35>
śarpare || satvāderayamapavādaḥ| yadi tu śarpare netyevocyeta tarhi
prakṛte satve naṣidhde'pi kupvoḥ ka pau syātām| vāsaḥ
kṣaumam, adbhiḥ psātamiti| visarjanīyavacanātu vakāramātraṃ
bādhyate ityāśayenāha--na tvanyaditi| tadeva sphuṭayati--iheti|
prau.ma.- so'padādau (pā.sū.8-3-38>
so'padādau|| apadādyoriti| sūtre tu vyatyayenaikavacanam| yadi tu
khar viśeṣyate'padādau kharīti, tadā yathāśrutaṃ sādhu| kiṃ tu
ṭhkhari' iti maṇḍūakaplutyā'navartyamiti kleśaḥ| na
cānuvṛterutaratrāpyāvaśyakatā, kharaṃ vinā vasargasya durlabhatvena
tadanuvṛterniṣphalatvāt| pāśakalpakakāmyeṣviti vṛtiḥ |
sambhavapradarśanametanna tu parigaṇanam, anyasyāsambhavāt| prātaḥ
kalpamiti| adhikaraṇaśaktipradhānasyāpi prātaḥ śabdasyeha vṛtiviṣaye
śaktimatparatvam| doṣābhūtamahaḥ, divābhūtā rātriritivat| gīḥ
kāmyatīti| iha "iṇaḥ ṣaḥ"(pā.sū.8-3-39)iti ṣatvaṃ na, tatrāpi "kāmye
roreva"ityasyānuvṛteḥ| yadi tu tatrevedaṃ paṭhaḷeta tarhi
ṣatvamātrapratiṣedhe'pi pūrveṇa satvaṃ syāt||
prau.ma.-iṇaḥ ṣaḥ (pā.sū.8-3-39>
"iṇaḥ ṣaḥ"|| pūrvasyāpavādaḥ| idaṃ sūtraṃ ṭhsaḥ' iti ca dvayamapi
utaratrānuvartate| yathāsambhavamiṇaḥ parasya ṣo'nyasya sa iti vivekaḥ|
puraḥ praveṣṭavyā iti| pṛ pālanapūraṇayoḥ,
"bhrājabhāsa--"(pā.sū.3-2-177)ityādinā kvip| tadantājjas| jasantatāṃ
sphuṭīkartuṃ pūḥ parāvityupanyastam||
prau.ma.-idudupadhasya cāpratyayasya (pā.sū.8-3-41>
idudupadhasya || idutau upadhe yasya samudāyasya tasyāvayavo yo
visardajanīya iti vaiyadhikaraṇyena sambandhaḥ| ṭhapratyayasya' iti tu
apratyayo yo visarga iti sambadhyate| sambhavati sāmānādhikaraṇye
vaiyadhikaraṇyasyānyāyyatvāt| na ca visargasya pratyayatvamaprasidhdamiti
vācyam, agniḥ karotītyādau sthānivadbhāvena tatprasidhdeḥ| na
cātideśaṃ prati tripādyā asidhdatvaṃ śaṅkyam, "apratyayasya" iti
niṣedhenaiva sidhdatvajñāpanāt| ata evāgnirityādau rorvisargaḥ
sidhyati| anyathā'padāntatvānna syāt| visargavidhistu punarityādau
caritārthaḥ| jñāpakaṃ ca viśeṣāpekṣam| tena "acaḥ
parasmin--"(pā.sū.1-1-57)iti tripādyāṃ na pravartate| evaṃ ca
"pūrvatrāsidhde na sthānivat" iti siddhānta uktayuktimūlako na tu
vācanika iti prapañcitaṃ śabdakaustubhe'smābhiḥ| nanvevaṃ mātuḥ
kāryamityādau ṣatvaṃ syāditi cet ? na, kaskādiṣu
bhrātuṣputraśabdasya pāṭhena ṅsiṅsorata ukārātparasya ṣatvaṃ neti
jñāpanāt| evaṃ ca ṭhapratyayasya' ityetadapivyadhikaraṇameveti
haradatenoktaṃ nātīvādartavyam| 
māturiti| atra hi "ṛta ut"(pā.sū.8-1-111)ityekādeśaśāstraṃ visargaṃ
prati paramparayā prayojakam| ākare tu "ekādeśaśāstranimitāt"iti
pracuraḥ pāṭhaḥ| tatra ekādeśaśāstraṃ nimitaṃ yasya ukārasya
tasmādityarthaḥ|
muhuḥ kāmeti| iha "kupvoḥ ka pau"(pā.sū.8-3-37)iti pravartate|
vṛtau muhuskāmeti sakāraḥ kvacit paṭhaḷte, tatprāmādikamiti
haradataḥ|
prau.ma.- dvistriścaturiti kṛtvo'rthe(pā.sū.8-3-43>
dvistriścaturiti|| iha dvitribhyāṃ sujantābhyāṃ sāhacaryāt catuḥ
śabdo'pi sujanta eva grahīṣyate| kṛtvo'rthagrahaṇaṃ
jñāpakam--sāhacaryaṃ na sarvatra vyavasthāpakamiti| tena
"dīdhīvevīṭām"(pā.sū.1-1-6)ityatra dhātusāhacarye'pi āgamasyeṭo
grahaṇam| ubhayatra vibhāṣā ceyam| caturityasya
"idudupadhasya--"(pā.sū.8-3-41)iti prāpte, itarayostvaprāpte| na ca
tayorapi "isusoḥ sāmarthye"(pā.sū.8-3-44)ityanena sidhdiḥ
śaṅkyā| isa- pratyayasya arthavataśca tatra grahaṇāt| catuṣkapāla iti|
ṭhidudupadhasya'(pā.sū.8-3-41)iti nityaṃ ṣaḥ|
vyapekṣāvirahe'pīti| tiṣṭhatu sarpiṣkuṇḍikāmānayetyādāvityarthaḥ|
vyapekṣāyāmiti| idaṃ sarpiṣkuṇḍikāyā ityatretyarthaḥ| yatu
pārāyaṇikā āhuḥ--ṭhkaskādiṣu sarpaṣkuṇḍikādīnāṃ pāṭha
utarapadasthatve'pi ṣatvārthaḥ' iti| tadbhāṣyavirodhādupekṣyam| 
ayaḥ sahiteti| na tvayaso vikāraḥ, "jānapada--"(pā.sū.4-1-42)iti
ṅīṣprasaṅgāt| ayaskarṇīti| aya iva karṇau yasyāḥ|
"nāsikodarauṣṭha--"(pā.sū.4-1-55)ityādinā ṅīṣ||
prau.ma.- adhaḥ śirasīpade (pā.sū.8-3-47>
adhaḥ śirasī||etayoriti| sūtre tu ṣaṣṭhÂayāḥ sthāne prathamā bodhyā|
adhaspadamiti| mayūravyaṃsakāditvātsamāsaḥ| śiraspadamiti|
ṣaṣṭhīsamāsaḥ||
|| iti visargasandhiḥ||
